Unlike existing image-fMRI VQA datasets, which typically provide only a few broad and weakly controlled questions per image, NSD-VQA provides on average 20 question-answer pairs per image across 20 controlled question categories that disentangle multiple levels of visual understanding. This enables more reliable and interpretable evaluation despite limited fMRI test data. Together, Brain-IT-VQA and NSD-VQA provide both a strong predictive framework and a tool for studying brain representations. Using this benchmark, we quantify which forms of visual and semantic information can be reliably decoded from fMRI responses to natural images. We further analyze the contributions of different brain regions across question types.

</details>


### [38] [Non-Forgetting Knowledge Allocation with Bi-level Competition for Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29592)
*Xiang Tan,Run He,Yawen Cui,Mengchen Zhao,Yan Wu,Tianyi Chen,Huiping Zhuang,Xiaonan Luo,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出NoFA-BC方法，通过非遗忘分配器和双层竞争机制，解决了基于预训练模型的类增量学习中适配器分配次优和遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于预训练模型的类增量学习方法采用适配器机制，通过任务特定适配器进行训练，并在推理时对所有适配器采用统一的知识分配机制。然而，这种统一分配机制忽略了任务之间的差异性，导致适配器利用率次优。此外，在类增量学习约束下，分配器在任务演进时容易出现遗忘问题。

Method: 提出Non-Forgetting Allocation with Bi-Level Competition（NoFA-BC）方法，包含三个核心组件：(1)非遗忘分配器（NFA）：将分配器训练转化为递归最小二乘问题，实现等价于使用所有数据训练的分配器；(2)双层竞争机制（BLC）：包含任务内Winner-Takes-All（WTA）机制提取每个任务中最显著的logit表示适配器贡献，以及任务间Last-Ones-Fall（LOF）消除机制抑制无关适配器；(3)稳定性增强（SE）过程：进一步提升旧任务的性能。

Result: 通过双层竞争机制，每个适配器的参与比例可以根据每个输入进行定制化调整，实现了更优的适配器知识分配，同时有效缓解了分配器的遗忘问题。

Conclusion: NoFA-BC方法通过将分配器训练转化为递归最小二乘问题并结合双层竞争机制，成功解决了基于预训练模型的类增量学习中适配器分配次优和遗忘问题，在保持旧任务性能的同时实现了更好的知识分配。

Abstract: Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) with pre-trained models (PTMs) aims to sequentially adapt PTMs to new categories without forgetting old knowledge. Built upon PTMs, existing adapter-based methods mainly train models via distinct task-specific adapters, and present a uniform knowledge allocation for each adapter during inference. However, this allocation mechanism ignores the nature of task discrepancy and leads to suboptimal utilization of adapters. Also, under CIL constraint, an allocator is prone to forgetting when tasks evolve. To address these issues, we propose a Non-Forgetting Allocation with Bi-Level Competition (NoFA-BC). NoFA-BC constructs a non-forgetting allocator (NFA) by transforming the allocator training into a recursive least-squares problem and achieves an allocator equivalent to that trained with all data. Based on the NFA, a Bi-Level Competition (BLC) including an intra-task level Winner-Takes-All (WTA) mechanism and inter-task Last-Ones-Fall (LOF) elimination is proposed to provide better allocation of adapter knowledge. WTA extracts the most significant logit within a task to represent the adapter's contribution and LOF suppresses the irrelevant adapters. With BLC, participation ratio of each adapter can be tailored for each input. Moreover, a Stability Enhancement (SE) process is incorporated to further improve the performance of old tasks.

</details>


### [39] [CogniVerse: Revolutionizing Multi-Modal Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Cognitive Reflection and Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29602)
*Xiang Fang,Wanlong Fang,Changshuo Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CogniVerse是一个新颖的多模态检索增强生成(MMRAG)框架，通过认知启发的推理方法和数学严谨性，解决了现有框架中的噪声检索、跨模态语义错位等问题，在知识密集型问答任务中显著提升了准确性和连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MMRAG框架存在关键局限性，包括：(1)检索结果包含噪声和不相关信息；(2)跨模态语义对齐不准确；(3)缺乏自适应推理能力；(4)局部和全局上下文生成缺乏连贯性。这些问题限制了多模态大语言模型在知识密集型问答任务中的表现。

Method: CogniVerse集成了三个协同组件：(1)认知反思模块，动态评估检索必要性并过滤相关多模态内容，减少噪声和计算开销；(2)多模态检索模块，在黎曼流形上使用信息几何对齐嵌入，并通过谱图理论优化知识图谱，确保检索的精确性和连贯性；(3)层次化生成模块，采用基于最优传输的损失函数，平衡token级准确性和全局语义连贯性。

Result: 大量实验表明，CogniVerse在准确性和连贯性方面显著优于最先进的系统，同时降低了检索延迟。该方法在多个评估指标上均取得了卓越性能。

Conclusion: CogniVerse通过认知启发和数学严谨的方法成功解决了现有MMRAG框架的局限性，证明了将认知理论与先进数学工具相结合的有效性，为多模态检索增强生成领域提供了新的研究方向和技术路径。

Abstract: Multi-modal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MMRAG) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for enhancing Multimodal Large Language Models in knowledge-intensive question answering by integrating external visual, textual, and structural knowledge. However, existing MMRAG frameworks suffer from critical limitations, including noisy and irrelevant retrieval, cross-modal semantic misalignment, lack of adaptive reasoning, and incoherent generation across local and global contexts. We introduce \textbf{CogniVerse}, a novel MMRAG framework that addresses these challenges through a cognitive-inspired, mathematically rigorous approach. Drawing from human-like reasoning, CogniVerse integrates three synergistic components: (1) a Cognitive Reflection Module that dynamically assesses retrieval necessity and filters relevant multi-modal content, reducing noise and computational overhead; (2) a Multi-modal Retrieval Module that aligns embeddings in a Riemannian manifold using information geometry and refines knowledge graphs via spectral graph theory, ensuring precise and coherent retrieval; and (3) a Hierarchical Generation Module that employs an optimal transport-based loss to balance token-level accuracy and global semantic coherence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CogniVerse significantly outperforms state-of-the-art systems in both accuracy and coherence, while reducing retrieval latency.

</details>


### [40] [Learning Context-Conditioned Predicate Semantics via Prototype Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29610)
*NamGyu Jung,Chang Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出AlignG方法，通过原型反馈学习上下文条件化的谓词语义，动态调整多义谓词表示以解决场景图生成中的歧义问题，在VG-150和GQA-200数据集上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 场景图生成中存在多义谓词建模的核心挑战——同一谓词在不同上下文中含义不同。现有方法通过将谓词分解为多个静态原型或检索语义相似样本来处理，但这些策略保持谓词表示静态，无法根据图像特定证据重组语义，导致在模糊上下文中出现系统性混淆。

Method: 提出AlignG框架，通过原型反馈学习上下文条件化的谓词语义。具体机制：1）从每张图像的关系候选中推断上下文条件化的谓词语义；2）将适应后的语义反馈以重新校准关系表示；3）通过将适应过程锚定到全局语义中心，防止语义漂移，同时允许在场景提供一致关系线索时进行选择性重组。

Result: 在VG-150和GQA-200数据集上持续超越SOTA基线。在SGDet设置下，VG-150的F@100提升+1.4，GQA-200的F@100提升+2.7。可视化结果显示原型能根据场景证据选择性地合并或分离谓词，展现出一致的上下文依赖性重组。

Conclusion: AlignG成功实现了谓词语义的动态重组，解决了静态表示无法适应上下文的根本局限，为场景图生成中多义谓词建模提供了新范式。原型相似性的可视化证明了方法能够根据场景证据进行合理的语义重组。

Abstract: In scene graph generation, a central challenge is modeling polysemous predicates whose meanings shift across contexts. Prior approaches address this issue by decomposing predicates into multiple static prototypes or retrieving semantically similar exemplars. However, these strategies keep predicate representations static and cannot reorganize semantics to reflect image-specific evidence, leading to systematic confusions in ambiguous contexts. We propose AlignG, which learns context-conditioned predicate semantics via prototype feedback. AlignG infers context-conditioned predicate semantics from the relation candidates within each image and feeds the adapted semantics back to recalibrate relation representations. The learning objective anchors this adaptation to global semantic centers, preventing semantic drift while still allowing selective reorganization when the scene provides consistent relational cues. Experiments on VG-150 and GQA-200 show consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines, with F@100 improvements of +1.4 on VG-150 and +2.7 on GQA-200 under SGDet. We further visualize per-image prototype similarity shifts and observe coherent context-dependent reorganization where prototypes selectively merge or separate predicates according to scene evidence. The code is available at https://github.com/Namgyu97/AlignG-SGG.pytorch.

</details>


### [41] [DiffSpot: Can VLMs Spot Fine-Grained Visual Differences in Web Interfaces?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29615)
*Linhao Zhang,Aiwei Liu,Yuan Liu,Xiao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了DiffSpot基准测试，用于评估视觉语言模型在网页界面中检测细微视觉差异的能力，发现现有模型在此任务上表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型(VLMs)在高级图像-文本对齐方面取得了进展，但感知细微视觉差异的能力仍然有限。这在渲染网页界面中尤为重要，因为局部视觉变化既是细粒度感知的诊断测试，也是GUI智能体和设计工具的实际需求。

Method: 提出DiffSpot基准：(1)通过变异HTML中目标元素的单个CSS属性构造受控图像对；(2)重新渲染页面并记录改变的属性、元素和变异幅度；(3)使用接地门控保留像素差异仅限于目标元素的图像对；(4)包含4,400对图像，其中3,900对有差异（平衡分布在13个CSS属性操作符和三个难度层级），加上500对无差异图像用于幻觉控制。

Result: 在13个前沿VLMs上进行零样本评估发现：(1)最佳模型仅能识别40.7%的真实变化；(2)所有模型在困难层级的召回率均低于23%；(3)难度强烈依赖于属性类型；(4)像素幅度和CLIP距离均无法可靠预测召回率。

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型在细粒度视觉感知任务上存在显著局限性，特别是在检测网页界面细微差异方面，这揭示了现有模型在精细视觉理解能力上的不足。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have made strong progress on high-level image-text alignment, yet their ability to perceive subtle visual differences remains limited. We study this problem in rendered web interfaces, where localized visual changes are both a diagnostic test of fine-grained perception and a practical requirement for GUI agents and design tools. We introduce \textbf{DiffSpot}, a code-driven benchmark for open-ended spot-the-difference on web interfaces. DiffSpot constructs controlled image pairs by mutating a single CSS property of a target element in self-contained HTML, re-rendering the page, and recording the changed property, element, and mutation magnitude. A grounding gate retains only pairs whose rendered pixel difference is confined to the target element. The benchmark contains 4{,}400 pairs, including 3{,}900 has-diff pairs balanced across 13 CSS-property operators and three difficulty tiers, plus 500 no-diff pairs for hallucination control. Evaluating 13 frontier VLMs zero-shot, we find that even the best model identifies only $40.7\%$ of true changes, with Hard-tier Recall below $23\%$ for every model. DiffSpot further shows that difficulty is strongly property-dependent: across CSS operators, neither pixel magnitude nor CLIP distance reliably predicts Recall.

</details>


### [42] [AgentCVR: Active Multi-Agent Cross-Video Reasoning via Script-Simulated Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29643)
*Yilun Qiu,Jiahe Wang,Cilin Yan,Jiayin Cai,Xiaolong Jiang,Yao Hu,Chun Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AgentCVR是一个用于跨视频推理的多智能体框架，通过迭代式协调专业化视觉和音频智能体来提取目标证据，并采用脚本模拟强化学习实现高效训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨视频推理（CVR）需要跨多个视频检索、对齐和聚合证据，是多媒体智能的关键前沿。当前多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在CVR任务上表现不佳，因为简单的单遍策略将多个视频编码到共享的压缩上下文中，可能会模糊稀有但关键的证据信息。

Method: 提出AgentCVR多智能体框架，将CVR视为主动证据获取任务。框架包含：1）主智能体迭代协调专业化的视觉智能体和音频智能体进行针对性证据提取；2）引入脚本模拟强化学习，使用LLM生成的语义脚本和轻量级文本模拟器优化智能体策略，避免了在线探索中昂贵的多模态推理。

Result: 在综合CVR基准测试中，AgentCVR优于单遍基线方法，并达到与最先进闭源系统相当的性能，特别是在复杂的跨视频对齐和定位任务中表现出色。

Conclusion: 多智能体迭代协调方法有效解决了跨视频推理中的证据检索与对齐问题，脚本模拟强化学习为智能体训练提供了高效可行的替代方案，避免了传统多模态在线推理的高昂计算成本。

Abstract: Cross-Video Reasoning (CVR) has emerged as a critical frontier in multimodal intelligence, requiring models to retrieve, align, and aggregate evidence distributed across multiple videos. Current Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) often struggle with CVR, as simple single-pass strategies encode multiple videos into a shared compressed context, potentially obscuring rare but critical evidence. In this paper, we propose AgentCVR, a multi-agent framework that treats CVR as an active evidence-acquisition task. AgentCVR employs a Master Agent to iteratively coordinate specialized Visual and Audio Agents for targeted evidence extraction. To ensure efficient training, we introduce Script-Simulated RL, which optimizes the agent's policy with LLM-generated semantic scripts and a lightweight text-based simulator, bypassing costly multimodal inference during online exploration. Experimental results on a comprehensive CVR benchmark show that AgentCVR outperforms single-pass baselines and achieves comparable performance to state-of-the-art closed-source systems, particularly in complex cross-video alignment and localization. To ensure reproducibility, our code is available at https://github.com/wang-jh24/AgentCVR.

</details>


### [43] [MARTIAN: A Rendering Framework for Aerial Mars Imagery from HiRISE Orbital Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29647)
*Dario Pisanti,Georgios Georgakis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MARTIAN，一个开源的基于Blender的渲染框架，利用真实的HiRISE轨道地图产品生成可控光照条件和不同高度下的火星地形真实航空视图，并附带精确的姿态标注，以解决火星视觉导航训练数据稀缺问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 火星航空导航需要能够适应火星表面多样光照条件和地形形态的鲁棒视觉流水线。然而，训练和评估此类方法的关键瓶颈是缺乏大规模、带标注的航空数据集。

Method: 开发了MARTIAN框架，这是一个基于Blender的开源渲染工具，利用真实的HiRISE轨道地图产品合成真实的火星地形航空视图。该框架支持可控光照条件和可变高度设置，并能生成带有准确姿态标注的观测数据。

Result: 该框架已在关于Ingenuity和未来火星旋翼飞行器的地图定位系统研究中得到应用验证。在该应用中，使用合成数据训练的深度图像匹配器成功在真实的火星图像上进行了评估。

Conclusion: MARTIAN直接解决了火星视觉导航训练数据稀缺的问题，已在GitHub上公开发布，为火星航空导航研究提供了重要的数据生成工具。

Abstract: Aerial navigation on Mars requires vision-based pipelines that are robust to the diverse illumination conditions and terrain morphology of the Martian surface. A key bottleneck for training and evaluating such methods is the scarcity of large-scale, annotated aerial datasets. We present MARTIAN, an open-source Blender-based rendering framework that leverages real HiRISE orbital map products to synthesize realistic aerial views of the Martian terrain under controllable lighting conditions and at varying altitudes. MARTIAN generates observations with accurate pose annotations, directly addressing the scarcity of training data for vision-based navigation on Mars. The framework has been validated through its deployment in concurrent work on map-based localization systems for Ingenuity and future Mars rotorcraft, where synthetically trained deep image matchers were successfully evaluated on real Mars imagery. MARTIAN is publicly available at: https://github.com/nasa-jpl/martian.

</details>


### [44] [SuperVoxelGPT: Adaptive and Ordered 3D Tokenization for Autoregressive Shape Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29655)
*Yuan Li,Congyi Zhang,Xifeng Gao,Xiaohu Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SuperVoxelGPT框架，通过自适应且有确定顺序的超体素标记化方法，在保持生成质量的同时显著提升3D生成的效率和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自回归多模态大语言模型在3D生成中面临结构性权衡困境：紧凑集合表示丢弃了确定性的空间顺序，导致序列预测模糊；而均匀或基于八叉树的体素网格虽保留顺序但存在严重冗余和过长序列问题。这种权衡限制了高分辨率形状的稳定高效自回归3D生成。

Method: 提出SuperVoxelGPT表示优先框架：1) 首先预测粗略的几何显著性分布；2) 使用显著性引导的质心Voronoi剖分构建形状自适应超体素分割，为复杂区域分配细粒度单元、平滑区域分配较大单元；3) 引入SuperVoxelVAE；4) 在文本和有序超体素布局条件下，微调预训练MLLM以自回归生成超体素token。

Result: 在Trellis-500K数据集上的实验表明，SuperVoxelGPT将token序列长度减少至均匀体素标记化的12.8%，同时达到最先进的生成质量，相比先前方法平均提速10倍。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了空间顺序保持与冗余减少之间的矛盾，为高效高质量的自回归3D生成提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive multimodal large language models (MLLMs) enable 3D generation but struggle to scale to high-resolution shapes due to
  inadequate 3D tokenizations. Compact set-based representations discard deterministic spatial ordering, leading to ambiguous sequence
  prediction, while uniform or octree-based voxel grids preserve ordering at the cost of severe redundancy and excessively long
  sequences. This structural trade-off limits stable and efficient autoregressive 3D generation.
  We present SuperVoxelGPT, a representation-first framework that resolves this tension through adaptive and deterministically ordered
  supervoxel tokenization. Given a prompt, we first predict a coarse geometric saliency distribution and construct a shape-adaptive
  supervoxel partition using saliency-guided centroidal Voronoi tessellation, allocating fine-grained cells to complex regions and
  larger cells to smooth regions. Conditioned on the text and ordered supervoxel layout, we introduce a SuperVoxelVAE and fine-tune a
  pretrained MLLM to autoregressively generate supervoxel tokens.
  Experiments on Trellis-500K show that SuperVoxelGPT reduces token sequence length to 12.8% of uniform voxel tokenization while
  achieving state-of-the-art generation quality and an average 10$\times$ speedup over prior methods.

</details>


### [45] [OccamToken: Efficient VLM Inference with Training-Free and Budget-Adaptive Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29657)
*Geng Li,Guohao Chen,Ting Chen,Shilin Shan,Kuangji Zuo,Bofan Lyu,Tuo An,Gen Li,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OccamToken提出了一种免训练的视觉Token剪枝框架，通过基于寄存器锚定的相对证据测试替代传统的绝对排序方法，有效解决了注意力汇和冗余问题，在极低保留率下仍能保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型剪枝方法多采用绝对排序范式（保留固定Top-K Token），存在脆弱性：注意力汇扭曲了Token重要性排序，且图像冗余和查询依赖性使得固定的Token预算在不同输入间不可靠。

Method: 提出OccamToken框架，利用寄存器Token作为稳定参考来吸收低信息注意力模式。该方法不进行全局重要性排序，而是评估视觉Token是否提供了超越寄存器参考的信息，通过源自寄存器注意力的动态阈值进行图像自适应冗余剪枝和查询自适应相关性剪枝。

Result: 在LLaVA-NeXT、LLaVA-v1.5和Qwen3-VL上无需额外训练即可提升准确率-效率权衡。在LLaVA-NeXT上，将2880个视觉Token压缩至约40个（保留率1.4%），同时保留了超过93%的原始准确率。

Conclusion: OccamToken成功解决了现有剪枝范式的局限性，为视觉语言模型提供了一种稳健、高效且精确的视觉Token压缩方案，即使在极端压缩场景下也能保持稳定的性能。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) rely on long visual token sequences for visual understanding, making the prefill stage expensive in both computation and memory. Most existing pruning methods follow an absolute-ranking paradigm, assigning importance scores to visual tokens and retaining a fixed top-K subset. In this work, we argue that this paradigm is fundamentally brittle: attention sinks distort token importance rankings, while image redundancy and query-dependent visual evidence make fixed token budgets unreliable across inputs. We propose OccamToken, a training-free framework that replaces absolute token ranking with register-anchored relative evidence testing. Instead of asking which tokens are globally important, OccamToken evaluates whether a visual token provides information beyond a register-based reference. Our key insight is that register tokens naturally absorb low-information attention patterns, making them a stable reference for identifying genuinely informative visual evidence. Based on this principle, OccamToken performs both image-adaptive redundancy pruning and query-adaptive relevance pruning through dynamic thresholds derived from register attention. Across LLaVA-NeXT, LLaVA-v1.5, and Qwen3-VL, OccamToken consistently improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off without additional training. Notably, on LLaVA-NeXT, it reduces 2,880 visual tokens to approximately 40 while preserving over 93% of the original accuracy, enabling stable visual token compression even in the extreme 1.4% retention regime.

</details>


### [46] [Geometry-Guided Modeling of Foundation Features Enables Generalizable Object Shape Deformation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29661)
*Yiyao Ma,Kai Chen,Zhongxiang Zhou,Zhuheng Song,Dongsheng Xie,Zelong Tan,Rong Xiong,Qi Dou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种可泛化的变形学习框架，通过将类别级形状模板显式变形以匹配目标观测，实现单目3D形状恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目3D形状恢复对于几何理解至关重要，但在任意视角和未见过的物体类别上实现鲁棒泛化仍是一个重大挑战。

Method: 提出变形学习框架，通过显式变形类别级形状模板来重建3D物体。引入几何引导的特征建模机制，用模板拓扑丰富基础特征以获得几何感知表示；提出视角自适应特征聚合模块，利用多视图模板特征和相机姿态来丰富规范模板表示。

Result: 实验表明该方法在处理大形状变化和多样视角方面显著优于最先进方法，对新颖类别展现出强泛化能力，并有效支持下游灵巧机器人操作任务。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了单目3D形状恢复在视角和物体类别上的泛化难题，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Monocular 3D shape recovery is fundamental to geometric understanding, yet achieving robust generalization across arbitrary viewpoints and unseen object categories remains a significant challenge. In this paper, we present a generalizable deformation learning framework that reconstructs 3D objects by explicitly deforming a category-level shape template to match the target observation. To address complex shape variations between the template and the target, we introduce a geometry-guided feature modeling mechanism. This process first enriches foundation features with template topology to yield a geometry-aware representation, which is then explicitly correlated with the target observation to guide precise deformation. Furthermore, to bridge the disparity between the fixed template and arbitrary target views, we propose a view-adaptive feature aggregation module. This module leverages multi-view template features and their corresponding camera poses to enrich the canonical template representation, ensuring robust feature alignment regardless of the target's perspective. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms state-of-the-art methods in handling large shape variations and diverse viewpoints, exhibiting strong generalization to novel categories and effectively supporting downstream real-world dexterous robotic manipulation tasks. Project homepage: https://GODeform.github.io/

</details>


### [47] [SAFE-Pruner: Semantic Attention-Guided Future-Aware Token Pruning for Efficient Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29662)
*Shilin Ma,Chubin Zhang,Changyuan Wang,Yuji Wang,Yue Wu,Zixuan Wang,Jingqi Tian,Zheng Zhu,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SAFE-Pruner，一个用于视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的即插即用式视觉令牌剪枝框架，通过引入未来层的注意力线索进行剪枝决策，实现了显著的推理加速，同时保持任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型的实时推理对机器人控制至关重要。现有的视觉令牌剪枝方法主要基于浅层线索进行剪枝决策，这可能导致丢弃深层所需的视觉信息，从而影响模型性能。

Method: 提出SAFE-Pruner框架：1）识别语义注意力一致性现象（VLA模型在执行步骤中持续关注同一语义实体）；2）设计前瞻性策略预测深层令牌显著性，避免过早移除关键令牌；3）引入自适应子任务划分策略检测注意力突变，提高预测准确性和剪枝可靠性。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中进行大量实验，实现高达1.89倍加速，成功率下降仅不到1.7%，性能超越现有最先进方法达1.9%。

Conclusion: SAFE-Pruner通过引入未来层注意力线索成功解决了现有剪枝方法的局限性，在保证性能的前提下实现了稳定的推理加速，为机器人控制的实时VLA推理提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Real-time inference of vision-language-action (VLA) models is essential for robotic control. While visual token pruning has shown strong potential for accelerating inference, most existing methods mainly base pruning decisions on shallow-layer cues and risk discarding visual information required by deep layers. To address this issue, we propose SAFE-Pruner, a plug-and-play pruning framework that incorporates attention cues of future layers into pruning decisions. Specifically, we identify semantic attention consistency, the tendency that VLA models concentrate their attention probability mass on the same semantic entity across execution steps. Based on this observation, we design a forward-looking strategy to forecast the token saliency in deep layers, which prevents the premature removal of critical tokens and leads to more stable acceleration. We further introduce an adaptive subtask division strategy to detect abrupt attention shifts, thereby improving forecasting accuracy and pruning reliability. Extensive experiments in simulation and real-world settings demonstrate that our method achieves up to 1.89x speedup with a minimal degradation in success rate of less than 1.7%, while outperforming state-of-the-art methods by up to 1.9%.

</details>


### [48] [Unsupervised Semantic Segmentation Facilitates Model Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29691)
*Xiaoyan Yu,Lisa Mais,Jannik Franzen,Peter Hirsch,Nick Lechtenbörger,Andreas Mardt,Dagmar Kainmüller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于无监督语义分割的简单可视化协议，用于直观理解自监督学习视觉Transformer的行为，揭示不同模型的位置偏置和扩展特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习产生了多种视觉Transformer模型，但现有研究发现的模型理解（如对比学习和掩码图像建模模型之间的差异）尚未充分传播到更广泛的社区，有时会将对比学习模型的特有见解错误地推广到掩码图像建模模型。需要一种简单直观的方法让广大受众理解模型行为。

Method: 提出一种简单且易于解释的可视化协议，基于无监督语义分割结果的可视化。目标不是最大化分割性能，而是展示跨图像一致出现的模型行为。在层和表示层面对多种自监督学习模型进行基准测试。

Result: 获得了关于不同位置偏置和扩展行为的新见解，包括发现DINOv3-Large模型标记中存在强烈的边界伪影。这些见解补充并帮助传达了一系列先前的研究发现。该协议还能够清晰地区分位置效应与局部性偏置（locality bias，后者在文献中被研究得更为广泛）。

Conclusion: 该可视化协议使模型理解对广泛社区变得简单直观，已公开发布在GitHub上，将促进更广泛的模型理解研究。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has produced a diverse landscape of vision transformers (ViTs) whose pretrained representations support a wide range of downstream tasks. Towards a better understanding of these models, a body of work has assessed the mechanics of their self-attention as well as the types of information captured across their representations, revealing, for example, stark differences between models trained with contrastive learning (CL) and masked image modeling (MIM). However, these advances in model understanding have not yet fully permeated the broader community, where insights specific to CL models are sometimes generalized to MIM models. To make model understanding straightforward and intuitive for a broad audience, we propose a simple and easily interpretable visualization protocol.
  Our protocol is based on visualizing unsupervised semantic segmentation results, yet our goal is not to maximize segmentation performance. Instead, it allows us to convey model behaviors that consistently emerge across images. Benchmarking a diverse set of SSL models across layers and representations, we obtain novel insights into distinct positional biases and scaling behaviors, including strong boundary artifacts in DINOv3-Large model tokens. These insights complement and help communicate a range of previous findings.
  Our protocol further enables a clear visual distinction between positional effects and the closely related but distinct locality bias, which has been studied much more extensively in the literature. The protocol is publicly available on GitHub and we believe it will catalyze further model understanding for a broad community.

</details>


### [49] [SLAD : Shared LoRA Adapters for Task Specific Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29726)
*Reda Bensaid,Yassir Bendou,Vincent Gripon,François Leduc-Primeau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了SLAD方法，通过低秩适应和参数共享策略解决任务特定蒸馏中teacher-student特征对齐问题，实现了更快训练速度和更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限环境中，任务特定蒸馏（将同一基础模型的大版本知识转移到小版本）变得流行。然而现有方法存在一个问题：虽然对teacher模型进行fine-tuning能提高其性能，但进行linear probing反而能实现更好的知识蒸馏。研究发现这是由于teacher在fine-tuning过程中与student产生了特征表示错位。

Method: 本文提出SLAD方法，包含两个核心策略：(1) 利用低秩适应来保持特征对齐，从而改善知识转移；(2) 在联合训练过程中，通过两个编码器之间的adapter参数共享策略进一步增强特征对齐。这种设计避免了传统fine-tuning导致的特征错位问题。

Result: 实验结果表明：SLAD实现了更好的teacher-student特征对齐；不仅student模型性能提升，teacher模型性能也有改善；训练速度比传统fine-tuning快2倍；在多个分类和分割数据集上进行了广泛验证，达到了任务特定蒸馏框架中的state-of-the-art性能。

Conclusion: 通过解决特征对齐问题，SLAD在任务特定蒸馏框架中实现了更高的准确性和转移效率，为资源受限环境下的模型适配提供了更优解决方案。

Abstract: In the context of resource-constrained environments such as embedded systems, adapting reduced-size foundation models to downstream tasks has become increasingly popular. This has recently motivated the emerging setting of task-specific distillation, where a larger and a smaller version of the same foundation model are both adapted to the same downstream task, with the goal of transferring knowledge from the former to the latter. Recent work has demonstrated the benefits of using a larger version of the same foundation model to assist the adaptation of a smaller one. Typically, the larger model (teacher) is first adapted via fine-tuning or linear probing before its knowledge is distilled into the smaller model (student). While fine-tuning the teacher often increases its performance, recent work showed that probing it leads to better knowledge distillation to the student. Our findings show that this is mainly due to a mis-alignment in feature representation between the teacher and the student which occurs during the teacher's fine-tuning. Inspired by existing efforts to preserve previously learned knowledge, we first propose leveraging low-rank adaptation, resulting in better feature alignment and therefore better knowledge transfer. Drawing from this insight, we further enhance the feature alignment through a parameter-sharing strategy of the adapters between the two encoders during joint training. Our proposed method, SLAD, shows better feature alignment between the teacher and student, which results in increased performance for not only the student but also the teacher model, while being 2x faster to train than fine-tuning. Through extensive experiments on multiple classification and segmentation datasets, we demonstrate the improved accuracy and transfer efficiency of our method, achieving state-of-the-art performance in the task-specific distillation framework.

</details>


### [50] [S2MDF: A Plug-And-Play Layer for Intersection-Free Multi-Object Signed Distance Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29761)
*Deniz Sayin Mercadier,Federico Stella,Aurel Bizeau,Nicolas Talabot,Pascal Fua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Compositional implicit surface representations model scenes as collections of objects, each encoded by a Signed Distance Field (SDF). A fundamental limitation of this approach is that multiple SDFs can produce geometries that interpenetrate, violating physical plausibility. Existing mitigation strategies rely on soft penalty terms that reduce but do not eliminate intersections, and require careful loss weighting. To truly prevent interpenetration, we propose a hard constraint on vector-valued SDFs and introduce S2MDF, a lightweight plug-and-play module that enforces the constraint on any object-compositional SDF representation without architectural modifications. It introduces negligible computational overhead and is compatible with linearly-interpolated standard meshing algorithms such as Marching Cubes. It can be applied during training or as a post-processing step. Experiments on multiple state-of-the-art compositional methods show that S2MDF reduces intersections to numerical precision while preserving reconstruction quality, outperforming existing mitigation strategies.

</details>


### [51] [GeoMag: Geometric-Aware Video Motion Magnification via State Space Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29762)
*Kecheng Han,Yuchen Zhang,Bingqing Liu,Boqiang Guo,Wenbin Zheng,Shiyuan Pei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了GeoMag框架和Geo-200K数据集，解决了视频运动放大中结构不一致和训练数据不足的问题，实现了线性复杂度的全局一致运动放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频运动放大（VMM）方法在复杂几何变换下存在结构不一致问题；基于学习的方法面临CNN全局上下文受限与Transformer计算成本高的权衡困境；现有训练协议主要关注简单线性运动，无法捕捉真实世界视频中的几何和成像复杂性。

Method: 提出GeoMag框架，基于状态空间模型（State Space Models）实现具有线性复杂度的全局一致运动放大；构建Geo-200K大规模合成数据集，引入丰富的几何变换和传感器逼真的降质效果，提升训练信号的多样性和真实性。

Result: 在合成和真实世界基准测试上的大量实验表明，GeoMag在视觉保真度和计算效率方面均优于现有方法，同时产生更少的伪影和更好的结构一致性。

Conclusion: GeoMag成功解决了VMM中的关键挑战，通过几何感知设计和高质量合成数据集实现了结构一致性好、计算效率高的运动放大效果。

Abstract: Video Motion Magnification (VMM) reveals imperceptible dynamics but often suffers from structural inconsistencies under complex geometric transformations. Existing learning-based methods generally face a trade-off between the limited global context of CNNs and the high computational cost of Transformers. In addition, current training protocols, largely dominated by simple linear motion, fail to capture the geometric and imaging complexities encountered in real-world videos. To address these issues, we propose GeoMag, a geometric-aware VMM framework built upon State Space Models to achieve globally consistent motion amplification with linear complexity. We further construct Geo-200K, a large-scale synthetic dataset that introduces rich geometric transformations together with sensor-realistic degradations, improving the diversity and realism of training signals. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world benchmarks show that GeoMag consistently outperforms prior methods in visual fidelity and computational efficiency, while producing fewer artifacts and better structural consistency.

</details>


### [52] [Energy-Aware NECO for Single-Pass Pixel-wise Out-of-Distribution Detection in Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29773)
*Boyuan Zhang,Huanshan Huang,Yifei Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Energy-Aware NECO，一种单次前向传播的像素级分布外(OOD)检测器，通过融合NECO几何比率与Energy分数，在miniMUAD数据集上实现0.8539 AUROC，优于现有基线方法，同时保持高效的单次推理优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人的可靠语义分割需要在分布偏移下同时实现精确的密集预测和鲁棒的不确定性估计。现有的强不确定性基线方法如Monte Carlo Dropout需要多次随机前向传播，难以在边缘平台上部署，因此需要一种高效的单次传播检测方法。

Method: 提出Energy-Aware NECO方法：1) 从解码器特征计算中心化的NECO式几何比率；2) 结合基于logits的Energy分数；3) 使用纯分布内验证集统计量对两个组件进行标准化；4) 通过凸组合融合两个分数，形成混合检测分数。整个方法仅需单次前向传播。

Result: 在miniMUAD子集上进行评估，使用真实像素级OOD标签：混合分数AUROC达到0.8539，超越NECO-only (0.8280)、Energy-only (0.8171)和集成预测熵基线 (0.8124)。定性和操作点分析表明混合检测器提升了整体排序性能，同时保持了单次设计的高效性优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合检测器成功结合了NECO和Energy两种方法的优势，在保持单次前向传播效率的同时，显著提升了OOD检测性能，为移动机器人边缘部署提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Reliable semantic segmentation for mobile robots requires both accurate dense prediction and robust uncertainty estimation under distribution shift. Strong uncertainty baselines such as Monte Carlo Dropout often require repeated stochastic forward passes and are difficult to deploy on edge platforms.
  We propose Energy-Aware NECO, a single-pass pixel-wise out-of-distribution (OOD) detector for semantic segmentation. The method combines a centered NECO-style geometric ratio computed from decoder features with a logit-based Energy score. Both components are standardized using statistics fitted on a pure in-distribution validation split and fused through a convex combination.
  We evaluate the method on the miniMUAD subset using true pixel-level OOD labels. The proposed hybrid score achieves an AUROC of 0.8539, outperforming NECO-only (0.8280), Energy-only (0.8171), and an ensemble predictive-entropy baseline (0.8124). Additional qualitative and operating-point analyses show that the hybrid detector improves overall ranking performance while preserving the efficiency advantages of a single-pass design.
  Code is available at https://github.com/boyuan-zhangx/Energy-Aware_NECO

</details>


### [53] [Improving CLIP Adaptation by Breaking Tail Alignment for Source-Free Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29776)
*Shuai Yi,Yixiong Zou,Yuhua Li,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了自适应尾部-头部对齐（ATHA）方法，通过反直觉地将低相似度的“尾部标记”推离文本嵌入而非强行对齐，解决了CLIP在跨域少样本学习（CDFSL）中的过拟合问题，实现了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对CLIP在跨域少样本学习（CDFSL）中因域偏移大、数据稀缺导致的性能下降问题，现有微调范式盲目将所有图像块标记与文本嵌入对齐。研究发现，强制对齐缺乏语义信息的低相似度“尾部标记”会导致过拟合，而“推开”这些标记反而有益。

Method: 提出自适应尾部-头部对齐（ATHA）微调策略。该方法打破传统的均匀对齐范式，转而采用自适应对齐策略：针对包含足够语义信息的标记加强对其，而对低相似度的“尾部标记”实施对齐减弱（即推离文本嵌入），以防止过拟合并利用预训练知识。

Result: 在四个具有挑战性的CDFSL基准测试上进行的广泛实验验证了ATHA的有效性，实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了大域偏移和数据稀缺下打破对齐比强制对齐更有效的现象，为CLIP的跨域少样本微调提供了新的理论解释和有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) such as CLIP demonstrate strong zero-shot generalization, but their performance significantly degrades in cross-domain scenarios with scarce target-domain training data (Cross-Domain Few-Shot Learning, CDFSL). In this paper, we focus on the target-domain few-shot finetuning in the CLIP-based CDFSL task. Prevailing finetuning paradigms uniformly align all image patch tokens with their corresponding textual embeddings. However, we find a counterintuitive phenomenon: actively pushing away certain low-similarity image tokens, termed "tail tokens", from their textual embeddings consistently improves target-domain performance. We delve into this phenomenon and provide a novel interpretation: under great domain shifts and scarce training data, the model can hardly extract semantic information from visual inputs; therefore, the common belief of alignment is valid only for tokens already containing sufficient semantic information; for tail tokens, forcing the alignment would lead to excessive overfitting to the scarce training, while breaking the alignment is more useful. Motivated by this, we propose Adaptive Tail-Head Alignment (ATHA), a novel fine-tuning strategy for CLIP that transforms the conventional uniform alignment paradigm to an adaptive alignment paradigm, with both alignment strengthening and weakening. Extensive experiments on four challenging CDFSL benchmarks validate our state-of-the-art performance. Our code is available at https://github.com/shuaiyi308/ATHA.

</details>


### [54] [Train the Agent, Not the Expert: Learning to Harness Heterogeneous Experts for Multi-Turn Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29894)
*Yaowu Fan,Tao Han,Dazhao Du,Andy J. Ma,Jia Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了VisHarness，一个可训练的视觉智能体，通过学习协调异构视觉专家模型来解决多种视觉任务，在推理分割、广义指代分割、密集小目标检测和指代计数等任务上显著优于现有通用模型，并与任务专用模型表现相当或更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的计算机视觉模型通常针对隔离的任务进行优化（如检测、分割、计数等），难以直接支持通用视觉智能，尤其是需要复杂语言理解和密集小目标感知的任务。

Method: 提出VisHarness框架，将高层感知、推理和决策与底层任务执行解耦；通过轻量级训练学习协调异构视觉专家的策略；引入动态视觉记忆归档机制以缓解多轮交互带来的视觉token开销累积问题，实现高效的在策略强化学习训练。

Result: 在四个代表性基准测试（推理分割、广义指代分割、密集小目标检测、指代计数）上，VisHarness显著优于现有通用模型，并与任务专用模型达到相当或更优的性能。

Conclusion: 该范式在保留智能体通用智能的同时，充分利用了专用视觉模型在具体视觉任务中的精度优势，证明了视觉专家协调策略的泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent progress in computer vision has produced a wide range of powerful specialized models for detection, segmentation, counting, and other visual tasks. However, these models are usually optimized for isolated task formulations, making it difficult to directly support general-purpose visual intelligence, especially when a task requires complex language understanding and dense small-object perception. In this paper, we propose VisHarness, a trainable visual agent that decouples high-level perception, reasoning, and decision-making from low-level task execution. Instead of training a model to solve a specific visual task, VisHarness learns to harness a set of carefully designed heterogeneous visual experts. This paradigm preserves the general intelligence of the agent while fully leveraging the precision advantages of specialized visual models in concrete visual tasks. With only lightweight training, VisHarness learns a generalizable visual expert-harnessing policy and can solve common fundamental vision tasks under various complex conditions through multi-turn interactions with visual expert models. To enable efficient on-policy reinforcement learning training in a live environment, we introduce dynamic visual memory archiving, which mitigates the rapidly accumulating visual-token overhead caused by multi-turn interactions with visual expert models. Experiments on four representative benchmarks covering reasoning segmentation, generalized referring segmentation, dense small-object detection, and referring counting demonstrate that VisHarness substantially outperforms existing general-purpose models and achieves competitive or superior performance compared with task-specific models.

</details>


### [55] [CityGen: Structure-Guided City-Style Synthesis for Cross-City Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29935)
*Zezhong Qian,Zhao Yang,Lu Tan,Zhihao Yan,Weiyi Hong,Haizhuang Liu,Yawei Jueluo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了CityTransfer-Bench基准和CityGen框架，用于评估和提升自动驾驶系统在跨城市部署中的泛化能力，实现零标注的城市适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统通常在有限的地理区域内训练和评估，当部署到新城市时面临可扩展性问题。跨城市部署时，外观、道路拓扑和交通模式等方面的域偏移会导致严重的性能下降。现有方法（域适应、数据增强、合成数据生成）依赖于标注的目标数据、特定城市标注或任务特定设计，限制了其可扩展性和整体评估的有效性。

Method: 提出了两个核心贡献：（1）CityTransfer-Bench：一个地理上不相交的基准，用于评估跨城市泛化能力，涵盖感知、分割和规划任务；（2）CityGen：一个基于扩散模型的生成框架，通过HD地图条件合成和城市级视觉提示引导，实现零标注城市适应。

Result: 大量实验表明，CityGen在多个任务上持续提升了跨城市鲁棒性，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法为可泛化的自动驾驶建立了可扩展且标签高效的基础，解决了跨城市部署的关键挑战。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems are commonly trained and evaluated within limited geographic regions, which hinders their scalability when deployed in new cities. However, significant domain shifts in appearance, road topology, and traffic patterns often cause severe performance degradation under cross-city deployment. Existing approaches based on domain adaptation, data augmentation, or synthetic data generation typically rely on labeled target data, city-specific annotations, or task-specific designs, limiting their scalability and effectiveness for holistic evaluation. In this paper, we introduce CityTransfer-Bench, a geographically disjoint benchmark for evaluating cross-city generalization across perception, segmentation, and planning, and propose CityGen, a diffusion-based generative framework that performs zero-label city adaptation via HD-map-conditioned synthesis guided by city-level visual prompts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CityGen consistently improves cross-city robustness across multiple tasks, establishing a scalable and label-efficient foundation for generalizable autonomous driving.

</details>


### [56] [Mesh-Aware Epipolar Matching for Multi-View Multi-Person 3D Pose Estimation in Basketball](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29953)
*Li Yin,Qin Haobin,Tomohiro Suzuki,Calvin Yeung,Mariko Isogawa,Keisuke Fujii*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MAEM框架，一种无需训练的多视角多人3D姿态估计方法，通过结合单目3D人体网格重建和两阶段极线匹配策略，在团队运动场景中实现了超越现有无训练方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 团队运动场景中的多视角多人3D姿态估计面临三大挑战：(1) 运动员遮挡问题；(2) 团队制服导致的视觉外观相似性；(3) 标注多视角数据稀缺。学习型方法的有效性和泛化能力受限，而无训练方法的性能受限于2D关键点检测精度和跨视角关联的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Mesh-Aware Epipolar Matching (MAEM)框架：
(1) 前端采用单目3D人体网格重建模型；
(2) 设计基于恢复网格输出的两阶段极线匹配策略；
(3) 结合基于并查集的聚类与逐关节三角化，实现鲁棒的跨视角关联和精确的3D姿态重建。

Result: 在两个公共多视角篮球数据集上：(1) 持续超越现有无训练关联基线方法；(2) 在室内外篮球场景中实现具有竞争力的纯RGB性能；(3) SportCenter EPFL数据集上MPJPE/PA-MPJPE为59.8/40.7 mm；(4) Human-M3 Basketball数据集上MPJPE/PA-MPJPE为74.0/51.8 mm。

Conclusion: 证明了密集网格几何用于跨视角关联的有效性，无需目标域训练或微调即可实现优异性能，为多视角多人3D姿态估计提供了一种高效的无需训练解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-view multi-person 3D pose estimation in team sports scenarios remains challenging due to player occlusions, appearance similarity caused by team uniforms, and the scarcity of annotated multi-view data, all of which limit the effectiveness and generalization capability of learning-based methods. In contrast, the performance of training-free approaches is inherently constrained by the accuracy of 2D keypoint detection and the robustness of cross-view association. To address these challenges, we propose Mesh-Aware Epipolar Matching (MAEM), a training-free framework for multi-view multi-person 3D pose estimation. Our method employs a monocular 3D human mesh recovery model as the frontend and introduces a two-stage epipolar matching strategy based on the recovered mesh outputs. Specifically, the proposed framework combines disjoint-set-union-based clustering with per-joint triangulation to achieve robust cross-view association and accurate 3D pose reconstruction. Experiments on two public multi-view basketball datasets demonstrate that MAEM consistently outperforms existing training-free association baselines while achieving competitive RGB-only performance in both indoor and outdoor basketball scenarios. MAEM achieves MPJPE/PA-MPJPE scores of 59.8/40.7 mm on SportCenter EPFL and 74.0/51.8 mm on Human-M3 Basketball, highlighting the effectiveness of dense mesh geometry for cross-view association without requiring target-domain training or fine-tuning.

</details>


### [57] [EVL-ECG: Efficient ECG Interpretation With Multi-Aspect Heterogeneous Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29977)
*Dang Hong Nguyen,Nhi Ngoc-Yen Nguyen,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了EVL-ECG框架，一种跨架构知识蒸馏方法，用于将大型ECG基础模型压缩为适合临床边缘环境部署的高效模型，同时保持高诊断精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型ECG基础模型虽然精度高但计算需求极大，难以部署在资源受限的临床边缘环境中。传统知识蒸馏方法在跨异构架构迁移知识时，无法有效捕捉ECG信号复杂的时空依赖关系，限制了其在ECG诊断中的应用。

Method: EVL-ECG框架提出三项ECG感知创新：(1)多头交叉注意力对齐，协调架构差异以保留细粒度形态特征；(2)基于最优传输的视觉特征匹配，利用最优传输在ECG导联间保持全局结构关系；(3)几何架构内关系匹配，蒸馏教师模型的潜在诊断推理逻辑。

Result: 在多个ECG基准测试中，EVL-ECG相比现有基线方法实现了AUC提升高达2.4%，临床准确率提升1.1%。成功构建了一个高效的20亿参数ECG基础模型，适用于资源受限的临床环境。

Conclusion: EVL-ECG框架有效解决了大型ECG基础模型在临床边缘环境部署的关键挑战，通过跨架构知识蒸馏在保持诊断质量的同时显著降低计算需求，为资源受限医疗场景提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: High-fidelity ECG interpretation is increasingly reliant on massive foundation models, yet their deployment in clinical edge-care remains hindered by extreme computational demands. While knowledge distillation (KD) is a promising solution, traditional methods fail to capture the complex spatio-temporal dependencies of ECG signals when transferring knowledge across heterogeneous architectures. In this paper, we propose EVL-ECG, a framework specifically designed for cross-architecture distillation of cardiac diagnostic logic. EVL-ECG introduces three ECG-aware innovations: (1) Multi-Head Cross-Attention Alignment, which harmonizes architectural discrepancies to preserve fine-grained morphological features; (2) Optimal Transport-based Visual Feature Matching, utilizing optimal transport to maintain global structural relationships across ECG leads despite mismatched token representations; and (3) Geometric Intra-Architecture Relation Matching, which distills the latent diagnostic reasoning of the teacher model. Evaluations across ECG benchmarks demonstrate that EVL-ECG yields improvements of up to 2.4% AUC and 1.1% clinical accuracy over existing baselines. Notably, EVL-ECG establishes an efficient 2B-parameter ECG foundation model, suitable for resource-constrained clinical environments.

</details>


### [58] [Genetically Aligned Patient Representations Improve Hematological Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29980)
*Muhammed Furkan Dasdelen,Fatih Ozlugedik,Ilaria Looser,Rao Muhammad Umer,Christian Pohlkamp,Carsten Marr*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一个将单白细胞图像与染色体畸变（核型）和体细胞突变进行对齐的框架，通过两阶段训练策略，在血液学诊断任务中超越了现有的切片级病理学基础模型，并提供了疾病和基因改变的检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态对齐已被证明能显著提高下游诊断任务的性能。血液细胞学的独特之处在于视觉单细胞评估通常与细胞遗传学和分子遗传学相结合用于血癌诊断，因此需要开发能够将单白细胞图像与基因数据对齐的框架。

Method: 采用两阶段训练策略：（1）在1500多名患者的队列上，使用iBOT头对transformer聚合器进行自监督的纯视觉预训练；（2）通过监督对比损失对急性髓系白血病患者进行基因对齐。框架将单白细胞图像与染色体畸变（核型）和靶向基因面板的体细胞突变进行对齐。

Result: 基因对齐的患者编码器改善了血液学诊断任务，性能优于切片级病理学基础模型。模型提供了现成的疾病和基因改变检索能力。将基因数据纳入患者编码器提高了患者表征的质量。

Conclusion: 该框架与临床诊断工作流程相一致，为未来的多模态血液学专用AI铺平了道路，代码和模型权重已公开发布。

Abstract: Multimodal alignment of histopathology encoders with transcriptomic and genomic data has been shown to significantly improve performance in downstream diagnostic tasks. Hematological cytology is unique in that visual single-cell evaluation is often paired with cytogenetics and molecular genetics for blood cancer diagnosis. In this study, we present a framework to align single white blood cell images with chromosomal aberrations (karyotype) and somatic mutations from targeted gene panels. Our training strategy follows a two-stage approach: (i) self-supervised, vision-only pretraining of a transformer aggregator using an iBOT head on a cohort of over 1500 patients, and (ii) genetic alignment via supervised contrastive loss on acute myeloid leukemia patients. Our genetically aligned patient encoder improves hematological diagnostic tasks, outperforming slide-level histopathology foundation models. Additionally, the model provides off-the-shelf retrieval capabilities for diseases and genetic alterations. Incorporating genetic data into patient encoders increases the quality of patient representations, providing a framework that aligns with clinical diagnostic workflows and paves the way for future multimodal hematology-specific AI. The code and model weights are available at https://github.com/marrlab/GenBloom.

</details>


### [59] [EarlyTom: Early Token Compression Completes Fast Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30010)
*Hesong Wang,Xin Jin,Lu Lu,Chenhaowen Li,Jian Chen,Qiang Liu,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了EarlyTom，一个无需训练的视觉token压缩框架，通过在视觉编码器内部进行早期压缩，显著降低了视频大模型的TTFT和计算开销，同时保持准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视频大语言模型虽然性能强大，但在实际部署中存在效率问题。尽管现有方法能在保持准确率的同时实现极低的token保留率，但它们大多仅在预填充的后期阶段进行压缩，导致视觉编码器本身未被优化。论文发现视觉编码过程占据了首次生成时间（TTFT）的很大比例，因此在编码器内部进行压缩仍有很大探索空间。

Method: 论文提出了EarlyTom框架，这是一个无需训练的token压缩方法。其核心创新包括：（1）在视觉编码器内部进行早期视觉token压缩，而非在编码器之后；（2）引入了解耦的空间token选择策略以提升整体压缩效果。通过在编码过程中而非编码后进行压缩，实现了更好的TTFT降低和更高的吞吐量。

Result: 在单块NVIDIA A100 GPU上使用LLaVA-OneVision-7B模型测试，EarlyTom实现了：TTFT降低高达2.65倍，FLOPs减少高达61%，同时保持了与完整token基线相当的准确率。这些改进显著提升了Video-LLMs在实际生产场景中的部署可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了在视觉编码器内部进行早期token压缩的有效性，为视频大语言模型的高效部署提供了新的解决方案。EarlyTom的提出使得Video-LLMs在实际生产环境中的应用变得更加实用和可行。

Abstract: Video large language models (Video-LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in video understanding tasks. However, their practical deployment is still hindered by the inefficiency introduced by processing massive amounts of visual tokens. Although recent approaches achieve extremely low token retention ratios while maintaining accuracy comparable to full-token baselines, most of them perform compression only at the late stage of prefilling, leaving the efficiency of the vision encoder unoptimized. In this paper, we first show that vision encoding contributes a large portion to the time-to-first-token (TTFT). Therefore, instead of compressing visual tokens only after the vision encoder, performing compression inside the encoder still leaves substantial room for exploration. Based on this insight, we propose EarlyTom, a training-free token compression framework that performs early-stage visual token compression inside the vision encoder, enabling significantly better TTFT reduction and higher throughput. In addition, we introduce a decoupled spatial token selection strategy that improves the overall compression effectiveness. EarlyTom reduces TTFT by up to 2.65x and FLOPs by up to 61% on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU for the LLaVA-OneVision-7B model, while maintaining accuracy comparable to the full-token baseline. These improvements substantially enhance the practicality of deploying Video-LLMs in real-world production scenarios.

</details>


### [60] [VisualThink-VLA: Visual Intermediate Reasoning for Effective and Low-Latency Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30011)
*Mingjian Gao,Wenqiao Zhang,Yuqian Yuan,Yang Dai,Binhe Yu,Zheqi Lv,Haoyu Zheng,Jiaqi Zhu,Zhiqi Ge,Zixuan Wan,Siliang Tang,Yueting Zhuang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VISUALTHINK-VLA，一个用于视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略的视觉中间推理框架，通过紧凑的视觉证据接口和选择性路由机制，在保持高成功率的同时将推理延迟从多秒级降低到亚秒级（在BridgeData V2上实现22.8倍加速）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略中的文本链式思考(Chain-of-Thought)在具身控制场景中存在两个关键问题：(1)无关或弱文本信息会干扰动作预测；(2)自回归文本解码引入过多延迟，无法满足实时闭环执行需求。

Method: 提出VISUALTHINK-VLA框架：1)通过紧凑的视觉证据接口引导动作预测，保持空间精度并避免解码开销；2)采用定制的选择性路由机制学习视觉证据标记，实现低延迟推理同时保持高容量专业化；3)引入VisualEvidence-Kit资源，包含VisualEvidence-Agent构建的754.7k条VLA指令数据集，用于路由监督和反事实忠诚度测试。

Result: 在多个基准测试和真实机器人评估中，VISUALTHINK-VLA在大多数基准上达到最高成功率，并将推理增强基线的延迟从多秒级降至亚秒级。具体而言，在BridgeData V2数据集上，将步延迟从ECoT的8.377秒降至0.367秒，实现22.8倍加速。

Conclusion: VISUALTHINK-VLA成功解决了VLA策略中中间推理的延迟瓶颈问题，通过视觉中间推理范式在保持高准确性的同时实现了实时性能，为具身控制场景下的VLA策略提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent work has begun to equip vision-language-action (VLA) policies with explicit intermediate reasoning. In embodied control, however, textual chain-of-thought is a poor fit: irrelevant or weakly textual information can interfere with action prediction, while autoregressive text decoding adds too much latency for real-time closed-loop execution. We present VISUALTHINK-VLA, a visual intermediate-reasoning framework for accurate, low-latency VLA policies. Our bootstrapping philosophy is to guide action with effective visual thinking: VISUALTHINK-VLA bootstraps action prediction through a compact visual-evidence interface that preserves spatial precision while avoiding decoding overhead. Besides, to further improve performance and efficiency, VISUALTHINK-VLA adopts a tailored selective routing mechanism to learn the visual evidence tokens, enabling low-latency inference while preserving high-capacity specialization. We also introduce VisualEvidence-Kit, a supervision-and-audit resource centered on a VisualEvidence-Agent that constructs a 754.7k VLA instructions VisualEvidence-Set for route supervision and counterfactual faithfulness tests. Across multiple benchmarks and real-robot evaluation, VISUALTHINK-VLA achieves the highest success rate on most benchmarks while reducing the multi-second latency of reasoning-augmented baselines to the sub-second regime. For example, on BridgeData V2, it reduces step latency from 8.377,s with ECoT to 0.367,s, achieving a 22.8 times speedup.

</details>


### [61] [DocRetriever: A Plug-and-Play Framework for Multimodal Document Retrieval with Comprehensive Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30027)
*Ruofan Hu,Menghui Zhu,Jieming Zhu,Bo Chen,Shengyang Xu,Minjie Hong,Xiaoda Yang,Sashuai Zhou,Li Tang,Tao Jin,Zhou Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DocRetriever框架，通过布局感知稀疏嵌入和可泛化重排序器解决多模态文档检索问题，并构建新基准MultiDocR进行严格评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法存在三个主要问题：(1)密集嵌入的粗粒度特性模糊了显式语义，无法利用文档的结构显著信息；(2)监督重排序模型存在泛化瓶颈，性能严重依赖领域特定训练数据；(3)现有基准测试缺乏多样化评估维度和全面相关性标注，限制可靠评估。

Method: 提出DocRetriever即插即用框架：(1)布局感知稀疏嵌入技术，实现有效的混合编码且无需OCR开销；(2)可泛化重排序器，利用推理增强演示和优化采样策略提高少样本设置准确性；(3)构建新基准MultiDocR以实现更严格的评估。

Result: 在多个多样化基准测试上的实验验证了DocRetriever优于现有最先进方法的性能表现。

Conclusion: DocRetriever通过创新的布局感知稀疏嵌入技术和泛化重排序器有效解决了多模态文档检索的关键挑战，为该领域提供了更有效且可泛化的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal documents contain diverse elements, such as tables, figures, and layouts, which can complicate retrieval tasks. While current approaches typically combine dense visual embedding models with supervised rerankers to achieve high-precision retrieval, they face inherent limitations. First, the coarse-grained nature of dense embeddings tends to obfuscate explicit semantics, failing to leverage structurally salient information. Second, supervised reranking models suffer from generalization bottlenecks, as their performance heavily relies on domain-specific training data. Furthermore, existing benchmarks often lack diverse assessment dimensions and comprehensive relevance annotations, limiting reliable evaluation. To address these challenges, we propose DocRetriever, a plug-and-play framework. It enhances visual retrieval via a layout-aware sparse embedding technique, enabling effective hybrid encoding without the overhead of optical character recognition (OCR). We also introduce a generalizable reranker that leverages reasoning-augmented demonstrations and optimized sampling to improve accuracy in few-shot settings. Finally, we construct a new benchmark, MultiDocR, to enable more rigorous evaluation. Experiments across diverse benchmarks validate DocRetriever's superiority over state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [62] [Towards Consistent Video Geometry Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30060)
*Zhu Yu,Jingnan Gao,Runmin Zhang,Lingteng Qiu,Zhengyi Zhao,Rui Peng,Yichao Yan,Kejie Qiu,Siyu Zhu,Si-Yuan Cao,Hui-Liang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ViGeo是一个前馈基础模型，用于从视频序列中恢复空间密集且时间一致的几何信息，支持流式、全序列和长视频推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理视频几何恢复时面临时间一致性不足、推理模式受限等问题。需要一种统一模型，能够在不同推理场景（在线流式、离线全序列、长视频）下都能有效工作，同时保证几何信息的空间密度和时间连贯性。

Method: 1) 基于纯Transformer架构，无任务特定的架构修改；2) 提出动态分块注意力机制，训练时同时暴露双向和因果时序上下文，测试时可自适应调整注意力模式；3) 引入基于补全的数据精炼框架，训练视频深度补全教师模型，利用稀疏噪声标注生成密集、时间连贯且几何可靠的训练目标；4) 统一预测深度图、点图和表面法向量。

Result: 仅在公开数据集上训练，ViGeo在以下任务上均达到SOTA性能：在线/离线/长视频深度估计、表面法向量估计、视频点图估计。

Conclusion: ViGeo通过动态分块注意力机制和补全式数据精炼框架，成功实现了视频几何恢复的统一解决方案，在多种推理模式下无需重训练即可自适应，证明了纯Transformer架构在视频几何任务中的有效性。

Abstract: This work presents ViGeo, a feed-forward foundation model for recovering spatially dense and temporally consistent geometry from video sequences. Built upon a plain transformer architecture without task-specific architectural modifications, ViGeo supports streaming, full-sequence, and long-video inference within a unified model. The key design is dynamic chunking attention, which exposes the model to both bidirectional and causal temporal contexts during training and allows it to adapt its attention pattern at test time without retraining. To improve supervision quality, we further introduce a completion-based data refinement framework. This framework trains a video depth completion teacher that conditions on sparse and noisy annotations and exploits video/multi-view context to produce dense, temporally coherent, and geometrically reliable training targets. Beyond depth and point maps, ViGeo also predicts surface normals within the same framework. Trained solely on public datasets, ViGeo achieves state-of-the-art performance across online, offline, and long-video depth estimation, surface normal estimation, and video point map estimation.

</details>


### [63] [FakeVLM-R1: Internalizing Physical Laws via CoT for Synthetic Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30062)
*Leqi Zhu,Junyan Ye,Kaiqing Lin,Zhiyuan Yan,Conghui He,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FakeVLM-R1框架，通过结合GRPO优化与批判性思维链机制，赋予模型双向辩证推理能力，在合成图像检测任务上实现SOTA性能，同时提升可解释性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于大型多模态模型（LMMs）的可解释检测方法依赖海量伪造数据的模仿学习，缺乏真正的因果推理能力，易产生解释性幻觉，导致检测性能受限。

Method: 在监督微调（SFT）基础上，集成群组相对策略优化（GRPO）与批判性思维链（CoT）机制；推理阶段执行"双向辩证推理"——提出伪造假设的同时调用物理常识构建真实性反证；构建FakeClue++数据集，引入真实图像物理定律引导的标注作为统一真实性锚点。

Result: 在多个基准测试中达到SOTA性能；实现高精度、逻辑可解释的检测；解决现有方法对真实图像的过度拒绝偏差；展示了对扰动的泛化能力与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: FakeVLM-R1通过赋予模型类人批判性思维能力，成功突破现有可解释检测方法的瓶颈，在检测精度、可解释性与鲁棒性方面取得显著提升，为合成图像检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: The development of generative artificial intelligence technologies has propelled the visual realism of synthetic images to an unprecedented level. Although current interpretable detection methods based on Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have made certain progress, they still rely on imitation learning derived from massive volumes of forged data. Consequently, they lack genuine causal reasoning capabilities and are prone to explanatory hallucinations. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose FakeVLM-R1, aiming to endow the model with human-like critical thinking capabilities when performing synthetic detection tasks. Building upon Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), this framework integrates Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a Critical Thinking Chain-of-Thought (CoT) mechanism. During the inference phase, the model executes a "bidirectional dialectical reasoning" process: while proposing a forgery hypothesis, it must simultaneously invoke physical commonsense to construct an authenticity counter-proof. Furthermore, we constructed the FakeClue++ dataset with high-quality samples, which extensively introduces annotations guided by the physical laws of authentic images, providing a unified authenticity anchor for the model. Experiments confirm that FakeVLM-R1 achieves SOTA performance the evaluated models across multiple benchmarks. It not only achieves high-precision, logically interpretable detection but also resolves the over-rejection bias of existing methods against real images, demonstrating generalization and robustness against perturbations.

</details>


### [64] [Native Audio-Visual Alignment for Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30073)
*Longbin Ji,Guan Wang,Xuan Wei,Chenye Yang,Xiangrui Liu,Zhenyu Zhang,Shuohuan Wang,Yu Sun,Jingzhou He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NAVA框架，通过专用的音视频对齐空间实现高质量、精确同步的音视频联合生成，仅需6.3B参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时频联合生成开源方法存在两大局限：双塔设计配合后验对齐会削弱音视频的细粒度协同演化，而三模态完全统一设计将语义条件与低层同步耦合在一起，两者均无法很好地平衡音视频的一致性和独立性。

Method: 提出NAVA（原生音视频对齐）框架，采用“先对齐后融合”的MMDiT架构：首先在专用的交互空间建立音视频对应关系，然后用外部上下文条件化联合去噪过程，实现从模态感知的音视频对齐到模态共享的联合去噪的过渡。此外，引入Timbre-in-Context条件化机制，将参考音色线索与相应语音片段关联，实现可控语音音色。

Result: 在Verse-Bench和Seed-TTS数据集上的实验及用户研究表明，NAVA在仅6.3B参数下实现了更优的视频质量、精确的音视频同步、有竞争力的音频质量以及更强的参考音色可控性。

Conclusion: NAVA成功解决了现有方法的局限性，证明了专用的音视频交互空间配合外部上下文条件化的设计优于双塔后验对齐或三模态完全统一方案，为音视频联合生成提供了更有效的技术路径。

Abstract: Joint audio-video generation aims to synthesize temporally synchronized and semantically coherent visual-acoustic content. However, existing open-source methods mainly rely on either dual-tower designs with posterior alignment or fully unified tri-modal designs that mix textual context, audio and video in one shared space. The former weakens fine-grained audio-video co-evolution, while the latter couples semantic conditioning with low-level synchronization. To address these limitations, we propose NAVA, a Native Audio-Visual Alignment framework for joint audio-video generation. NAVA is built upon context-conditioned native audio-visual alignment: it first establishes audio-video correspondence in a dedicated interaction space, and then uses external context to condition the joint denoising process. Specifically, NAVA is instantiated with an Align-then-Fuse MMDiT architecture, which transitions from modality-aware audio-video alignment to modality-shared joint denoising. Furthermore, we introduce Timbre-in-Context Conditioning to associate reference timbre cues with corresponding speech spans to achieve controllable speech timbre. Experiments on Verse-Bench and Seed-TTS, together with a user study, demonstrate that NAVA achieves superior video quality, precise audio-visual synchronization, competitive audio quality, and stronger reference-timbre controllability using only 6.3B parameters.

</details>


### [65] [OmniCD: A Foundational Framework for Remote Sensing Image Change Detection Guided by Multimodal Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30168)
*Chenhao Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了OmniCD框架，这是一个基于多模态语义指导的遥感变化检测基础框架，通过融合图像和文本提示（如文本描述、语义地图、地理空间元数据），实现了从二元变化检测到零样本语义变化理解的统一处理，并在多个基准测试中取得了最先进的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感变化检测在城市监测和灾害评估等应用中至关重要，但传统方法在不同场景间的泛化能力较弱，难以适应多样化的检测需求。为解决这一泛化问题，需要构建一个能够统一处理多种变化检测任务的基础框架。

Method: 提出OmniCD框架，采用多模态语义指导方法，将图像和文本提示（文本描述、语义地图、地理空间元数据）融入统一架构。框架包含分层场景检索模块和变化检测模块，并引入风格解缠机制以提升跨域鲁棒性。同时构建了RSITCD大规模多模态数据集，包含30万+标注的图像-文本对。

Result: 在多个基准测试中达到了最先进的性能，展现出强大的适应性和跨域泛化能力，成功支持从二元变化检测到零样本语义变化理解的多种任务。

Conclusion: OmniCD框架通过多模态语义指导实现了遥感变化检测的统一与增强，建立了坚实的通用型遥感变化检测系统基础，为该领域的未来发展提供了新的研究方向和技术支撑。

Abstract: Change detection (CD) in remote sensing is vital for applications such as urban monitoring and disaster assessment, yet traditional methods struggle with generalization across diverse scenarios. We present OmniCD, a foundational framework that unifies and enhances remote sensing CD through multimodal semantic guidance. OmniCD incorporates image and text prompts -- such as textual descriptions, semantic maps, and geospatial metadata -- into a unified architecture, supporting tasks from binary CD to zero-shot semantic change understanding. The framework integrates a hierarchical scene retrieval module and a change detection module, reinforced by a style disentanglement mechanism for improved cross-domain robustness. We further introduce RSITCD, a large-scale multimodal dataset with 300K+ annotated image-text pairs. Extensive experiments show that OmniCD achieves state-of-the-art performance across benchmarks, demonstrating strong adaptability and setting a solid foundation for general-purpose CD systems in remote sensing.

</details>


### [66] [LiveSVG: Zero-Shot SVG Animation via Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30174)
*Matan Levy,Ran Margolin,Bar Cavia,Dvir Samuel,Yael Pritch,Shmuel Peleg,Alex Rav Acha,Ariel Shamir,Dani Lischinski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiveSVG是一种零样本SVG动画生成方法，通过视频扩散模型生成目标视频，再使用可微渲染将矢量图形拟合到视频中，实现了复杂运动的SVG动画生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SVG动画方法在处理复杂运动时存在明显局限：基于LLM的代码合成难以表达精细的非刚性贝塞尔变形；Score Distillation Sampling (SDS)提供的梯度噪声较大，且通常需要骨架等类别特定的先验知识。

Method: 1) 使用冻结的图像到视频模型，根据输入SVG图像和运动提示生成可预览的目标视频；
2) 通过可微渲染将原始SVG拟合到目标视频；
3) 提出无骨架的双层运动表示：组级单应性变换用于粗粒度姿态调整，路径级贝塞尔控制点偏移用于局部变形；
4) 引入球堆积重着色策略解决像素级拟合中的颜色对应模糊问题。

Result: 在AniClipart和本文提出的ChallengeSVG基准测试上显著优于现有方法；构建了ChallengeSVG基准，包含复杂的多物体场景；生成的动画与提示对齐且完全可编辑。

Conclusion: 直接参考视频拟合是一种实用且鲁棒的SVG动画生成途径，无需类别特定先验即可生成高质量的矢量动画，为可编辑矢量动画提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce LiveSVG, a zero-shot approach for generating Scalable Vector Graphics (SVG) animations using video diffusion models. Current SVG animation methods struggle with complex motions: LLM-based code synthesis fails to express fine, non-rigid Bézier deformations, while Score Distillation Sampling (SDS) provides noisy gradients and often requires category-specific priors like skeletons. In contrast, LiveSVG fits vector geometry directly to an explicitly generated target video. Given an input SVG image and a motion prompt, we generate a previewable target video using a frozen image-to-video model, then fit the original SVG to this video via differentiable rendering. Our fitting stage is skeleton-free, utilizing a dual-level motion representation that combines per-group homographies for coarse articulation with per-path Bézier control-point offsets for local deformations. To resolve color-induced correspondence ambiguities during pixel-wise fitting, we introduce a novel sphere-packing recolorization strategy. We also present ChallengeSVG, a benchmark of complex, multi-object scenes that exposes the limitations of prior work. Evaluations demonstrate that LiveSVG significantly outperforms existing methods on both AniClipart and ChallengeSVG, establishing direct reference-video fitting as a practical, robust route to prompt-aligned and fully editable vector animation.

</details>


### [67] [Cycle Consistency in Video Object-Centric Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30211)
*Rongzhen Zhao,Zhiyuan Li,Ruonan Wei,Juho Kannala,Joni Pajarinen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出隐式循环一致性(ICC)方法，通过将循环一致性约束从受限的槽空间转移到连续的重构流形上，解决了显式循环一致性(ECC)在视频对象中心学习(OCL)中导致的特征崩塌问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督视频对象中心学习(OCL)旨在发现不同的对象并在时间上关联它们。虽然循环一致性(CC)在多目标跟踪(MOT)领域已成熟，但无法直接应用于OCL的潜在槽空间。与MOT中确定性和理想的对象表示不同，OCL槽由于非唯一场景分解而具有内在的随机性和模糊性。在槽上强制执行显式循环一致性(ECC)会施加严格的均值寻求，严重惩罚模型探索替代但同样有效的分解，从而导致特征崩塌。

Method: 提出隐式循环一致性(ICC)方法，将循环一致性约束从受限的槽空间转移到连续的重构流形上。这种方法鼓励槽在共同解释视觉场景方面达成软共识，而不是强制进行严格的点对点特征对齐。

Result: 在复杂的视频OCL基准测试上进行的大量实验表明，ICC避免了特征崩塌，并且优于ECC基线方法。

Conclusion: 隐式循环一致性成功解决了显式循环一致性在OCL中的局限性，通过在重构流形上施加软约束而非刚性对齐，实现了更好的场景分解效果，为视频对象中心学习提供了更稳定有效的训练范式。

Abstract: Self-supervised video Object-Centric Learning (OCL) aims to discover distinct objects and associate them across time, whereas self-supervised Multi-Object Tracking (MOT) focuses on associating pre-defined object detections or segmentations. Although well-established in MOT, Cycle Consistency (CC) cannot naively or explicitly apply to the latent slot space of OCL. Unlike the deterministic and ideal object representations in MOT, OCL slots are inherently stochastic and ambiguous due to non-unique scene decompositions. Enforcing explicit cycle consistency (ECC) on slots imposes rigid mean seeking. This severely penalizes the model for exploring alternative but equally valid decompositions, thereby driving towards feature collapse. To resolve this dilemma, we propose \textit{Implicit Cycle Consistency (ICC)}, which shifts the cycle-consistency constraint from the restrictive slot space to the continuous reconstruction manifold, encouraging slots to reach a soft consensus on collectively interpreting the visual scene rather than forcing rigid point-to-point feature alignment. Extensive experiments on complex video OCL benchmarks demonstrate that ICC avoids feature collapse and outperforms ECC baselines. Our source code, model checkpoints and training logs are provided on https://github.com/Genera1Z/ICC.

</details>


### [68] [Déjà View: Looping Transformers for Multi-View 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30215)
*Alessandro Burzio,Tobias Fischer,Sven Elflein,Qunjie Zhou,Riccardo de Lutio,Jiawei Ren,Jiahui Huang,Shengyu Huang,Marc Pollefeys,Laura Leal-Taixé,Zan Gojcic,Haithem Turki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DéjàView模型，通过显式迭代架构替代传统深层Transformer，用单个循环模块在推理时可调节的K次细化步骤实现多视角3D重建，以更少参数和计算量匹配或超越更大规模的基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前馈式3D重建Transformer参数量已超十亿，但研究发现连续的Transformer层往往表现出类似操作的重复应用，多视角重建Transformer随解码器深度逐步细化预测。作者认为模型深度部分换取了迭代能力，但以低效的独特参数为代价，因此提出将迭代显式地融入架构中。

Method: DéjàView模型采用单个循环Transformer模块，对每视角特征进行K次细化步骤的循环应用。模型训练一次后，K作为推理时的计算调节旋钮暴露出来。该架构使用共享参数的循环模块替代传统深层的独立参数层。

Result: 在涵盖室内、室外、物体中心和驾驶场景的五个重建基准上，以少量参数和相当或更低的计算量匹配或显著超越更大的前馈基线。关键发现：在匹配训练数据和计算条件下，相同的循环模块公式优于具有独立每步参数的相同变体。

Conclusion: 显式迭代不仅是容量的计算高效替代品，更是多视角3D重建更强的归纳偏置。研究验证了将迭代显式化而非通过深度隐式实现的有效性，为3D重建架构设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Recent feed-forward 3D reconstruction transformers have scaled to over a billion parameters, following the broader trend of increasing model capacity in computer vision. Yet emerging evidence suggests that contiguous transformer layers often behave like repeated applications of similar operations, and multi-view reconstruction transformers refine their predictions progressively across decoder depth. We posit that model depth partially buys iteration, paid for inefficiently in unique parameters, and instead make that iteration explicit in architecture. Our model, DéjàView, applies a single looped transformer block recurrently to per-view features for K refinement steps. Trained once, it exposes K as an inference-time compute knob, matching or outperforming substantially larger feed-forward baselines across five reconstruction benchmarks spanning indoor, outdoor, object-centric, and driving scenes, while using a fraction of their parameters and comparable or lower compute. Importantly, the same looped block formulation outperforms an otherwise identical variant with independent per-step parameters under matched training data and compute, suggesting that explicit iteration is not merely a compute-efficient substitute for capacity but a stronger inductive bias for multi-view 3D reconstruction.

</details>


### [69] [IP-Adapter Is All You Need: Towards Fine-Tuning-Free Diffusion-Based Talking Face Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30230)
*Hao Wu,Xiangyang Luo,Hao Wang,Jiawei Zhang,Yi Zhang,Jinwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需微调的说话人脸生成框架，直接利用预训练的Stable Diffusion和IP-Adapter权重，通过三个无参数组件解决身份漂移、同步误差和时序不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散模型的说话人脸生成方法仍需要特定任务的微调和大规模视听数据集，导致计算成本高昂，阻碍了扩散方法在研究社区中的可扩展性和可访问性。

Method: 1) 基于预训练的Stable Diffusion和IP-Adapter构建免微调框架，利用IP-Adapter的视觉嵌入能力挖掘唇部语义；2) 设计三个无训练参数组件：Structurist（显式解耦和重组唇部和外观特征以缓解身份漂移）、Structure Controller（基于准单调运动趋势自适应精炼嵌入以实现精确唇形同步）、Noise Sensor（引入高斯先验检测并抑制闪烁抖动伪影以增强时序一致性）。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在唇形同步准确性（PCLD至少提升0.16）和视觉保真度（FID至少改善0.7）方面均优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 成功建立了首个无需微调的扩散模型说话人脸生成框架，在降低计算成本的同时实现了更优的性能表现，提升了方法的可扩展性和可访问性。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of diffusion models, talking face generation has made remarkable progress. However, existing diffusion-based methods still require task-specific fine-tuning and large-scale audiovisual datasets, resulting in high computational costs that hinder scalability and accessibility of diffusion-based approaches across the research community. To address this, we propose a finetuning-free paradigm that directly performs talking face generation using the pretrained weights of Stable Diffusion and IP-Adapter. This backbone leverages the visual embedding capability of IP-Adapter to mine lip-related semantics from the pretrained Stable Diffusion. To address the challenges of identity drift, synchronization errors, and temporal instability, we also design three trainable-parameterfree components: (1) the Structurist, which explicitly disentangles and reassembles lip and appearance features to mitigate identity drift and appearance distortion; (2) the Structure Controller, which adaptively refines embeddings based on quasi-monotonic motion trends for precise lip synchronization; and (3) the Noise Sensor, which introduces Gaussian prior to detect and suppress flicker and jitter artifacts and enhance temporal consistency. Experimental results show that our method outperforms existing SOTA approaches in both lip-sync accuracy (at least 0.16 gain in PCLD) and visual fidelity (at least 0.7 improvement in FID), establishing a novel fine-tuning-free diffusion framework for talking face generation.

</details>


### [70] [Beyond 3D VQAs: Injecting 3D Spatial Priors into Vision-Language Models for Enhanced Geometric Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30231)
*Chun-Hsiao Yeh,Shengyi Qian,Manchen Wang,Yi Ma,Joseph Tighe,Fanyi Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GASP框架，通过将几何先验直接注入LLM的transformer层来改善视觉语言模型的3D空间推理能力，无需3D VQA数据即可在空间基准测试上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLM在3D空间推理方面表现不佳。现有方法存在两个问题：(1)基于3D VQA数据集微调容易过拟合数据集特定偏差；(2)集成专用3D视觉编码器不够灵活且繁琐。作者认为真正的空间理解应从学习基本几何先验中产生，而非仅依赖高层VQA监督。

Method: 提出GASP（Geometric-Aware Spatial Priors）框架：1) 将几何先验直接注入LLM的transformer层；2) 使用小型对应头作为跨所有层的深度监督信号；3) 采用双重目标训练，利用大规模视频场景的真实几何：对比损失强制2D视角不变性，深度一致性监督解决3D几何歧义。

Result: 诊断发现标准VLM内部对应匹配准确率极低（常低于5%）。GASP训练将峰值层对应提升至70%以上，保持85%以上的时间鲁棒性，而基线仍低于5%。在下游空间基准上取得显著提升：All-Angles Bench提升18.2%，VSI-Bench提升29.0%，且未使用任何3D VQA数据训练。

Conclusion: 从基本几何先验学习是构建更可靠3D空间推理VLM的一个有前景且可泛化的路径，证明了无需3D VQA监督也能有效提升空间理解能力。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) often struggle with robust 3D spatial reasoning. Prevailing methods that rely on fine-tuning with 3D visual question-answering (VQA) datasets may overfit dataset-specific biases, while integrating specialized 3D visual encoders is often inflexible and cumbersome. In this paper, we argue that genuine spatial understanding should emerge from learning fundamental geometric priors, not only from high-level VQA supervision. We propose GASP (Geometric-Aware Spatial Priors), a framework that injects these priors directly into the LLM's transformer layers. GASP employs a small correspondence head, applied as a deep supervision signal across all layers, and is trained with a dual objective leveraging ground-truth geometry from large-scale video scenes: a contrastive loss on ground-truth point correspondences enforces 2D view-invariance, while a depth consistency supervision resolves 3D geometric ambiguities. Our analysis first provides a diagnostic showing that standard VLMs' internal correspondence matching accuracy is very low (often below 5%). We then demonstrate that our training substantially improves this behavior, boosting peak layer-wise correspondence to over 70% and maintaining over 85% temporal robustness while baselines remain below 5%. These internal improvements translate to significant gains on downstream spatial benchmarks including +18.2% on All-Angles Bench and +29.0% on VSI-Bench, all without training on any 3D VQA data. Our findings indicate that learning from fundamental geometric priors is a promising and generalizable pathway towards VLMs with more reliable 3D spatial reasoning.

</details>


### [71] [BullingerDB: A Dataset for Handwritten Text Recognition and Writer Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30235)
*Marco Peer,Anna-Scius Bertrand,Patricia Scheurer,Andreas Fischer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文发布了BullingerDB——一个大规模历史文档基准数据集，包含20,898页、796位作者跨越六十年的手稿，用于多语言历史文本识别和时间感知的作者检索任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缺乏大规模多语言历史文档基准数据集，特别是包含时间维度和作者信息的语料，以支持历史文档文本识别和长期作者风格变化研究。

Method: 构建基于Heinrich Bullinger书信的语料库（20,898页，499,222行文本），包含拉丁语和早期新高地德语等多语言内容及作者身份、时间等元信息；在文本识别任务上评估TrOCR等模型；在作者检索任务上引入时间感知的temporal nDCG评估指标。

Result: 文本识别方面，TrOCR模型达到最优性能，CER为9.1%；作者检索方面，mAP达到78.3%，时间一致性检索可行，但长期风格变化带来了挑战。

Conclusion: BullingerDB为多语言历史文本识别和时间感知作者分析建立了新的基准，推动了历史文档分析领域的发展。

Abstract: We present BullingerDB, a large-scale benchmark dataset for historical document analysis based on the correspondence of Heinrich Bullinger (1504-1575). The corpus comprises 20,898 pages and 499,222 text lines written by 796 writers over six decades, featuring stylistic variation, multilingual content (mostly Latin and Early New High German) as well as meta-information such as writer identity and time. We evaluate BullingerDB on text recognition and writer retrieval. TrOCR, the best performing model, achieves a CER of 9.1%. For writer retrieval, we introduce a temporal nDCG metric to assess time-aware retrieval. While temporally coherent retrieval is achievable, mAP (78.3%) scores indicate challenges due to long-term stylistic variation. With BullingerDB, we aim to establish a new benchmark for multilingual historical text recognition and temporally-aware writer analysis.

</details>


### [72] [SAM3D-Phys: Towards Multi-Object Interactive Simulation in Real World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30239)
*Xin Dong,Weijian Deng,Lihan Zhang,Tianru Dai,Wenfeng Deng,Yansong Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了SAM3D-Phys框架,通过将场景重建与SAM3D的生成式3D先验相结合,从重建的真实场景中恢复完整的、可物理模拟的物体几何形状,使重建场景中的物体能够进行基于物理的交互模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代多视图重建方法虽然能生成视觉准确的环境,但由于遮挡和观测受限,物体通常是不完整的,不适合进行物理模拟。该研究旨在解决从重建场景中恢复完整可模拟物体几何的问题,实现与场景中嵌入物体的物理交互。

Method: 首先从多视图图像重建场景以获得场景几何和物体的部分观测;然后利用SAM3D从部分观测推断完整物体几何;最后通过两种互补策略恢复场景一致的物体状态:(1)物理约束空间优化算法,迭代对齐恢复的物体到原始位置;(2)掩码引导的外观蒸馏模块,基于观测图像优化纹理保真度。

Result: SAM3D-Phys能够生成适合基于物理模拟的清晰物体表示,实现了重建场景中多个物体的同时且物理一致的交互模拟。

Conclusion: 该方法成功弥合了视觉重建与物理模拟之间的差距,通过恢复完整物体几何并在场景中恢复其位姿和外观,使重建场景中的物体能够进行真实的物理交互模拟。

Abstract: This work addresses the problem of recovering complete, simulatable object geometry from reconstructed real-world scenes, enabling physics-based interaction with objects embedded in the scene. While modern multi-view reconstruction methods can produce visually accurate environments, objects are often incomplete due to occlusions and limited observations, making them unsuitable for physics simulation. To address this limitation, we propose SAM3D-Phys, a framework that integrates scene reconstruction with generative 3D priors of SAM3D to recover physically simulatable objects. Our approach first reconstructs the scene from multi-view images to obtain scene geometry and partial observations of objects. We then leverage SAM3D to infer complete object geometry from these partial observations. To ensure that the recovered objects remain consistent with the reconstructed scene, we restore scene-consistent object states through two complementary strategies: a physics-constrained spatial optimization algorithm that iteratively aligns the recovered object to its original location, and a mask-guided appearance distillation module that refines texture fidelity based on the observed images. By recovering complete object geometry and restoring its pose and appearance within the scene, SAM3D-Phys produces clean object representations suitable for physics-based simulation, enabling simultaneous and physically consistent interactive simulation of multiple objects within a reconstructed scene. Project page: https://chnxindong.github.io/sam3d-phys/

</details>


### [73] [Reinforcement Learning with Robust Rubric Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30244)
*Ya-Qi Yu,Hao Wang,Fangyu Hong,Xiangyang Qu,Gaojie Wu,Qiaoyu Luo,Nuo Xu,Huixin Wang,Wuheng Xu,Yongxin Liao,Zihao Chen,Haonan Li,Ziming Li,Dezhi Peng,Minghui Liao,Jihao Wu,Haoyu Ren,Dandan Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RLR³方法，将强化学习从任务级验证扩展到标准级验证，通过评分标准实现视觉语言任务的细粒度多标准监督，在15个基准测试上取得4.7分的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法仅适用于确定性可检查任务，但许多视觉语言任务具有部分可验证性，需要多标准监督（如感知细节、推理步骤、约束条件）。评分标准虽然提供了细粒度监督的自然接口，但其有效性依赖于在线RL中的执行准确性。

Method: 提出RLR³框架，将实例特定的评分标准通过两条路径执行：(1) LLM提取器配合确定性验证器；(2) LLM作为评判者处理不可验证标准。引入最小暴露策略（对提取器屏蔽真实答案，对评判者屏蔽图像）确保评分忠实性，采用分层聚合优先核心标准，缓解rollout组内分数饱和问题。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B模型和15个基准测试上评估，RLR³持续优于RLVR基线，相比基础模型提升4.7分，超越官方instruction-to-thinking模型差距。受控审计证实确定性验证和最小暴露策略显著降低可利用的假阳性。

Conclusion: RLR³成功将强化学习扩展到多标准视觉语言任务，通过鲁棒的评分标准奖励机制实现有效细粒度监督，显著提升性能同时减少漏洞利用风险。

Abstract: While Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is effective for deterministically checkable tasks, many vision-language tasks are partially verifiable, demanding multi-criteria supervision (e.g., perceptual details, reasoning steps, and constraints). Rubrics provide a natural interface for this fine-grained supervision, but their effectiveness depends on the execution accuracy during online RL. We propose Reinforcement Learning with Robust Rubric Rewards ($\text{RLR}^3$), extending RLVR from task-level verification to criterion-level verification. $\text{RLR}^3$ routes instance-specific rubrics through two execution paths: an LLM-as-an-extractor paired with a deterministic verifier, or an LLM-as-a-Judge for non-verifiable criteria. To ensure faithful scoring, $\text{RLR}^3$ introduce a minimal exposure strategy that masks ground truths from extractors and images from judges. Furthermore, $\text{RLR}^3$ employs hierarchical aggregation to prioritize essential criteria over additional criteria, and mitigates score saturation within rollout groups. Evaluated on Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B across 15 benchmarks, $\text{RLR}^3$ consistently outperforms RLVR, yielding a 4.7-point improvement over the base model and exceeding the official instruct-to-thinking model gap. Controlled audits confirm our deterministic verification and minimal exposure significantly reduce exploitable false positives.

</details>


### [74] [GenClaw: Code-Driven Agentic Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30248)
*Junyan Ye,Jun He,Zilong Huang,Dongzhi Jiang,Xuan Yang,Rui Chen,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GenClaw，一种代码驱动的智能体图像生成范式，模拟人类艺术家“构思-草图-上色”的创作流程，通过代码作为中间画布实现可控且可解释的视觉生成。


<details>
