  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态智能体依赖黑盒图像模型，仅能通过重写提示词进行迭代优化，缺乏直接操作画布的能力，导致大语言模型（LLM）在精确视觉构建中的潜力未被利用。

Method: 设计了一个三阶段框架：首先通过搜索和推理构建概念知识；其次利用代码（如SVG、HTML、Three.js）渲染可执行的视觉草图；最后使用图像生成模型补充纹理、材质及真实感。代码在此充当连接语言推理与像素合成的可控中间画布。

Result: 该方法成功将图像生成从黑盒范式转变为类似人类创作的分阶段过程，实现了程序逻辑与生成模型视觉表现力的无缝集成。

Conclusion: GenClaw证明了将代码作为中间媒介进行分层视觉构建的可行性，为构建高度可控且可解释的视觉生成系统迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Image generation models have evolved from text-conditioned pixel synthesis toward multimodal agents endowed with visual comprehension and tool invocation capabilities. Yet, existing agents remain at the mercy of underlying black-box image models. Their workflow is trapped in a repetitive cycle of prompt rewriting for generation refinement, leaving them with no mechanism to directly manipulate the canvas. In essence, the potential of LLMs to serve as a genuine "brush" for precise visual construction remains largely untapped. In this paper, we propose GenClaw, a code-driven agentic image generation paradigm that empowers the agent to create like a human artist: first conceptualizing, then sketching, and finally coloring. Specifically, the agent first constructs the conceptual knowledge and context through search and reasoning. It then utilizes code (e.g., SVG, HTML, Three.js) to render executable visual sketches. Finally, it employs an image generation model to supplement textures, materials, and photorealism. In this workflow, code serves as a controllable intermediate canvas bridging linguistic reasoning and pixel synthesis, seamlessly integrating programmatic logic with the visual expressiveness of generative models. By transforming image generation from a black-box paradigm into a staged process akin to authentic human creation, GenClaw offers a step toward for highly controllable and interpretable visual generation systems.

</details>


### [75] [Ambient-robust Inverse Rendering using Active RGB-NIR Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30250)
*Hoon-Gyu Chung,Jinnyeong Kim,Hyunwoo Kang,Seung-Hwan Baek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于主动RGB-NIR成像的环境鲁棒逆渲染方法，通过结合RGB环境光图像和NIR闪光图像，实现了在多种环境光照条件下的精确几何和反射属性重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逆渲染方法在重建几何和反射属性时，往往产生不准确的结果，且对环境光照条件敏感。需要一种能够适应不同环境光照条件的鲁棒方法。

Method: 提出了一种三阶段逆渲染方法：(1)利用近红外(NIR)闪光照明获得对人眼不可见但稳定的点光源着色，该着色对环境光照具有不变性；(2)结合多视角RGB环境光图像和主动NIR闪光照明下的NIR图像；(3)开发了一套配备RGB-NIR相机和NIR闪光灯的移动采集系统。通过利用RGB和NIR图像的互补优势实现精确重建。

Result: 构建了首个在多种环境光照条件下采集的多视角RGB-NIR逆渲染数据集；实验表明该方法优于现有方法，在多种环境光照场景下实现了精确的几何和反射属性估计。

Conclusion: 结合RGB和NIR主动成像技术能够实现环境光照不变的鲁棒逆渲染，有效解决了现有方法对环境光照敏感的关键问题。

Abstract: Inverse rendering aims to reconstruct geometry and reflectance of objects from images. Despite recent progress, existing methods often produces inaccurate reconstructions that are sensitive to ambient illumination conditions. Here we introduce an ambient-robust inverse rendering method enabled by active RGB-NIR imaging. Our key insight is to leverage near-infrared (NIR) flash illumination-imperceptible to human observers-to obtain stable point-light shading that is largely invariant to ambient illumination. By using multi-view RGB images illuminated by ambient light and NIR images acquired with active NIR flash illumination, we reconstruct accurate geometry and reflectance by exploiting the complementary benefits of RGB and NIR images via a three-stage inverse rendering method. To enable dense multi-view acquisition, we develop an active imaging system equipped with a RGB-NIR camera and a NIR flash mounted on a mobile base. Using this system, we collect the first multi-view RGB-NIR inverse rendering dataset captured under multiple ambient illumination conditions. Experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms prior approaches, achieving accurate geometry and reflectance estimation across multiple ambient lighting scenarios.

</details>


### [76] [Stable-Layers: Fine-Tuning Image Layer Decomposition Models with VLM-Scored Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30257)
*Ciara Rowles,Reshinth Adithyan,Nikhil Pinnaparaju,Vikram Voleti,Mark Boss*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Stable-Layers是一个强化学习框架，通过仅使用视觉语言模型（VLM）的反馈来微调预训练的图层分解模型，从而消除了对成对监督的需求，在Crello数据集上实现了更强的图层分离和更低的重建误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的图层分解方法需要成对的监督数据（ground truth分解结果），这种配对监督数据的获取成本高昂且困难。因此，本文旨在消除对成对监督的依赖，仅利用视觉语言模型的反馈来指导模型训练。

Method: 1. 基础模型：从Qwen-Image-Layered出发，应用Flow-GRPO算法配合LoRA适配器进行微调
2. 采样策略：对每张图像采样多个候选分解结果，使用VLM进行评分，基于组相对优势优化策略
3. 关键创新：设计了两阶段评估流程来解决VLM评分压缩问题
   - 第一阶段：在五个以编辑为中心的标准上进行结构化的单样本评分
   - 第二阶段：基于网格的校准步骤，让VLM对所有候选结果并排重新评分

Result: 在Crello数据集上相比基础模型取得了以下改进：
1. 更强的图层分离效果
2. 更少的空白或伪影严重的图层
3. 更低的单图层重建误差

Conclusion: Stable-Layers成功解决了从VLM获取可靠奖励信号的挑战，证明了在没有成对监督的情况下，通过精心设计的两阶段评估流程，可以有效利用VLM反馈进行强化学习训练，并在图层分解任务上取得优于基础模型的性能。

Abstract: We present Stable-Layers, a reinforcement learning framework that eliminates the need for paired supervision by fine-tuning a pretrained layer decomposition model using only feedback from a vision-language model (VLM). Starting from Qwen-Image-Layered, we apply Flow-GRPO with LoRA adaptation, sampling multiple candidate decompositions per image, scoring them with a VLM, and optimising the policy from group-relative advantages. The key challenge lies in designing a reliable reward signal: VLMs scoring samples in isolation tend to compress their judgements into a narrow band, leaving GRPO with little within-group variance to learn from. We address this with a two-stage evaluation pipeline that pairs structured per-sample scoring across five edit-centric criteria with a grid-based calibration step in which the VLM re-scores all candidates side-by-side. Stable-Layers produces decompositions with stronger layer separation, fewer blank or artifact-heavy layers, and lower per-layer reconstruction error on the Crello dataset compared to the base model.

</details>


### [77] [PhyGenHOI: Physically-Aware 4D Generation of Dynamic Human-Object Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30268)
*Omer Benishu,Gal Fiebelman,Sagie Benaim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhyGenHOI是一个生成物理准确的4D人-物交互场景的框架，通过结合运动扩散模型和物理仿真，实现文本驱动的动态人-物交互生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在生成人-物交互场景时难以保证物理准确性和视觉真实性。目标是给定静态3D人体和目标物体（以3D高斯泼溅表示），根据输入文本合成人体主动与物体交互的动态场景（如拳击或踢击动作）。

Method: 将人体建模为由运动扩散模型（MDM）驱动的语义智能体，物体建模为通过物质点法（MPM）模拟的物理智能体，使用3D高斯作为统一可微分表示。通过三个耦合机制监督交互：（1）窗口吸引损失，时序同步生成运动以拦截物体；（2）接触驱动重模拟步骤，在碰撞时触发物理一致的动量传递；（3）掩码视频-SDS目标，注入视频先验以增强接触真实性。

Result: 实验表明PhyGenHOI能够在多样化动作、人体和物体上生成物理一致的4D人-物交互场景，性能优于基线方法。

Conclusion: PhyGenHOI成功实现了物理准确且视觉逼真的4D人-物交互生成，为文本驱动的动态场景合成提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We address the task of generating physically accurate and visually faithful 4D Human-Object Interaction (HOI). Given a static 3D human and target object represented as 3D Gaussian Splats (3DGS), our goal is to synthesize dynamic scenes where the human actively engages with the object through actions, such as punching or kicking, in accordance with a given input text. To this end, we introduce PhyGenHOI, a novel framework that couples generative human motion with an explicit physical object simulation. We model the human as a semantic agent driven by a Motion Diffusion Model (MDM) and the object as a physical agent simulated via the Material Point Method (MPM), utilizing 3D Gaussians as a unified, differentiable representation. We supervise their interaction through three coupled mechanisms: (1) A Windowed Attraction Loss that temporally synchronizes generative motion to intercept the object; (2) A Contact-Driven Re-simulation step that triggers physically consistent momentum transfer upon impact; and (3) A Masked Video-SDS objective that injects video-based priors to enhance contact fidelity. Experiments show PhyGenHOI generates physically consistent 4D HOI across diverse actions, humans, and objects, outperforming baselines. Project page and videos: https://omerbenishu.github.io/PhyGenHOI/

</details>


### [78] [Boosting Image Quality Assessment Performance: Unsupervised Score Fusion by Deep Maximum a Posteriori Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30269)
*Zhongling Wang,Raymond Zhou,Shahrukh Athar,Wenbo Yang,Zhou Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于深度最大后验估计的无监督图像质量评估(IQA)分数融合框架,通过融合多个IQA模型的分数来提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单个IQA模型往往对特定类型的图像内容或失真存在偏差,取决于其设计原理和处理过程。因此需要融合多个模型的优势,同时削弱各模型的劣势。

Method: 提出了一种基于深度最大后验(MAP)估计的无监督IQA分数融合通用框架。该方法在分数层面进行细粒度的不确定性估计,以提高融合预测的准确性并降低不确定性。

Result: 全面实验表明,该方法优于单个IQA模型和其他融合方法,并且展现出在融合过程中拒绝"坏"模型的有趣能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的无监督IQA分数融合框架通过深度MAP估计实现了对多个IQA模型的有效整合,显著提升了图像质量预测的性能。

Abstract: Over the past decades, numerous Image Quality Assessment (IQA) models have emerged, aiming to predict the perceptual quality of images. However, individual models are often biased toward certain types of image content or distortions, depending on the design principle and process. An intuitive idea is to harness the strengths and mitigate the weaknesses of each IQA model, by fusing the scores of multiple models into a stronger one. Here we make one of the first attempts to seek an optimal solution for the idea and propose a general framework for unsupervised IQA score fusion using deep Maximum a Posteriori (MAP) estimation. The proposed model conducts fine-grained uncertainty estimation at the score level to increase the accuracy and reduce the uncertainty in fused predictions. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of the proposed model over individual IQA models and other fusion methods. It also exhibits an interesting capability of rejecting ``bad" models in the fusion process.

</details>


### [79] [Grounded 3D-Aware Spatial Vision-Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30307)
*An-Chieh Cheng,Yang Fu,Yatai Ji,Ligeng Zhu,Guanqi Zhan,Zhuoyang Zhang,Zhaojing Yang,Song Han,Yao Lu,Pavlo Molchanov,Vidya Nariyambut Murali,Jan Kautz,Xiaolong Wang,Hongxu Yin,Sifei Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GR3D是一个空间视觉语言模型，通过整合显式2D定位、隐式2D定位和单目3D定位三种能力，利用定位机制作为归纳偏置来增强视觉语言模型的空间理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在空间理解方面存在不足，需要引入定位机制作为有效的归纳偏置来增强模型的空间推理能力，将复杂的空间理解问题分解为基于定位的2D感知和后续的3D推理。

Method: 提出GR3D框架，包含三种互补的定位能力：(1)隐式定位机制，在生成过程中识别实体提及并插入区域token，实现实时引用视觉证据；(2)区域提示的单目3D定位设计，从定位区域查询预测相机视角下的3D边界框；(3)采用内参感知归一化和密集几何监督；(4)将空间理解问题分解为基于定位的2D感知与3D推理的组合流程。

Result: 在定位和非定位空间基准测试上均取得一致的改进，证明了定位作为归纳偏置的有效性；定位能力不仅提升了定位任务本身的性能，还增强了通用的空间理解能力。

Conclusion: 定位能力能够作为有效的归纳偏置增强视觉语言模型的空间理解，多种定位能力的协同作用可以在定位任务之外全面提升模型的空间理解性能，为VLM的空间推理提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: We present GR3D, a spatial vision language model equipped with three complementary grounding capabilities--explicit 2D grounding, implicit 2D grounding, and monocular 3D grounding--within a single framework. GR3D introduces an implicit grounding mechanism that identifies entity mentions during generation and inserts the corresponding region tokens into the text stream, allowing the model to reference visual evidence on the fly when producing spatial chain-of-thought responses. In parallel, a region-prompted monocular 3D grounding design predicts 3D bounding boxes in the camera view from grounded region queries, supported by intrinsic-aware normalization and dense geometric supervision. Together, these grounding capabilities enable GR3D to decompose complex spatial understanding problems into grounded 2D perception followed by 3D inference. GR3D achieves consistent improvements across grounded and non-grounded spatial benchmarks, demonstrating grounding as an effective inductive bias for strengthening spatial understanding in VLMs. These grounding capabilities collectively enhance general spatial understanding beyond the grounding task itself.

</details>


### [80] [City-Mesh3R: Simulation-Ready City-Scale 3D Mesh Reconstruction from Multi-View Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30310)
*Sayan Paul,Sourav Ghosh,Siddharth Katageri,Soumyadip Maity,Sanjana Sinha,Brojeshwar Bhowmick*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: City-Mesh3R是一个可扩展的端到端城市级3D网格重建框架，通过分治策略直接从大规模无序图像集合中重建封闭的水密3D网格，解决了现有方法在大规模场景中几何不完整和表面噪声的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市级多视角图像的3D表面重建面临规模和复杂性的巨大挑战。现有的基于NeRF和Gaussian Splatting的城市级重建方法无法恢复适合仿真的3D网格，存在几何不完整、缺失和不规则噪声表面的问题。而将小规模重建方法扩展到任意大规模城市场景在计算复杂度上不可行。

Method: 采用分治策略的端到端图像到网格重建方法。首先通过拓扑图像聚类、独立簇稀疏SfM和地图合并重建稀疏城市地图，无需穷尽的图像特征匹配。然后空间划分地图进行几何感知相机选择，接着进行密集表面重建和使用曲率感知自适应顶点密度重网格化的表面细化。最后将分块网格拼接生成城市全局网格。

Result: 在城市级重建数据集上的评估表明，该方法能生成高保真度的水密3D网格，具有规则的几何形状，能捕捉精细的表面细节，并且由于其分布式端到端处理特性，可以扩展到任意大规模场景。

Conclusion: City-Mesh3R成功实现了可扩展的城市级高质量3D网格重建，生成的网格适合下游3D仿真应用，为大规模场景重建提供了一个实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: City-scale 3D surface reconstruction from multiview images for downstream 3D simulation, poses highly challenging problems due to the scale and complexity of urban scenes. Existing city-scale 3D reconstruction methods based on NeRF, Gaussian Splatting etc. often fail to recover 3D meshes ready for simulation due to incomplete/missing geometry and irregular, noisy surfaces. Scaling existing small-scale 3D reconstruction methods to arbitrarily large urban scenes is highly infeasible due to their computational complexity. We present City-Mesh3R, a scalable framework for reconstructing watertight surface meshes directly from large unordered image collections. Unlike recent methods which use global sparse SfM point-cloud initialization followed by a distributed 3D dense reconstruction of large-scale scenes, our method follows an end-to-end images-to-mesh 3D reconstruction approach using a divide-and-conquer strategy. The sparse city map is reconstructed via topological image clustering, cluster-wise independent sparse SfM and map merging, without need for exhaustive image feature matching. Then this map is partitioned spatially to perform geometry-aware camera selection, followed by dense surface reconstruction and surface refinement using curvature-aware adaptive vertex density remeshing. These partition meshes are then stitched together to produce the global mesh of the city. The proposed end-to-end framework is evaluated on city-scale reconstruction datasets. As demonstrated by our qualitative and quantitative results, our proposed method yields high-fidelity watertight 3D meshes with regular geometry, capturing fine surface details, and is suitable for scaling to arbitrarily large scenes owing to the end-to-end processing in a distributed setting.

</details>


### [81] [Archon: A Unified Multimodal Model for Holistic Digital Human Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30311)
*Chong Bao,Shichen Liu,Lijun Yu,David Futschik,Stylianos Moschoglou,Shefali Srivastava,Ziqian Bai,Feitong Tan,Guofeng Zhang,Zhaopeng Cui,Sean Fanello,Yinda Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Archon，一个全预训练的人本导向统一多模态模型，用于整体化数字人生成，能够统一处理文本、音频、运动和视觉内容等七种模态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字人对沉浸式交互至关重要，但创建一个统一的模型来整体处理包括文本、音频、运动和视觉内容在内的多种模态仍然是一个开放的挑战。

Method: 1) 使用模态特定的tokenizer统一七种模态，采用原生自回归统一多模态模型在同步模态和72个多样化任务上进行预训练；2) 针对高保真说话人视频中的token爆炸挑战，引入内存高效的语义视频重参数化方法，实现4倍token压缩并保留细粒度动态，配合语义驱动的视频扩散解码器；3) 提出"Thinking in Modality"方法，将模糊的跨模态任务分解为模态思维链的逐步推理过程。

Result: 大量实验表明，Archon在多种数字人生成任务上实现了优越或相当的性能，验证了统一框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过统一多模态建模框架和创新的token压缩及思维链方法，成功实现了高质量、可控制的整体化数字人生成。

Abstract: Digital humans are fundamental to immersive interaction, yet creating a unified model for holistic modalities, including text, audio, motion, and visual content, remains an open challenge. In this paper, we present Archon, a fully pretrained, human-centric unified multimodal model for holistic avatar generation. Archon unifies seven modalities with modality-specific tokenizers, and a native autoregressive unified multimodal model pretrained on synchronized modalities and 72 diverse tasks to model holistic joint distributions. To address the token explosion challenge in high-fidelity talking videos, we introduce a memory-efficient semantic video reparameterization, achieving 4x token reduction while preserving fine-grained dynamics, coupled with a semantic-driven video diffusion decoder. We further propose a "Thinking in Modality" that decomposes ambiguous cross-modal tasks into stepwise thinking in an alternative chain of modality, progressively enhancing fidelity and controllability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Archon achieves superior or comparable performance across diverse digital human generation tasks, validating the effectiveness of our unified framework. Project page: https://zju3dv.github.io/archon/.

</details>


### [82] [VPG: Visual Prefix Guidance for Autoregressive Image and Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30317)
*Xinyao Liao,Qiyuan He,Yicong Li,Jiayin Zhu,Xiaoye Qu,Wei Wei,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Visual Prefix Guidance (VPG)的免训练推理时引导方法，通过对比生成前缀和损坏前缀下的模型输出，改善自回归图像和视频生成中的暴露偏差和前缀漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归图像和视频生成器在训练时使用教师强制（teacher-forced）的历史序列，但在推理时必须从自己生成的前缀中采样，导致暴露偏差（exposure bias）和前缀漂移（prefix drift）问题。现有的解决方案要么修改训练过程，要么应用主要针对外部语义条件（如类别标签或文本提示）的采样时引导，而非检验下一步预测是否为生成的前缀提供强后验支持。

Method: 提出Visual Prefix Guidance (VPG)方法，这是一种免训练的推理时引导技术。VPG通过以下步骤改善下一步预测：(1)对比模型在生成前缀和损坏前缀下的输出；(2)向增强生成前缀后验支持的候选方向外推logits，从而引导模型生成更符合前缀语境的内容。

Result: 在三个任务上进行验证：(1)使用VAR的类别条件图像生成：FID平均降低0.36；(2)使用Infinity的文本到图像生成：基准测试性能提升；(3)使用InfinityStar的文本到视频生成：基准测试性能提升。所有改进均无需重新训练基础模型。

Conclusion: VPG作为一种免训练的推理时引导方法，能够有效提升自回归图像和视频生成质量，在多个生成任务和模型架构上取得一致的性能改进，验证了通过后验支持验证进行引导的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Autoregressive image and video generators are trained with teacher-forced histories but must sample from their own generated prefixes at inference time, making them vulnerable to exposure bias and prefix drift. Existing remedies either modify training or apply sampling-time guidance aimed primarily at external semantic conditions, such as class labels or text prompts, rather than testing whether a next-step prediction provides strong posterior support for the generated prefix itself. We propose Visual Prefix Guidance (VPG), a training-free inference-time guidance method for autoregressive image and video generation. VPG improves next-step prediction by contrasting the model's output under the generated prefix with its output under a corrupted prefix, then extrapolating logits toward candidates that strengthen the posterior support of the generated prefix. Across class-conditional image generation with VAR, text-to-image generation with Infinity, and text-to-video generation with InfinityStar, VPG improves generation quality without retraining the base model, reducing FID on VAR by 0.36 on average and improving benchmark performance on both image and video generation.

</details>


### [83] [MonoPhysics: Estimating Geometry, Appearance, and Physical Parameters from Monocular Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30320)
*Daniel Rho,Jun Myeong Choi,Matthew Thornton,Biswadip Dey,Roni Sengupta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了MonoPhysics框架，通过可微分MPM仿真和3D高斯溅射技术，仅用单目视频即可恢复可变形物体的几何、外观和物理参数，性能接近多视角方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的逆向物理方法依赖多视角视频来恢复物理参数，多视角几何约束能解决尺度和3D结构问题。但在单目场景下，这些约束缺失，导致严重的尺度模糊、几何不准确，以及外观优化与物理仿真之间的弱耦合。

Method: 提出MonoPhysics框架，结合可微分MPM（物质点法）仿真和3D高斯溅射（3D Gaussian Splatting），联合优化几何、外观和物理参数。通过三个视觉-物理桥梁解决挑战：全局尺度对齐、物理感知的几何细化、可微分位置图。

Result: 在Vid2Sim数据集和新构建的弹塑性物体数据集上评估，MonoPhysics在单目设置下超越现有基线方法，且仅用单目相机即可达到与多视角基线相当的性能。

Conclusion: 通过精心设计的视觉-物理桥梁机制，成功实现了仅从单目观测进行精确的逆向物理参数估计，填补了单目与多视角方法之间的性能差距。

Abstract: Existing inverse physics methods recover physical parameters from multi-view videos, where geometric constraints across views resolve scale and 3D structure. In monocular settings, however, such constraints are absent, leading to severe scale ambiguity, inaccurate geometry, and weak coupling between appearance optimization and physical simulation. We propose MonoPhysics, a framework for monocular inverse physics estimation of deformable objects using differentiable MPM simulation and 3D Gaussian Splatting, which jointly optimizes geometry, appearance, and physical parameters from a single camera view. We address these challenges through three visual-physical bridges: global scale alignment, physics-aware geometry refinement, and a differentiable position map, which together enable accurate optimization from monocular observations alone. We evaluate on Vid2Sim and our new dataset of elastic and plastic objects, showing that MonoPhysics outperforms existing baselines in monocular settings and achieves performance comparable to multi-view baselines using only a single camera. Our project page is available at https://daniel03c1.github.io/MonoPhysics/

</details>


### [84] [Veda: Scalable Video Diffusion via Distilled Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30325)
*Shihao Han,Hao Yang,Xinting Hu,Xiaofeng Mei,Yi Jiang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Veda框架，通过统计感知的稀疏注意力方法，将tile选择重构为重建问题，在Waver-T2V-12B上实现5.1倍端到端加速，且不影响生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散Transformer在生成高分辨率长视频时受到自注意力二次计算复杂度的限制，而现有的稀疏注意力方法在高稀疏度下会导致生成质量下降。作者发现生成质量的关键不在于稀疏比率本身，而在于稀疏掩码与完整注意力的tile-wise几何结构的对齐程度。

Method: 提出Veda框架，将tile选择建模为完整注意力的显式重建问题。具体包括：(1)统计感知的tile评分机制；(2)头感知的tiling策略以减少估计误差和结构不匹配；(3)硬件高效的tile-skipping内核将理论稀疏性转化为实际加速。

Result: 在Waver和Wan2.1等大规模视频扩散模型上验证，对Waver-T2V-12B生成720P 10秒视频，实现5.1倍端到端加速和10.5倍自注意力加速，注意力开销从92%降至50%。加速效果随序列长度增长，具有良好的时空分辨率扩展性。

Conclusion: Veda证明了通过优化稀疏掩码与注意力几何的对齐，可以实现激进的稀疏性而不损失生成质量。该方法在各种模型上都能有效工作，为高分辨率长视频生成提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Scaling Diffusion Transformers to generate high-resolution, long videos is constrained by the quadratic cost of self-attention, and existing sparse attention methods degrade under high sparsity. We show empirically that generation quality is determined not by the sparsity ratio itself, but by how well the sparse mask aligns with the tile-wise geometry of full attention. Based on this insight, we propose Veda, a distilled sparse attention framework that formulates tile selection as an explicit reconstruction problem from full attention. Veda integrates statistics-aware tile scoring with head-aware tiling to reduce estimation error and structural mismatch, enabling aggressive sparsity. A hardware-efficient tile-skipping kernel converts theoretical sparsity into practical wall-clock speedups. Experiments on large video diffusion models, including Waver and Wan2.1, demonstrate substantial acceleration with no noticeable degradation in generation quality. To generate 720P 10-second videos on Waver-T2V-12B, Veda achieves a 5.1$\times$ end-to-end speedup and a 10.5$\times$ self-attention speedup, reducing attention overhead from 92% to 50%. Notably, the gains increase with sequence length, indicating that Veda scales favorably with spatiotemporal resolution across models.

</details>


### [85] [NeuROK: Generative 4D Neural Object Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30347)
*Chen Geng,Guangzhao He,Yue Gao,Yunzhi Zhang,Shangzhe Wu,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出NeuROK框架，通过学习数据驱动的运动学状态参数化，在潜在空间中生成逼真的4D动态物体变形，克服了传统基于预定义物理模型的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖预定义物理模型和系统辨识参数，局限于特定物体类别和小规模数据集，难以生成静态物体在各种物理条件下的真实时序变形。

Method: 学习一个表示物体所有可能状态的潜在空间和一个将采样潜在向量映射为合理变形形状的解码器，在大规模4D数据集上训练基于Transformer的编码器-解码器模型，从经典物理的拉格朗日力学角度在低维潜在空间中处理动力学。

Result: 在多种动态物体类型上展示了有效性和通用性，相比现有方法具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 该神经仿真框架成功将复杂的4D动态生成问题简化为低维潜在空间中的动力学问题，为构建全面的3D世界模型提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Data-driven approaches have revolutionized 3D vision, enabling transformers to effectively reconstruct and generate static 3D objects. However, generating simulative 4D dynamics -- realistic temporal deformations of static objects under various physical conditions -- remains challenging and often ad hoc, despite its importance in building comprehensive 3D world models. Most existing methods assume a predefined physical model and use system identification to estimate parameters, restricting these methods to specific categories and small-scale datasets. We propose that these restrictions can be overcome by learning a data-driven kinematic state parameterization for object-centric physical systems. Specifically, we learn both a latent space representing all possible states of the object and a decoder that maps any sampled latent to a plausibly deformed shape of the object. We refer to this parameterization as Neural Object Kinematics (NeuROK), and learn a transformer-based encoder-decoder model on a curated large-scale 4D dataset. This formulation and the learned model significantly simplify the generation of simulative dynamics since we only need to consider the dynamics within a low-dimensional latent space from the Lagrangian mechanics' perspective in classical physics. We demonstrate the effectiveness and generality of this neural simulation framework across diverse dynamic object types, showing clear advantages over prior works. Project page: https://chen-geng.com/neurok

</details>


### [86] [AdaState: Self-Evolving Anchors for Streaming Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30349)
*Yusuf Dalva,Pinar Yanardag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了自适应状态机制来替代自回归视频扩散模型中的静态首帧锚点，通过让模型在每个生成步骤中动态生成场景参考，显著改善了视频的动态表现和场景演进能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归视频扩散模型在生成流式视频时，结构性地锚定于第一帧。第一帧的键值表示在注意力缓存中占据特权位置，吸引过多注意力，从而抑制视频动态性，将场景构图锁定在初始视角，导致生成的视频在时间维度上较为浅层，运动、摄像机移动和场景演进被削弱。

Method: 提出用自适应状态（adaptive state）替代静态锚点，这是一个隐藏的潜变量，模型在每个块中与之一起进行去噪但从不渲染。模型通过同时关注前一状态和当前内容来生成自己的场景锚点，生成随内容演进的参考。该方法将时间视为相对概念，每个生成步骤都具有相同的位置结构，状态转换在每个块中相同，从而在生成过程中引入递归机制，其中去噪作为转换函数，KV缓存作为载体，无需外部模块。

Result: 实验证明，自适应状态显著改善了视频动态性，使生成视频中能够实现更丰富的运动和自然的场景演进。

Conclusion: 通过用自适应状态替代静态首帧锚点并引入递归生成机制，该方法有效解决了现有模型过度依赖首帧导致的动态性不足问题，在无需外部模块的情况下实现了更好的时序动态表现。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models generate streaming video by producing frames sequentially, conditioning each chunk on previously generated content. These models are structurally anchored to the first frame: its key-value representation occupies a privileged position in the attention cache and serves as the primary scene reference throughout generation. As the cleanest and most error-free position in the cache, this anchor draws disproportionate attention, suppressing video dynamics, and locking scene composition to the initial viewpoint even as the scene naturally evolves. The result is a temporally shallow video in which motion, camera movement, and scene progression are dampened in favor of static consistency. To address this, we replace the static anchor with an adaptive state, a hidden latent that the model denoises alongside content at every chunk but never renders. Rather than referencing a frozen first frame, the model generates its own scene anchor at each step by attending to both the previous state and the current content, producing a reference that evolves with the generated content. Unlike standard video generation, which encodes an absolute notion of time, our formulation treats time as relative: every generation step sees the same positional structure regardless of how far generation has progressed, and the state transition is identical at every chunk. Together, these properties introduce a recurrence into the generation process, where denoising serves as the transition function, and the KV cache serves as the carrier, requiring no external module. Experiments demonstrate that the adaptive state substantially improves video dynamics, enabling richer motion and natural scene progression within generated videos.

</details>


### [87] [PARCEL: Pool-Anchored Resampling with Conditioned Elastic Queries for Efficient Vision-Language Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30126)
*Selim Kuzucu,Alessio Tonioni,Vasile Lup,Bernt Schiele,Federico Tombari,Muhammad Ferjad Naeem*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了PARCEL，一种通过池锚定重采样和条件弹性查询实现高效视觉-语言理解的视觉标记化架构，在激进压缩下解决了现有方法的空间定位与细节保持冲突问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉-语言模型在推理时因视觉标记序列密集导致二次计算瓶颈。现有弹性视觉标记压缩方法在激进压缩下存在两个问题：(1)仅空间压缩会导致频谱混叠，模糊细粒度细节；(2)仅查询压缩用非局部摘要替代网格对齐标记，严重降低空间定位能力。

Method: 提出PARCEL架构，动态划分特征提取任务。通过建立空间池标记作为低频布局锚点，并通过池条件查询重采样机制将弹性查询标记条件化于这些锚点上，使查询标记专注于互补的视觉特征而非冗余的空间映射。

Result: 在27个基准测试上的广泛评估表明，PARCEL改进了性能-效率帕累托前沿，在各种视觉标记预算下始终优于现有的Matryoshka基线方法，同时保持了'一次训练，随处部署'的范式。

Conclusion: PARCEL成功解决了空间压缩与查询压缩之间的表征冲突，通过池锚定和条件化查询的协同机制，实现了在激进压缩下既保持空间定位能力又保留细粒度细节的目标，为高效的视觉-语言理解提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) map visual inputs into dense token sequences, imposing a quadratic computational bottleneck for inference. Elastic visual-token compression addresses this by training a single model that can run at multiple visual-token budgets. However, existing approaches struggle under aggressive compression. Spatial-only compression, as in nested pooling, behaves as an imperfect low-pass filter and induces spectral aliasing that obscures fine-grained detail. Query-only compression, as in nested query resampling, replaces explicit grid-aligned tokens with non-local summaries and substantially degrades spatial grounding. To resolve this representational conflict, we introduce PARCEL (Pool-Anchored Resampling with Conditioned Elastic Queries for Efficient Vision-Language Understanding), a visual tokenization architecture that dynamically partitions the labor of feature extraction. PARCEL establishes spatial pool tokens as low-frequency layout anchors and conditions elastic query tokens on these anchors through Pool-Conditioned Query Resampling. This encourages query tokens to focus on complementary visual features rather than redundant spatial mapping. Extensive evaluations across 27 benchmarks show that PARCEL improves the performance-efficiency Pareto frontier, consistently outperforming existing matryoshka baselines across visual-token budgets while preserving the "train once, deploy anywhere" paradigm.

</details>


### [88] [VideoMLA: Low-Rank Latent KV Cache for Minute-Scale Autoregressive Video Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30351)
*Hidir Yesiltepe,Jiazhen Hu,Tuna Han Salih Meral,Adil Kaan Akan,Kaan Oktay,Hoda Eldardiry,Pinar Yanardag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoMLA首次将多头潜在注意力（MLA）应用于视频扩散，通过共享低秩潜在变量大幅降低KV内存（92.7%）并提升吞吐量，在长序列生成中表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长序列视频扩散模型受限于固定滑动窗口KV缓存的内存与延迟瓶颈，且主要优化集中在窗口内容与位置编码，忽视了单头KV布局本身的改进。

Method: 提出VideoMLA，利用共享低秩内容潜在变量和解耦3D-RoPE位置键替代每头KV。通过理论分析揭示MLA瓶颈而非预训练谱决定了有效秩，验证了该架构在非低秩条件下的有效性。

Result: 在每层缓存中减少92.7%的KV内存；VBench测试显示短序列匹配基线，长序列得分最佳；在B200上吞吐量提升1.23倍，且在高压缩比下保持生成质量。

Conclusion: VideoMLA突破了传统KV布局限制，证明了MLA在视频扩散中即使不满足低秩假设也能高效运作，实现了内存效率与生成质量的双重提升。

Abstract: Long-rollout causal video diffusion has converged on a fixed-size sliding-window KV cache, with recent progress innovating within this layout by changing which tokens occupy the window or how their positions are encoded. The per-head KV layout itself, a dominant contributor to streaming memory and latency, has been mostly left unchanged. In this paper, we present the first study of Multi-Head Latent Attention (MLA) in video diffusion. VideoMLA replaces per-head keys and values with a shared low-rank content latent and a shared decoupled 3D-RoPE positional key, reducing per-token KV memory by 92.7% at every cached layer. We further investigate why MLA succeeds in video diffusion even though the spectral assumption often used to motivate it in language models does not hold: pretrained video attention is not low-rank, with 99%-energy effective rank far above any practical latent dimension. VideoMLA retains quality at compression ratios where direct spectral approximation would predict large reconstruction error. We show that the MLA bottleneck, rather than the pretrained spectrum, determines the effective rank: both spectral and random initialization occupy nearly the full rank budget from initialization, and training preserves this budget while adapting within it. On VBench, VideoMLA matches short-horizon streaming video diffusion baselines, achieves the best overall score at long horizons among evaluated methods, and improves throughput by 1.23x on a single B200.

</details>


### [89] [GMOS: Grounding Moving Object Segmentation in 3D Space and Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30352)
*Junyu Xie,Tengda Han,Weidi Xie,Andrew Zisserman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GMOS框架，通过在3D时空空间中对运动目标分割进行建模，直接处理RGB视频以生成3D感知且时间细粒度的多运动目标分割，克服了现有方法依赖2D辅助模态和忽略瞬时运动状态的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运动目标分割方法存在两个根本性局限：一是依赖预计算的2D辅助模态（如光流或点轨迹），缺乏3D几何信息；二是将运动视为序列级别属性，忽略了每个对象的瞬时运动状态。

Method: 提出GMOS框架，将MOS定位在3D空间和时间中，直接操作RGB视频生成3D感知、时间细粒度的多运动目标分割；同时提出前景-背景变体GMOS-S以实现更快的部署。构建GMOS-2K数据集（包含2,210个真实世界视频，带有逐对象的时序运动标注）并形式化MOS-I评估协议，包含三个互补的度量指标。

Result: GMOS在MOS、MOS-I和无监督视频目标分割基准测试中均达到最先进性能，运行速度显著快于先前的多目标MOS方法，并支持流式部署的在线推理。

Conclusion: 通过将运动目标分割建立在3D时空基础上并关注瞬时运动状态，GMOS框架成功克服了现有方法的局限性，为运动目标分割任务提供了更全面的解决方案，在性能和效率上均取得显著提升。

Abstract: Moving Object Segmentation (MOS) aims to discover, segment, and track objects that move independently of the camera. Current MOS methods, however, exhibit two fundamental limitations: they rely on pre-computed 2D auxiliary modalities such as optical flow or point trajectories that lack 3D geometric information, and they treat motion as a sequence-level attribute, overlooking the instantaneous motion state of each object. We address both by grounding MOS in 3D space and time, and propose GMOS, a framework that operates directly on RGB video to produce 3D-aware, temporally fine-grained segmentation of multiple moving objects, alongside a foreground--background variant GMOS-S for faster deployment. To support training and evaluation in this regime, we curate GMOS-2K, a dataset of 2,210 real-world videos with per-object temporal motion annotations drawn from five established Video Object Segmentation (VOS) benchmarks, and formalise MOS-I ("I" for instantaneous), a temporally fine-grained evaluation protocol with three complementary metrics. GMOS achieves state-of-the-art results across MOS, MOS-I, and Unsupervised VOS benchmarks, while running significantly faster than prior multi-object MOS methods and supporting online inference for streaming deployment.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [Lightweight Multimodal LLM-Enabled Cost-Effective Defect Grading of Power Transmission Equipment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28822)
*Tao Wang,Lipeng Zhu,Jiayong Li,Feng Gao,Siwen Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多模态大语言模型(MLLM)的电力传输设备缺陷分级框架,通过上下文学习和低秩适应监督微调,在三个缺陷分级任务上实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力传输设备缺陷分级对电力传输稳定性至关重要。现有机器学习方法虽然在缺陷检测方面表现强劲,但在更精细的缺陷分级领域面临两大挑战:1)难以有效融合专家经验;2)存在类别不平衡问题。

Method: 1)利用上下文学习挖掘商业MLLM在缺陷分级中的潜力,获得SOTA模型;2)通过向该模型发送二次请求,生成少量基于思维链的问答对,降低人工标注成本;3)使用这些高质量可解释的问答对,通过低秩适应(Low-Rank Adaptation)的监督微调方法训练Qwen3-VL-8B模型;4)仅微调语言模型层。

Result: 1)在三个电力传输设备缺陷分级任务上达到SOTA性能;2)验证了仅微调语言模型层即可获得最优性能;3)多任务联合微调证明了单个轻量级MLLM处理多个分级任务的可行性。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了在单一轻量级MLLM中处理多个缺陷分级任务,有效降低了人工标注成本,为电力传输设备缺陷分级提供了一种高效、可解释的解决方案。

Abstract: Defect grading of power transmission equipment (DGPTE) is crucial to the stability of electric energy transmission. Although existing machine learning methods exhibit strong capabilities in defect detection, they are plagued by difficulties in integrating expert experience and facing class imbalance in more refined defect grading field. To address this issue, this paper introduces a novel defect grading framework based on multimodal large language model (MLLM). Specifically, this approach maximizes the commercial MLLMs' potential of DGPTE through in-context learning and obtains the state-of-te-art (SOTA) model. By sending a secondary request to this model, a small number of chain of thought-based question-answer pairs (Q\&As) are generated, which effectively reduces the cost of manual annotation. In this way, these high-quality interpretable Q\&As are used to train Qwen3-VL-8B via Low-Rank Adaption-based supervised fine-tuning (SFT). Experimental results on three DGPTE tasks demonstrate that fine-tuning only the language model layer yields the SOTA performance. Furthermore, multi-task joint fine-tuning verifies the feasibility of handling multiple grading tasks within only a single lightweight MLLM.

</details>


### [91] [What are They Thinking? Delineation, Probing and Tracking of Concepts in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28823)
*Mohamed Abdelwahab,Michelle Yu Collins,Sihan Chen,Yi Cheng Zhao,Zafarullah Mahmood,Jiading Zhu,Soliman Ali,Jonathan Rose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种构建低成本线性探针的方法，用于检测大语言模型（LLM）嵌入向量中的特定概念。通过定义概念、训练层级探针及跨上下文追踪，验证了在多个模型和概念上的有效性，为大规模监控模型“思考”过程提供了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型影响力的扩大，理解其决策机制变得至关重要。目前的挑战在于如何开发一种低成本、易于应用且通用的方法，以便在模型正常运行期间监控其内部嵌入向量中广泛存在的概念（即模型的“思考”内容）。

Method: 1. **概念界定与数据集构建**：通过创建包含概念存在与缺失的对比数据集来精确定义概念。
2. **探针训练与测试**：在LLM的任意层级训练和测试一组线性探针以检测概念，并探究所需探针的复杂度。
3. **跨上下文追踪**：验证探针在更大上下文中追踪概念的能力。
实验涵盖了四个独立概念和三个不同的LLM。

Result: 实验结果表明，所训练的线性探针能够有效检测LLM嵌入向量中的特定概念，并能跨更大的上下文追踪这些概念。该方法在四个不同的概念和三个不同的LLM上均得到了验证。

Conclusion: 本研究迈出了构建大规模概念探针能力的第一步，证明了通过线性探针低成本监控模型内部状态的可行性。随着该流程扩展至更多概念，将能够轻松实现对新型模型的监控能力。

Abstract: As the influence of LLMs expands, it is imperative to gain insight into their decisions. One way to do that is to develop probes that detect the presence or absence of a broad set of concepts within the embeddings computed in an LLM - which is what we might say a model is "thinking" about. Such probes should be low-cost and easily applicable to any LLM, so that monitoring for many concepts is possible during normal operation.
  In this paper, we take the first steps towards developing the capability of creating many such probes by defining and executing examples of the key tasks needed: first, the careful delineation of a concept through the creation of a dataset with the concept both present and then absent. Then, the training and testing of a set of linear probes to detect the concept on any layer of an LLM, including an exploration of the complexity of the probe needed. Finally, we show that such probes can track concepts across larger contexts. This is done with four separate concepts and three different LLMs. When this process is scaled to many more concepts, it will create the ability to easily monitor new models.

</details>


### [92] [A Modular Architecture for Typologically Controlled Lexicon Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28824)
*Sankalp Tattwadarshi Swain,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个模块化框架,用于构建可发音、类型学合理且具有语义结构的人工词汇库,通过概率语法实现了优于现有方法的音系连贯性和类型学真实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建具有可发音性、类型学合理性和语义结构的人工词汇库仍是计算语言学的开放挑战。现有的人工语言生成器要么缺乏形式音系规则保证,要么依赖于不透明、不可复现的基于大语言模型的流水线。

Method: 提出了一个模块化框架:(1)从PHOIBLE数据库采样音位清单;(2)在可互换的音系语法(确定性语法、优选论OT和最大熵MaxEnt)下生成词形;(3)通过Swadesh-Leipzig-Jakarta本体论分配语义,并实现显式的形式-语义对齐。

Result: 在100-5,000个词形的词汇库规模上,通过字符n元组困惑度、对数似然和KL散度相对于PHOIBLE进行评估,结果表明概率语法在音系连贯性和类型学真实性两个维度上始终优于确定性和随机基线。

Conclusion: 该框架成功生成了具有更好音系连贯性和类型学真实性的人工词汇库,相比现有方法提供了更优的解决方案,为计算语言学中的人工词汇构建提供了可复现的模块化方法。

Abstract: Constructing artificial lexicons that are pronounceable, typologically plausible, and semantically structured remains an open challenge in computational linguistics. Existing conlang generators either lack formal phonotactic guarantees or delegate generation to opaque, non-reproducible LLM-based pipelines. We propose a modular framework that samples phoneme inventories from PHOIBLE, generates word forms under interchangeable phonological grammars (deterministic, OT, and MaxEnt), and assigns meanings via a Swadesh--Leipzig--Jakarta ontology with explicit form--meaning alignment. Evaluation on character $n$-gram perplexity, log-likelihood, and KL divergence against PHOIBLE across lexicon sizes of 100-5,000 forms shows that probabilistic grammars consistently outperform deterministic and random baselines on both phonotactic coherence and typological realism.

</details>


### [93] [MechELK: A Mechanistic Interpretability Framework for Eliciting Latent Knowledge in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28825)
*Ji-jun Park,Soo-joon Choi,Jiwon Jeong,Taeyang Yoon,Ju-Wan Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了MechELK框架，将机制可解释性与潜在知识提取相结合，通过定位、验证和提取三阶段方法有效挖掘大语言模型中编码但未在输出中表现的事实和推理知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常在其内部表示中编码事实和推理知识，但这些知识并未忠实地反映在其表面输出中（即潜在知识现象）。现有提取方法（如对比一致性搜索CCS）依赖对比激活模式，在复杂多步推理任务中表现不佳；而机制可解释性工具主要用于理解模型行为，而非提取隐藏知识。

Method: MechELK采用统一的三阶段框架：（1）定位：使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征分析和激活修补来识别承载知识的表示；（2）验证：采用因果探测区分真实的潜在知识与虚假相关性；（3）提取：应用表示工程技术在不修改模型权重的情况下提取隐藏知识。

Result: 在TruthfulQA、精选的欺骗性对齐基准和Quirky LM数据集上评估，MechELK平均提取准确率达到84.7%，比CCS高出6.2%，比直接线性探测高出9.1%。关键地，在模型表面输出错误或回避的案例中，MechELK成功识别出78.3%的潜在知识。

Conclusion: MechELK框架成功地将机制可解释性与潜在知识提取相结合，证明了其在AI安全应用（包括欺骗性对齐检测）中的实用价值，为理解和提取大语言模型中的隐藏知识提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently encode factual and reasoning knowledge in their internal representations that is not faithfully reflected in their surface-level outputs -- a phenomenon known as \emph{latent knowledge}. Existing approaches to eliciting latent knowledge, such as Contrastive Consistency Search (CCS), rely on contrastive activation patterns and struggle with complex multi-step reasoning tasks, while mechanistic interpretability tools have primarily been used to \emph{understand} model behavior rather than to \emph{extract} hidden knowledge. We present \textbf{MechELK}, a unified three-stage framework that bridges mechanistic interpretability and latent knowledge elicitation. MechELK operates through: (1) \textbf{Locate} -- using Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) feature analysis and activation patching to identify knowledge-bearing representations; (2) \textbf{Verify} -- employing causal probing to distinguish genuine latent knowledge from spurious correlations; and (3) \textbf{Elicit} -- applying representation engineering to surface hidden knowledge without modifying model weights. Evaluated on TruthfulQA, a curated Deceptive Alignment benchmark, and the Quirky LM dataset, MechELK achieves an average elicitation accuracy of 84.7\%, outperforming CCS by 6.2\% and direct linear probing by 9.1\%. Crucially, MechELK successfully identifies latent knowledge in 78.3\% of cases where the model's surface output is incorrect or evasive, demonstrating its utility for AI safety applications including deceptive alignment detection.

</details>


### [94] [From Context Shift to Stylistic Collapse: Why Training Objectives Matter More Than Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28826)
*Rohan Mahapatra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM训练管线中的语言特征重塑问题：指令微调导致语言熵崩塌和复杂标点抑制，RLHF无法缓解此问题，但强控制机制可显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM训练管线严重重塑语言特征，导致极端的语言重分布。先前研究关注AI生成文本与人类文本的差异，而本文聚焦于LLM训练管线本身的语言特征重塑问题，探究对齐目标如何重新分配风格概率质量。

Method: 分析17个模型（410M-100B+参数），使用24个语言动机探针；对比基础模型与指令微调模型；测试不同控制强度（lambda值：1.0和5.0）；统计检验发散模式的显著性差异。

Result: 指令微调系统在话语和结构维度系统性地导致语言熵崩塌（平均放大率1,949-16,853%，峰值5,181-209,675%）；复杂标点抑制至基线的3.2-23.2%；RLHF未恶化该效应（p>0.25）；弱干预（lambda=1.0）加剧崩塌240%，强控制（lambda=5.0）改善40.5%，以200-1000倍规模劣势超越前沿模型96.7-98.2%；lambda=5.0实现distinct-4高15%、词汇多样性高27%、重复率低78%。

Conclusion: 现代LLM在RLHF和规模扩展下仍重新分配风格概率质量。当前对齐管线存在结构性局限：偏好优化重塑了标准质量指标不可见但分布探针可检测的语言分布，对AI检测、训练数据污染和长期语言演化具有启示意义。

Abstract: In modern LLMs, linguistic features function not as stylistic artifacts but as probes of probability mass, allocated under training alignment objectives. Language models trained with contemporary pipelines exhibit severe reshaping of linguistic features, leading to extreme language re-distribution. While previous stylometric analyses explored linguistic differences between AI-generated and human texts, we focus on the reshaping plaguing the LLM training pipeline itself. We analyze 17 models (410M-100B+ parameters) across 24 linguistically-motivated probes, documenting that instruction-tuned systems systematically collapse language entropy along discourse and structural dimensions (mean amplification: 1,949-16,853%, peaks: 5,181-209,675%), while selectively suppressing complex punctuation to 3.2-23.2% of baseline frequencies. These effects do not worsen under RLHF, as divergence patterns are statistically indistinguishable (p > 0.25) across matched base and instruction-tuned model pairs. Weak intervention (lambda=1.0) exacerbates collapse by 240%, while strong control (lambda=5.0) achieves 40.5% improvement and outperforms frontier models by 96.7-98.2% despite 200-1000x scale disadvantage. Additionally, lambda=5.0 delivers 15% higher distinct-4, 27% higher vocabulary diversity, and 78% lower repetition than moderate regularization, establishing that alignment requires sufficient control strength, not merely distributional smoothing. Our findings underscore how modern LLMs reallocate stylistic probability mass, despite RLHF and scale. More broadly, our work reveals a structural limitation of current alignment pipelines: preference optimization reshapes language distributions invisible to standard quality metrics yet detectable through distributional probes, with implications for AI detection, training data contamination, and long-term linguistic evolution.

</details>


### [95] [RightNow-Arabic-0.5B-Turbo: An Open Sub-1B Arabic Language Model via Vocabulary Injection and Edge-First Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28827)
*Jaber Jaber,Osama Jaber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了RightNow-Arabic-0.5B-Turbo，一个基于Qwen2.5-0.5B的5.18亿参数阿拉伯语专用解码器大语言模型。通过添加阿拉伯语token、持续预训练、监督微调、直接偏好优化和权重融合等技术，该模型在三个阿拉伯语基准测试中达到35.9%的平均准确率，超越了同类开源模型，以1/3的参数规模与Falcon-H1-1.5B持平，以1/18的参数量达到SILMA-9B 67%的性能，边缘构建版本量化后仅398MB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的开源阿拉伯语大语言模型存在两极分化问题：(1)参数量小于10亿的多语言模型将阿拉伯语作为次要考虑（如Qwen2.5-0.5B、Falcon-H1-0.5B）；(2)参数量在70亿到700亿之间的阿拉伯语专用模型需要服务器级硬件运行（如Jais、AceGPT、ALLaM、SILMA）。唯一尝试的参数量小于20亿的阿拉伯语专用模型Kuwain-1.5B并未公开发布权重。因此亟需开发一个小参数量、高性能且开源的阿拉伯语专用模型。

Method: 该方法基于Qwen2.5-0.5B构建5.18亿参数的阿拉伯语专用模型，训练流程包括：(1)通过均值子token初始化方法添加27,032个阿拉伯语token；(2)在8张H100 GPU上使用FSDP、FlashAttention变长打包和Liger融合核技术，对5.04亿阿拉伯语token进行持续预训练；(3)对129,116对阿拉伯语指令进行监督微调，采用仅响应损失掩码策略；(4)对6,750对阿拉伯语偏好数据进行直接偏好优化(DPO)；(5)对三个检查点进行权重融合(weight soup merging)。

Result: 在lm-evaluation-harness的三个阿拉伯语基准测试(COPA-ar、Arabic HellaSwag、ArabicMMLU)上，融合模型达到35.9%的平均准确率，超越所有同参数级别的开源模型。在COPA-ar上以58.4%的准确率与Falcon-H1-1.5B持平，但参数量仅为其三分之一；以1/18的参数量达到SILMA-9B平均性能的67%。边缘构建版本量化至398MB(q4_k_m)，在单张H100上通过llama.cpp以批大小1实现635 tokens/s的推理速度。所有代码(25个脚本共5,555行)、权重(bf16、int8及四种GGUF量化)和基准测试脚本均已开源。

Conclusion: 本研究成功填补了小参数量阿拉伯语专用模型的空白，证明了通过精细的模型设计、token扩展、多阶段训练和模型融合技术，可以在极小的参数规模下实现竞争力的阿拉伯语能力。该模型在参数效率上表现突出，支持边缘设备部署，为资源受限场景下的阿拉伯语NLP应用提供了实用解决方案。所有资源的开源发布为研究社区提供了可复现的研究基础。

Abstract: Open Arabic large language models split into two classes: sub-1B multilingual models that treat Arabic as an afterthought (Qwen2.5-0.5B, Falcon-H1-0.5B), and 7B-70B Arabic-specialized models that require a server to run (Jais, AceGPT, ALLaM, SILMA). The one published attempt at a sub-2B Arabic-specialized model, Kuwain-1.5B, never released its weights. We present RightNow-Arabic-0.5B-Turbo, a 518M-parameter Arabic-specialized decoder LLM built on Qwen2.5-0.5B. The pipeline adds 27,032 Arabic tokens via mean-subtoken initialization, continues pretraining on 504M Arabic tokens on 8xH100 with FSDP, FlashAttention varlen packing, and Liger fused kernels, then applies supervised fine-tuning on 129,116 Arabic instruction pairs with response-only loss masking, direct preference optimization on 6,750 Arabic preference pairs, and weight soup merging across three checkpoints. On three lm-evaluation-harness Arabic benchmarks (COPA-ar, Arabic HellaSwag, ArabicMMLU) the merged model reaches 35.9% mean accuracy, beats every same-class open model, ties Falcon-H1-1.5B on COPA-ar (58.4%) at one-third the size, and recovers 67% of SILMA-9B's mean at 1/18 the parameters. The edge build quantizes to 398 MB (q4_k_m) and delivers 635 tokens/s at batch size 1 on a single H100 via llama.cpp. All code (5,555 lines across 25 scripts), weights (bf16, int8, and four GGUF quantizations), and benchmark scripts are released at https://huggingface.co/RightNowAI/RightNow-Arabic-0.5B-Turbo.

</details>


### [96] [Micro-Macro Retrieval: Reducing Long-Form Hallucination in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28828)
*Yujie Feng,Jian Li,Zhihan Zhou,Pengfei Xu,Yujia Zhang,Xiaoyu Li,Xiaohui Zhou,Alan Zhao,Xi Chen,Xiao-Ming Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出M2R（微宏检索）框架，通过双层检索机制（宏观检索外部证据+微观重用关键信息）确保关键信息在生成过程中靠近输出位置，从而有效减少长文本生成中的幻觉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在长文本生成中容易产生幻觉，冗余检索上下文和长推理链会放大事实错误。研究发现关键信息越靠近模型输出，事实准确率越高，但现有检索增强语言模型（RALMs）无法确保这种邻近性——它们通过多轮检索注入外部证据，但无法保证关键信息保持在输出附近，存在关键信息-输出邻近性瓶颈。

Method: 提出M2R（微宏检索）框架，采用检索-生成同步策略：宏观层面从外部源检索粗粒度证据；微观层面从推理过程中构建的关键信息库中提取核心结果并在生成答案时重用。训练采用基于课程学习的强化学习策略，配合定制化的规则奖励，使模型稳定习得检索和基础技能。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的广泛实验证明了M2R的有效性，特别是在长上下文场景中表现优异，成功减少了长文本任务中的幻觉现象。

Conclusion: M2R通过创新的微宏双层检索机制直接解决了关键信息-输出邻近性瓶颈问题，为减少长文本生成中的幻觉提供了有效方案，在长上下文设置中展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve impressive performance across many tasks but remain prone to hallucination, especially in long-form generation where redundant retrieved contexts and lengthy reasoning chains amplify factual errors. Recent studies highlight a critical phenomenon: the closer key information appears to the model outputs, the higher the factual accuracy. However, existing retrieval-augmented language models (RALMs) lack effective mechanisms to ensure this proximity - external evidence is injected into reasoning via multi-turn retrieval, but this cannot ensure key information stays close to the outputs. We propose Micro-Macro Retrieval (M2R), a novel retrieve-while-generate framework to fill this gap. At the macro level, M2R retrieves coarse-grained evidence from external sources; at the micro level, it extracts essential results from a key information repository built during reasoning and reuses them while generating answers. This design directly addresses the key-information-to-output proximity bottleneck, effectively reducing hallucination in long-form tasks. M2R is trained with a curriculum learning-based reinforcement learning strategy using customized rule-based rewards, enabling stable acquisition of retrieval and grounding skills. Extensive experiments across different benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of M2R, especially in lengthy-context settings.

</details>


### [97] [Aryabhata 2: Scaling Reinforcement Learning for Advanced STEM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28829)
*Ritvik Rastogi,Vishal Singh,Tejas Chaudhari,Sandeep Varma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了Aryabhata 2模型，这是一个针对竞争性STEM考试（如JEE和NEET）的推理优化语言模型，通过强化学习后训练实现，在STEM推理任务上超越基础模型，同时减少高达64%的输出token使用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞争性STEM考试（如JEE和NEET）需要多步符号推理、精确数值计算和跨物理、化学、数学的深度概念理解。尽管现有大语言模型在通用推理基准上表现良好，但在面向数百万学生疑问的大规模部署场景中，难以提供领域特定且结构一致的问题解决方案。

Method: 利用PhysicsWallah的内部题库构建高质量训练课程，通过可验证奖励的强化学习对GPT-OSS-20B进行后训练。训练方法结合了持续性强化学习与逐步扩大的rollout组规模的广泛探索策略。

Result: 在竞争性考试基准（JEE Main、JEE Advanced、NEET）和分布外推理数据集（AIME、HMMT、MMLU-Pro、MMLU-Redux 2.0、GPQA）上评估，Aryabhata 2在竞争性STEM推理上超越了基础模型GPT-OSS-20B，同时输出token数量大幅减少（最高减少64%）。

Conclusion: Aryabhata 2通过强化学习后训练成功提升了STEM考试推理能力，实现了性能提升与推理效率优化的双重目标，为大规模教育场景中的STEM问题解答提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Competitive STEM examinations such as JEE and NEET require multi-step symbolic reasoning, precise numerical computation, and deep conceptual understanding across physics, chemistry, and mathematics. Recent large language models perform strongly on common reasoning benchmarks, yet they remain difficult to deploy at scale, where millions of student doubts demand domain-specific, consistently structured problem solving.
  We introduce Aryabhata 2, a reasoning-focused language model for competitive STEM examinations, trained via reinforcement-learning post-training. Using PhysicsWallah's internal question banks, we construct a high-quality training curriculum and post-train GPT-OSS-20B through reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. Training combines prolonged reinforcement learning with broadened exploration via progressively larger rollout group sizes.
  We evaluate Aryabhata 2 on competitive examination benchmarks, including JEE Main, JEE Advanced, and NEET, as well as out-of-distribution reasoning datasets such as AIME, HMMT, MMLU-Pro, MMLU-Redux 2.0, and GPQA. Results show that Aryabhata 2 outperforms its base model GPT-OSS-20B on competitive STEM reasoning while requiring substantially fewer output tokens (up to 64\% fewer).

</details>


### [98] [Benchmarking Open-Source Safety Guard Models: A Comprehensive Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28830)
*Reetu Raj Harsh,Bhaskarjit Sarmah,Stefano Pasquali*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文评估了14个开源安全防护模型，在包含79,331个样本的综合基准测试中发现，较小的Qwen Guard（4B参数）达到了最高的召回率（83.97%），而较大的模型如Llama Guard（12B）会遗漏多达75%的不安全内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型（LLMs）越来越多地部署在安全关键型应用中，需要构建强大的内容审核机制。研究旨在全面评估现有安全防护模型的性能，为生产环境中的模型选择提供实用指导。

Method: 1）评估了14个开源安全防护模型；2）构建包含79,331个样本的综合基准测试，覆盖8个NIST AI风险框架安全类别；3）整合四个数据集（HarmBench、StrongREJECT、RealToxicityPrompts和BeaverTails）；4）筛选聚焦于安全相关内容（暴力、仇恨言论、骚扰、性内容、自杀/自残、亵渎、威胁和健康虚假信息）；5）以召回率作为关键评估指标。

Result: 1）Qwen Guard（4B参数）取得最高召回率（83.97%）；2）大型模型如Llama Guard（12B）和GPT-OSS Safeguard（20B）表现保守，遗漏高达75%的不安全内容；3）模型规模与安全检测性能不相关；4）通用型防护模型优于专用型模型。

Conclusion: 研究揭示模型规模并非安全检测性能的决定因素，较小的模型可能在实际部署中表现更优。召回率是安全应用的关键指标，因为漏检有害内容比误报带来更大风险。研究结果为生产环境中的安全防护模型选择提供了重要的实证依据。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications, robust content moderation becomes essential. We present a comprehensive evaluation of 14 open-source safety guard models on a curated benchmark of 79,331 samples spanning 8 NIST AI Risk Framework safety categories. Our benchmark aggregates four diverse datasets (HarmBench, StrongREJECT, RealToxicityPrompts, and BeaverTails), filtered to focus exclusively on safety-relevant content (violence, hate speech, harassment, sexual content, suicide/self-harm, profanity, threats, and health misinformation). We find that recall is the critical metric for safety applications, as missing harmful content poses greater risk than false positives. Our evaluation reveals surprising results: Qwen Guard (4B parameters) achieves the highest recall (83.97%) while larger models like Llama Guard (12B) and GPT-OSS Safeguard (20B) exhibit conservative behavior, missing up to 75% of unsafe content. We demonstrate that model size does not correlate with safety detection performance and that general-purpose guard models outperform specialized ones. These findings provide practical guidance for selecting safety guard models in production deployments.

</details>


### [99] [S3Mem: Structured Spatiotemporal Scene-Event Memory for Long-Horizon Interactive Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28831)
*Encheng Su,Jinouwen Zhang,Jianyu Wu,Qiucheng Yu,Chen Tang,Pengze Li,Lintao Wang,Yizhou Wang,Xinzhu Ma,Shixiang Tang,Aoran Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出S3MEM框架，通过结构化场景-事件记忆单元和锚点敏感检索机制，解决长周期交互智能体在回答早期事件问题时证据链不完整的问题，在多个环境中实现了更优的准确率-效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期交互智能体虽然积累了大量轨迹历史，但仍然无法可靠地回答关于早期事件的问题。主要瓶颈不在于上下文长度，而在于长期记忆的轨迹到答案接口。当历史以纯文本块存储并使用标准检索增强生成(RAG)查询时，系统往往检索到局部相关但链条不完整的证据，特别是在处理空间、时间、重复事件和多跳状态问题时表现不佳。

Method: 提出S3MEM（结构化场景-事件情景记忆框架）：1)将轨迹写入结构化记忆单元；2)通过锚点敏感检索机制检索证据；3)暴露紧凑的令牌预算感知证据接口用于答案时推理。S3MEM作为一个结构化证据利用器，将智能体轨迹转换为查询对齐的支持证据。

Result: 在四个环境（Crafter、Jericho、SciWorld、ALFWorld）上评估，在共享冻结答案时协议下：1)S3MEM在所有四个环境中一致超越Vanilla RAG；2)在Crafter、Jericho和ALFWorld上超越Graph-NoReader，在SciWorld上与之匹配但使用显著更少的证据令牌；3)三个改编的基线（A-MEM-inspired、MemoryOS-adapted、LightMem-adapted）虽在部分设置中优于Vanilla RAG，但无一能达到S3MEM的整体准确率-效率前沿水平。

Conclusion: 在当前冻结答案时协议下，结构化写入和锚点敏感证据路由为长周期交互问答提供了比更通用的记忆接口更强的准确率-效率前沿，证明了结构化记忆设计在长周期交互QA任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon interactive agents often accumulate large trajectory histories yet still fail to answer questions about earlier events reliably. We argue that the main bottleneck is not context length alone, but the trajectory-to-answer interface of long-term memory. When histories are stored as plain-text chunks and queried with standard retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), systems often retrieve locally relevant but chain-incomplete evidence, especially for spatial, temporal, repeated-event, and multi-hop state questions. We propose S3MEM, a structured scene-event episodic memory framework for long-horizon interactive question answering (QA). S3MEM writes trajectories into structured memory units, retrieves evidence through anchor-sensitive retrieval, and exposes a compact token-budget-aware evidence interface for answer-time inference. In this sense, S3MEM is a structured evidence harness that converts agent trajectories into query-aligned support. We evaluate S3MEM on two internal headline environments (Crafter, Jericho) and two out-of-family environments (SciWorld, ALFWorld). Under a shared frozen answer-time protocol, S3MEM consistently outperforms Vanilla RAG across all four environments, surpasses Graph-NoReader on Crafter, Jericho, and ALFWorld, and matches it on SciWorld while using dramatically fewer evidence tokens. Three adapted recent baselines -- A-MEM-inspired, MemoryOS-adapted, and LightMem-adapted -- improve over Vanilla RAG in several settings, but none matches S3MEM's overall accuracy-efficiency frontier. Overall, the evidence supports a bounded conclusion: under the current frozen answer-time protocol, structured writing and anchor-sensitive evidence routing provide a stronger accuracy-efficiency frontier for long-horizon interactive QA than more generic memory interfaces.

</details>


### [100] [A comparative study of transformer-based embeddings for topic coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28832)
*Alex Ding,Tarun Rapaka,Willy Rodriguez,Jason Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了Transformer模型规模（2200万至130亿参数）对主题建模质量的影响。结果表明，在BERTopic流程中，模型大小对主题连贯性和差异性的影响微乎其微，较小的模型即可达到与大模型相当的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主题建模旨在通过词共现模式组织文本，LDA是常用方法。尽管基于Transformer的语言模型提供了更好的文档表示，且模型规模通常影响任务性能，但模型大小对主题质量的具体影响尚不明确。

Method: 作者在BERTopic流水线中系统地测试了七种不同规模的Transformer语言模型（从MiniLM到LLaMA-2），并在多种语料库上使用连贯性（coherence）和差异性（divergence）指标评估主题质量。

Result: 实验结果显示，模型规模（从2200万到130亿参数）对主题质量的影响可以忽略不计。较小的模型能够实现与大型模型相媲美的性能。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在使用BERTopic进行主题建模时，无需依赖庞大的模型即可获得高质量的主题，较小的模型同样有效，这为计算资源受限的场景提供了参考。

Abstract: Topic modeling is a branch of Natural Language Processing (NLP) that aims to organize large collections of texts into coherent groups according to word co-occurrence patterns, with Latent Dirichlet Allocation (LDA) remaining one of the most widely used and interpretable probabilistic approaches. Recent advances in NLP, particularly transformer-based language models, offer improved document representations. It is also known that the size of the model (in terms of number of parameters) has a significant impact in the performance of the language models on different pre-defined tasks. In this study, we systematically examine the effect of model size on topic quality by analyzing the performances of seven transformer-based language models (from small models such as MiniLM to large ones such as LLaMA-2) in a BERTopic pipeline on a variety of corpora. Topic quality is evaluated using coherence and divergence metrics following R{ö}der et al. (2015). Our results indicate that model size, ranging from 22 million to 13 billion parameters, has a negligible impact on the quality of the topic, suggesting that smaller models can achieve comparable performance to larger models.

</details>


### [101] [Assessing Dutch Syllabification Algorithms and Improving Accuracy by Combining Phonetic and Orthographic Information through Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28834)
*Gus Lathouwers,Wieke Harmsen,Catia Cucchiarini,Helmer Strik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文开发了荷兰语音节化的深度学习框架，在字典词、借词和伪词三个数据集上达到99.65%的单词准确率，并证明了结合语音和正字法信息可以提高音节化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音节化（将单词划分为音节）由于存在大量规则和例外，训练高精度算法仍具挑战性。现有研究存在三个主要问题：（1）缺乏对荷兰语音节化算法的全面比较评估；（2）尚未开发基于现代深度学习的荷兰语正字法音节化框架；（3）语音和正字法音节化算法一直被分开研究，从未结合探索。

Method: 研究目标分为两部分：（a）评估现有荷兰语音节化算法的性能，（b）探究将语音和正字法信息结合到单一模型中是否能提高音节化性能。方法上，比较了四种算法（Brandt Corstius、Liang、Trogkanis-Elkan CRF模型和新提出的深度学习模型）在三个不同数据集（字典词、借词、伪词）上的表现。

Result: 数据驱动的算法在几乎所有条件下都优于基于知识的算法。新开发的深度学习方法相比文献中最佳结果，达到99.65%的单词准确率，提升了0.14%。分析表明，添加语音信息能改善音节化性能的单词是那些正字法歧义可以通过发音信息解决的单词。

Conclusion: 研究表明结合语音和正字法信息能够提高音节化性能，特别是在正字法存在歧义时语音信息可以发挥作用。未来研究可以探索语音信息在其他正字法处理任务中的益处，同时新开发的深度学习框架也可应用于荷兰语以外的其他语言。

Abstract: Syllabification describes the task of dividing words into syllables. Due to many rules and exceptions, training an algorithm to perform syllabification with high accuracy remains a challenge. Throughout the last decades, different algorithms have been put forth for Dutch syllabification, yet a comprehensive comparative assessment has not been done. Additionally, deep learning has gained significant popularity within NLP in recent years, yet no modern deep-learning based framework has been developed for Dutch orthographic syllabification. Finally, phonetic and orthographic syllabification algorithms have been examined separately, but not in combination. The aim of the current research was twofold: (a) to examine the performance of existing Dutch syllabification algorithms, and (b) to investigate whether combining phonetic and orthographic information into a single model can increase syllabification performance. To compare the performance of algorithms, four algorithms (Brandt Corstius, Liang, Trogkanis-Elkan (CRF), and a newly conceived deep-learning model) were applied to three different datasets (dictionary words, loanwords, pseudowords). The algorithms show varying performance across datasets, with the data-driven algorithms outperforming a knowledge-based algorithm in all but one condition. The new deep-learning methods developed led to increased performance compared to the best found in the literature (99.65% word accuracy, a 0.14% improvement). An analysis of the words for which adding phonetic information improved syllabification performance indicates that these were words in which the orthographic ambiguity could be resolved by information on pronunciation. Future research could examine other areas where phonetic information can benefit orthographic processing. In addition, the newly developed deep learning frameworks can be applied to other languages than Dutch.

</details>


### [102] [GenesisFunc: Multi-Agent Data Generation for Accurate and Generalizable Function-Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28835)
*Hao-Xiang Xu,Chong Deng,Jiaqing Liu,Wen Wang,Qian Chen,Lujia Bao,Xiangang Li,Zhen-Hua Ling*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了GenesisFunc，一个自动化的函数调用训练数据生成流水线，通过多智能体框架生成多样化场景的对话数据，并结合多阶段评估系统确保数据质量。在8B模型上的微调实验表明，该方法生成的数据使模型在域内和域外泛化能力上均优于同规模开源模型，甚至达到部分API模型的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型通过函数调用（FC）扩展能力，但需要高质量、多样性和场景覆盖广泛的训练数据。然而，获取和标注真实函数调用数据具有挑战性，而现有的合成数据流水线存在API不可靠、工具可扩展性有限、多样性不足和质量控制薄弱等问题。

Method: 提出GenesisFunc自动化流水线：1）从广泛使用的公共基准中提取可靠工具作为基础；2）采用多智能体框架构建对话生成系统，生成跨越多样化场景的对话，同时保持多样性和质量；3）通过多阶段评估系统进一步强化数据的准确性。

Result: 在合成数据集上微调8B LLM后，通过广泛实验证明：1）在域内FC性能上优于同规模开源模型；2）在域外泛化能力上表现优异；3）FC能力达到部分最新API模型的水平；4）方法在下游工具上展现出强大的可扩展性。

Conclusion: GenesisFunc为函数调用训练数据的生成提供了一个可靠、可扩展的解决方案，生成的数据质量高、多样性强，能够有效提升模型的函数调用能力，具有良好的实际应用潜力和下游工具扩展能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) extend their capabilities through function-calling (FC), which relies on training data with high quality, diversity, and broad coverage of scenario. However, obtaining and annotating real function-calling data is challenging, while synthetic data from existing pipelines often suffers from unreliable APIs, limited tool scalability, insufficient diversity, and weak quality control. To address these, we present GenesisFunc, an automated pipeline for generating FC training data. Starting from reliable tools in widely used public benchmarks, our GenesisFunc employs a multi-agent framework to support a dialogue generation system that produces conversations spanning diverse scenarios, while maintaining both diversity and quality throughout the process. The accuracy of the data is further reinforced through a multi-stage evaluation system. We fine-tune an 8B LLM on the synthetic dataset and show through extensive experiments that it outperforms similarly sized open-source models in in-domain FC performance and out-of-domain generalization, while reaching FC capabilities comparable to some of the latest API-based models. In addition, our method demonstrates strong potential to scale effectively across downstream tools, underscoring its real-world applicability.

</details>


### [103] [SERC: LDPC-Inspired Semantic Error Correction for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28837)
*Gyumin Kim,Juhwan Park,Jaeha Kim,Seunggyun Han,Kyungrak Son,Ikbeom Jang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SERC框架，受LDPC编码启发，将文本生成视为语义噪声信道，通过稀疏验证策略高效检测并纠正LLM幻觉，使小模型超越大模型基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型存在严重的幻觉问题，影响其可靠性。现有的内在自校正方法因自偏倚而失败——模型难以在没有外部验证的情况下识别自身输出的错误。

Method: 1）将文本生成过程重构为语义噪声信道，将生成响应视为噪声损坏的码字；2）受低密度奇偶校验(LDPC)码启发，采用稀疏验证策略；3）生成低密度验证查询而非穷举检查所有事实；4）基于外部证据验证以高效检测和纠正错误。

Result: 在LongForm Bio和TruthfulQA基准上使用Llama-3-8B和Qwen2.5-14B评估，SERC超越内在自校正方法和强检索增强基线，在事实精确度(FactScore)上显著提升，使小语言模型在幻觉减少和信息保留方面超越更大基线。

Conclusion: SERC提供了一种无需训练、模型无关的解决方案，相比密集方法显著降低验证开销，在资源受限环境中实现了成本与保真度之间的最优权衡。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities, their reliability is significantly compromised by hallucinations. Existing intrinsic self-correction methods attempt to address this, but often fail due to self-bias, where models struggle to identify errors in their own outputs without external verification. To overcome these limitations, we propose the LDPC-inspired semantic error correction for retrieval-augmented generation (SERC), providing a theoretical framework to interpret and mitigate LLM hallucinations. We reformulate the text generation process as a semantic noisy channel, treating generated responses as noise-corrupted codewords. Inspired by low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes, SERC employs a sparse verification strategy: instead of exhaustively checking all facts, it generates low-density verification queries and validates them against external evidence to efficiently detect and correct errors. We evaluate SERC on LongForm Bio and TruthfulQA benchmarks using Llama-3-8B and Qwen2.5-14B. Experimental results demonstrate that SERC outperforms both intrinsic self-correction methods and strong retrieval-augmented baselines, demonstrating significant gains especially in factual precision (FactScore). Notably, SERC enables small language models (SLMs) to surpass the performance of larger baselines in hallucination reduction and information preservation. Our findings demonstrate that SERC provides a training-free, model-agnostic solution that significantly reduces verification overhead compared to dense methods, achieving an optimal trade-off between cost and fidelity in resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [104] [Specialty-Specific Medical Language Model for Immune-Mediated Diseases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28838)
*Veysel Kocaman,Gursev Pirge,Yigit Gul,Ace Vo,Zhenya Nargizyan,David Talby*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一个针对免疫学和传染病领域的命名实体识别模型，通过专家标注的371个病例报告数据集，实现了0.89的F1分数，显著优于基线和零样本方法，为临床文本分析提供了有效工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从自由文本医学叙述中提取详细临床信息仍然是一个实际挑战，特别是免疫介导和传染病的术语在不同来源中特别不一致，这限制了通用NLP系统以足够的粒度捕获相关生物医学概念的能力。

Method: 与两名临床专家合作，组装并手动注释了371个病例报告数据集，定义了12个实体类别（涵盖免疫介导和传染病及相关症状和临床描述符）。评估了多种建模策略，包括带有医疗保健特定嵌入的MedicalNER架构、基于BERT的token分类模型以及零样本NER系统。

Result: 最佳性能来自在临床领域嵌入上训练的基于transformer的模型，F1分数达到0.89，持续优于基线和零样本方法。专门的嵌入与专家注释的结合对于捕获细微疾病术语和提高异构生物医学文本的泛化能力特别有价值。提示的LLM基线在相同评估协议下表现明显较差，反映出在生成细粒度实体边界的跨度一致输出方面的困难。

Conclusion: 该模型为分析病例报告提供了结构化方法，可支持下游任务如队列识别、疾病监测和临床决策支持。专门领域嵌入与专家标注的协同作用有效解决了疾病术语不一致问题。

Abstract: Extracting detailed clinical information from free-text medical narratives remains a practical challenge for researchers and healthcare systems. Terminology for immune-mediated and infectious diseases is especially inconsistent across sources, which often limits the ability of general-purpose Natural Language Processing (NLP) systems to capture the relevant biomedical concepts with sufficient granularity. We developed a domain-specific Named Entity Recognition (NER) model tailored to identify disease-related entities occurring in immunology and infectious disease contexts. We assembled and manually annotated a dataset of 371 case reports in collaboration with two clinical specialists, defining twelve entity classes covering immune-mediated and infectious conditions as well as related symptoms and clinical descriptors. We evaluated several modeling strategies, including the MedicalNER architecture with multiple healthcare-specific embeddings, a BERT-based token classification model, and zero-shot NER systems. The strongest performance was obtained with a transformer-based model trained on clinical-domain embeddings, which reached an F1 score of 0.89, consistently outperforming baseline and zero-shot approaches. The combination of specialized embeddings and expert annotation proved particularly valuable for capturing nuanced disease terminology and improving generalization across heterogeneous biomedical text. The prompted LLM baseline achieved substantially lower performance under the same evaluation protocol, reflecting difficulties in producing span-consistent outputs for fine-grained entity boundaries despite detailed prompting. The resulting model provides a structured way to analyze case reports and can support downstream tasks such as cohort identification, disease monitoring, and clinical decision support.

</details>


### [105] [Thoughts-as-Planning: Latent World Models for Chain-of-Thoughts Optimization via Reinforcement Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28842)
*Dong Liu,Yanxuan Yu,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了Thoughts-as-Planning框架，将推理链优化形式化为潜在语义空间上的序列决策过程，通过学习潜在世界模型和支持多尺度抽象，在效率和可解释性方面超越了现有的推理链调优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在各种NLP任务中的成功使得推理链优化成为对齐模型行为与任务目标的关键步骤。然而，现有的推理链调优方法通常依赖于黑盒启发式方法或无梯度搜索，缺乏可解释性、泛化能力和样本效率。

Method: 提出Thoughts-as-Planning框架，将推理链优化形式化为潜在语义空间上的序列决策过程。将LLM建模为部分可观察环境，学习潜在世界模型来模拟推理链编辑对下游输出的影响。构建保持邻近性的嵌入空间来编码推理链-响应动态，通过梯度下降或强化学习进行规划。方法支持多尺度抽象，允许在token、segment和instruction级别进行推理链编辑并集成到统一的规划器中。

Result: 在语言理解和生成任务上的大量实验表明，Thoughts-as-Planning在效率、鲁棒性和泛化能力方面优于最先进的推理链调优基线方法，同时通过其结构化规划轨迹提供可解释性。

Conclusion: Thoughts-as-Planning框架成功解决了现有推理链调优方法的局限性，提供了一个更高效、鲁棒且可解释的解决方案，为LLM推理链优化开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: The success of large language models (LLMs) across diverse NLP tasks has elevated the importance of reasoning chain optimization as a critical step in aligning model behavior with task objectives. Existing reasoning chain tuning methods often rely on black-box heuristics or gradient-free search, which lack interpretability, generalization, and sample efficiency. In this work, we introduce \textbf{Thoughts-as-Planning}, a novel framework that formalizes reasoning chain optimization as a sequential decision-making process over a latent semantic space. We model the LLM as a partially observable environment and learn a latent world model that simulates the effect of reasoning chain edits on downstream outputs. A proximity-preserving embedding space is constructed to encode reasoning chain-response dynamics, enabling planning via gradient descent or reinforcement learning. Our method supports multi-scale abstraction, allowing reasoning chain edits at token, segment, and instruction levels to be integrated into a unified planner. Through extensive experiments on language understanding and generation tasks, we demonstrate that Thoughts-as-Planning outperforms state-of-the-art reasoning chain tuning baselines in efficiency, robustness, and generalization, while offering interpretability through its structured planning trajectory. Our code is available at https://github.com/FastLM/Thoughts-as-Planning.

</details>


### [106] [GPF-LiveNews: A Streaming Evaluation Protocol for Group-Conditioned Framing in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28848)
*Mohd Ariful Haque,Fahad Rahman,Kishor Datta Gupta,Roy George*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了GPF-LIVENEWS，一种用于审计大型语言模型(LLM)输出中群体条件化框架(framing)的流式评估协议和基准测试，通过扩展BBC/Reuters新闻锚点至42个身份标签和7个提示族，对23个托管模型进行了12次监控运行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署后的语言模型运行在非平稳环境中：模型版本、检索层、安全系统和现实世界输入都随时间变化。静态偏见基准虽然有用，但无法展示模型如何为不同提示受众构建新兴事件的框架，因此需要一种动态评估方法来审计LLM在新事件中的群体条件化框架表现。

Method: 引入GPF-LIVENEWS流式评估协议：(1)将BBC/Reuters新闻锚点扩展到42个身份标签和7个提示族；(2)使用语义敏感性和情感差异信号评估响应包；(3)进行12次监控运行，评估23个托管模型；(4)发布包含文章元数据、提示模板、实例化提示、模型输出元数据、评分表、文档和复现脚本的完整数据集。

Result: 在12次监控运行和23个托管模型的试点研究中发现：(1)Policy/Action提示产生最强的语义移动；(2)情感变化在各维度和提示族之间相对平稳；(3)发布了包含所有相关数据和代码的完整数据集。

Conclusion: 该研究将所有评分解释为观察窗口期的人类审查审计信号，而非永久性的公平性排名或有害偏见的直接证据。这强调了评估结果应作为持续监控工具，而非静态评判标准，为LLM在新事件中的群体框架化行为提供了动态审计框架。

Abstract: Deployed language models are evaluated in a non-stationary environment: model versions, retrieval layers, safety systems, and real-world inputs all change over time. Static bias benchmarks remain useful, but they do not show how models frame newly emerging events for different prompted audiences. We introduce GPF-LIVENEWS, a streaming evaluation protocol and benchmark snapshot for auditing group-conditioned framing in open-ended LLM outputs. The protocol expands fresh BBC/Reuters news anchors across 42 identity labels and seven prompt families, then evaluates response bundles using semantic-sensitivity and sentiment-disparity signals. In a pilot over 12 monitoring runs and 23 hosted models, Policy/Action prompts produce the strongest semantic movement, while sentiment variation is flatter across dimensions and prompt families. The released artifact includes article metadata, prompt templates, instantiated prompts, model-output metadata, score tables, documentation, and reproduction scripts. We interpret all scores as observed-window audit signals for human review, not as permanent fairness rankings or direct proof of harmful bias.

</details>


### [107] [Large language models reorganize representational geometry during in-context learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28854)
*Hua-Dong Xiong,Li Ji-An,Robert C. Wilson,Kwonjoon Lee,Xue-Xin Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究从神经科学视角出发，探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）表征空间的几何结构如何影响上下文学习（ICL）的有效性，发现ICL性能与表征结构系统相关，成功的ICL伴随着表征的几何重组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明LLMs能够通过上下文学习（ICL）在无需参数更新的情况下适应新任务，且已有机制分析识别了支持该行为的关键回路。然而，由于上下文计算依赖于高维表征空间中的变换，目前尚不清楚该空间的几何结构如何影响ICL的有效性。

Method: 受神经科学中将分类视为神经表征解缠的观点启发，作者假设ICL依赖于任务相关表征的成功在线解缠。为验证这一假设，研究者让LLMs对上下文示例进行分类，这些示例的标签由模型自身的内部表征定义，且具有已知结构，从而研究ICL性能与表征结构之间的关系。

Result: 研究发现：1）ICL性能与底层分类任务的表征结构系统相关；2）成功的ICL伴随着几何重组，增加了在线可分性；3）LLM的行为可用原型式算法描述，该算法在重塑表征以支持分类的同时整合证据。

Conclusion: 本研究为预训练LLMs中的ICL提供了几何学解释，确立了表征几何作为ICL的机制约束，并量化了预训练表征所能提供的与上下文学习所能利用的内容之间的差距。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit remarkable flexibility: they can adapt to novel tasks from in-context examples without any parameter updates, a capability known as in-context learning (ICL). Prior work on synthetic tasks has shown that ICL can implement specific algorithms, demonstrating architectural competence, and mechanistic analyses have identified key circuits that support this behavior. However, because in-context computation -- regardless of its algorithmic form -- relies on transformations in high-dimensional representation space, it remains unclear how the geometry of that space shapes ICL effectiveness. Motivated by the neuroscience view of classification as the untangling of neural representations, we hypothesize that ICL depends on the successful online untangling of task-relevant representations. To test this idea, we study how LLMs classify in-context examples whose labels are defined by the model's own internal representations with known structure. We show that ICL performance correlates systematically with the representational structure of the underlying classification task and that successful ICL is accompanied by geometric reorganization that increases online separability. We further find that LLM behavior is well described by a prototype-like algorithm that integrates evidence while reshaping representations to support classification. These findings offer a geometric account of ICL in pretrained LLMs, establish representational geometry as a mechanistic constraint on ICL, and quantify the gap between what pretrained representations afford and what in-context learning can exploit.

</details>


### [108] [From Data to Insights: Exploring Program-of-Thoughts Prompting for Chart Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28874)
*Yutong Qu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种零样本学习方法，通过Python程序作为中介，结合轻量级视觉语言模型和Program-of-Thought策略，并引入图表转字典的新颖辅助任务，实现了与现有方法相当的图表摘要性能，同时更具计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图表在传达数值数据洞察方面发挥关键作用，但语义视觉理解和数值推理需求使得图表描述和摘要成为具有挑战性的任务。现有的视觉语言模型(VLMs)缺乏验证统计事实正确性的鲁棒机制，且计算开销较大，难以满足实际应用需求。

Method: 采用零样本学习策略激励轻量级VLMs执行计算推理，以Python程序为中介推导有效的摘要统计数据用于图表理解。具体提出新颖的图表转字典(chart-to-dictionary)辅助任务，相比传统图表转表格方法提供更灵活的表示形式，并与Program-of-Thought(PoT)策略良好集成。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在语义和事实性评估指标上与现有图表摘要方法表现相当，在保证性能的同时实现了轻量化计算。

Conclusion: 零样本学习结合PoT策略和图表转字典任务能够有效解决图表摘要中的统计事实验证问题，在保持性能的同时降低了计算复杂度，为图表理解任务提供了一种高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Charts play a critical role in conveying numerical data insights through structured visual representations. However, semantic visual understanding and numerical reasoning requirements hinder the accurate description of charts, interpreting a challenging task in chart summarization. Despite recent advancements in visual language models (VLMs), approaches lack robust mechanisms for verifying statistical fact correctness and are computationally heavy. To address this gap, this paper explores a strategy of using zero-shot learning to motivate the lightweight VLMs to perform computational reasoning, via Python programs as intermediaries to derive valid summary statistics for chart understanding. Specifically, we introduce a novel chart-to-dictionary auxiliary task, offering a more flexible representation compared to traditional chart-to-table methods, making it particularly well-suited for integration with the Program-of-Thought (PoT) strategy. Experimental results demonstrate our strategy performs on par with existing chart summarization methods across semantic and factual metrics. Code is available on https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ZeroShot-PoT-C2T-5A6B.

</details>


### [109] [Hallucination Detection-Guided Preference Optimization for Clinical Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28910)
*Shamanth Kuthpadi Seethakantha,Dung Ngoc Thai,Vara Prasad Gudi,Simran Tiwari,Rami Matar,Avijit Mitra,Wenlong Zhao,Wael Salloum,Andrew McCallum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了IterSum和IterSum-PL两种方法，通过幻觉检测器引导迭代修正和偏好学习，显著减少LLM在临床文本摘要中的幻觉问题，同时保持摘要质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在摘要任务中表现出色，但在专业医疗应用中经常产生幻觉（不受支持或错误的陈述），这严重限制了其在临床场景中的可靠性和实用性。

Method: 提出IterSum方法，利用幻觉检测器引导推理时的迭代式摘要修正；进一步提出IterSum-PL，将检测器引导的修正轨迹转换为偏好对用于模型微调。两种方法分别在推理阶段和训练阶段提升事实准确性。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV临床笔记摘要任务上，IterSum将Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct的幻觉减少24%，IterSum-PL减少48%；人类专家和LLM-Jury评估表明两种方法均保持了摘要的流畅性、连贯性和相关性。

Conclusion: 检测器引导的迭代修正和偏好学习方法为提高临床摘要的事实准确性提供了有效的自动化解决方案，在显著降低幻觉的同时保持了摘要质量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise on summarization tasks, but they often produce hallucinations, which are unsupported or incorrect statements that limit their reliability in specialized healthcare applications. We introduce \itermodelfull (\itermodel), an inference-time method that leverages hallucination detectors to guide iterative summary revisions toward factual corrections. Building on this, we propose \itermodel for Preference Learning (\model), which converts detector-guided refinement trajectories into preference pairs for model finetuning. Extensive experiments show that our methods substantially reduce hallucinations for Llama and Gemma models in summarizing real-world clinical notes from \MimicIV. For example, \itermodel reduces 24\% and \model reduces 48\% hallucinations in Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct.
