Method: 提出EviLink框架，将schema linking重构为不确定性感知的schema需求推断问题：1) 多假设schema基础：考虑多个合理的SQL路径；2) 区分必需schema项与路径依赖的不确定项；3) 不确定性引导的证据获取：仅在需要时获取证据。

Result: 在BIRD-Dev和Spider2-Snow数据集上的实验表明，EviLink有效平衡了schema完整性、schema相关性和token成本。在Spider2-Snow上实现90.15%字段级严格召回率，平均使用123.30K tokens，在固定生成器下改善了下游SQL生成性能。

Conclusion: 将schema linking建模为不确定性感知的多路径推断问题是一个有效的新视角，相比传统的单路径确定性选择方法，能够更准确地识别schema需求并优化资源使用，为大规模Text-to-SQL任务提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Schema linking is a difficult and important step in large-scale Text-to-SQL, where systems must identify a compact yet sufficient schema context from large and ambiguous databases. Existing methods often treat schema linking as deterministic selection around a single SQL path, but complex questions may admit multiple valid realizations with different schema needs. We reframe schema linking as uncertainty-aware schema-need inference over multiple plausible SQL paths, where the system distinguishes required schema items from path-dependent uncertain ones and acquires evidence only where needed. We instantiate this reframing with EviLink, which combines multi-hypothesis schema grounding with uncertainty-guided evidence acquisition. Experiments on BIRD-Dev and Spider2-Snow show that this perspective improves the balance among schema completeness, schema relevance, and token cost. On Spider2-Snow, EviLink achieves 90.15% field-level strict recall rate, uses 123.30K average tokens, and improves downstream SQL generation under a fixed generator.

</details>


### [148] [Spurious Prompts: Can Irrelevant Prompts Steer Large Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29678)
*Pawel Batorski,Abtin Pourhadi,Jerzy Sarosiek,Przemyslaw Spurek,Paul Swoboda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现并验证了“虚假提示”（spurious prompts）现象——即与任务语义无关的提示词仍能有效引导大语言模型的行为，既能提升性能也能导致非预期行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注任务相关指令、示例或推理线索对模型的影响。本文作者探索一种不同的提示词敏感性：与任务语义无关的提示词是否仍能系统地影响模型行为。

Method: 提出“虚假提示”概念并设计简单的黑盒搜索方法来自动发现此类提示词。在推理和问答基准测试上，使用参数量从0.8B到27B的三个模型家族进行实验验证。

Result: 虚假提示可显著提升性能，在多项任务上匹配或超越标准提示基线及任务感知的提示优化方法。同时，虚假提示可诱导模型产生非预期行为，如总是选择第一个选项、输出错误答案、返回偶数/质数/小数等，而无需显式指令。

Conclusion: 大语言模型存在一种新型提示词敏感性：可被与任务语义无关的提示词系统性地引导，这对理解模型行为、优化提示策略及评估模型安全性具有重要启示。

Abstract: Large language models are highly sensitive to prompts, but this sensitivity is usually studied through task-relevant instructions, demonstrations, or reasoning cues. In this paper, we study a different form of prompt sensitivity: whether prompts that are semantically unrelated to the task can nevertheless steer model behavior. We call them spurious prompts and show their surprising efficacy. We also propose a simple black-box search procedure for discovering them. Across reasoning and question-answering benchmarks, using models ranging from 0.8B to 27B parameters and spanning three model families, we show that spurious prompts can improve performance, often matching or outperforming standard prompting baselines and task-aware prompt optimization. We further show that they can steer models toward unintended behaviors, such as repeatedly selecting the first answer option, producing incorrect answers, returning an even, prime or small number without explicitly instructing the model to do so. These findings reveal a new kind of prompt sensitivity: LLMs can be systematically steered by prompts that are unrelated to the task they are asked to solve. Our code is available at https://github.com/Batorskq/spurious

</details>


### [149] [Understanding Safety-Sensitive Expert Behavior in Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29708)
*Zhibo Zhang,Yuxi Li,Zhen Ouyang,Ling Shi,Kailong Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战了MoE大语言模型中安全性依赖于将有害请求路由到特定拒绝专家的直觉。研究发现路由模式主要由主题驱动，而安全性行为可以在不改变路由路径的情况下被修改。提出了RASET框架，通过识别关键安全专家并进行参数高效微调，揭示了MoE模型中存在的独特安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE大语言模型依赖稀疏的路由驱动专家激活机制，但安全对齐如何与专家特化路由交互仍缺乏探索。普遍直觉认为，安全性行为是通过将有害请求路由到专门的拒绝导向专家来控制的。需要实证研究来验证或反驳这一假设，理解MoE模型中安全机制的真实运作方式。

Method: 1. 实证分析已对齐MoE LLMs的路由模式，验证安全性与路由的关系；
2. 提出RASET（Router-Agnostic Safety-critical Expert Tuning）红队测试框架；
3. 通过对比路由敏感性准则识别安全关键专家；
4. 仅对选定专家应用参数高效微调，保持模型内在路由行为；
5. 相比路由引导干预，最小化语义干扰。

Result: 1. 已对齐MoE模型的路由模式主要由主题驱动，而非安全性驱动；
2. 安全性行为可以在几乎不改变内在路由路径的情况下被修改；
3. 安全执行局部化在少量专家子集中；
4. RASET能够在保持路由行为的同时有效探查和修改安全性；
5. 相比路由引导方法，参数高效微调导致的语义干扰更小。

Conclusion: 揭示了MoE模型中一种独特的安全风险：安全关键专家可被识别并针对性修改，而路由机制对此缺乏保护。这一发现强调了开发专家感知的对齐机制的必要性，现有的假设安全性由路由控制的观点是不准确的，需要新的防护策略。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) LLMs rely on sparse, router-driven expert activation, yet how safety alignment interacts with routed expert specialization remains underexplored. A common intuition is that safety behavior may be controlled by routing harmful requests to distinct refusal-oriented experts. In this work, we provide empirical evidence for a different picture: routing patterns in aligned MoE LLMs are largely topic-driven, while safety behavior can be altered with little change to the model's intrinsic routing path.
  Motivated by this observation, we present **RASET** (**R**outer-**A**gnostic **S**afety-critical **E**xpert **T**uning), a red-teaming framework that probes safety enforcement that is localized in a small subset of experts while preserving the model's intrinsic routing behavior. **RASET** identifies safety-critical experts via a contrastive routing-sensitivity criterion and applies parameter-efficient tuning only to the selected experts, minimizing semantic disruption relative to router-steering interventions. These results reveal a distinct MoE safety risk, highlighting the need for expert-aware alignment mechanisms.

</details>


### [150] [Personalized Turn-Level User Conversation Satisfaction Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29711)
*Zhefan Wang,Zhiqiang Guo,Weizhi Ma,Min Zhang,Quanjia Yan,Hengliang Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个个性化回合级用户对话满意度评估器，结合用户记忆和目标回合上下文生成满意度分数，并引入PersTurnBench基准，可在无需为每个模型收集新用户反馈的情况下比较不同生成模型的个性化满意度表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户对AI助手的满意度高度个性化：相同的回复可能满足一个用户但让另一个用户失望，这取决于每个用户的期望和之前的对话历史。现有的自动评估方法主要衡量通用的回复质量，难以判断某一回复是否在特定回合满足用户需求。

Method: 1) 将问题形式化为个性化回合级用户对话满意度评估；2) 构建对话满意度评估器，结合紧凑的用户记忆与目标回合上下文，生成满意度分数和不满导向的理由；3) 引入PersTurnBench基准，通过回放机制使用经验证的评估器评估生成模型，固定回放状态实现受控比较。

Result: 与人类满意度标注的元评估显示：个性化记忆和事后分数校准在有序一致性方面优于监督学习、基于检索和通用LLM-as-a-judge基线方法，在检测不满回合方面也取得改进。PersTurnBench能够在无需为每个候选模型收集新人类标签的情况下，比较通用生成模型和记忆增强的个性化系统。

Conclusion: 所提出的评估器和基准使研究人员能够在个性化满意度维度上比较候选生成模型，无需为每个模型收集新的用户反馈，为个性化对话系统的评估提供了有效工具。

Abstract: User satisfaction with AI assistants is highly personalized: the same response may satisfy one user but disappoint another depending on what each user expects and what they have asked for before. Existing automatic evaluation methods mostly measure generic response quality, making it difficult to judge whether a response satisfies a user at a specific turn. We study this problem as personalized turn-level user conversation satisfaction evaluation. We build a conversation satisfaction evaluator that combines compact user memories with target-turn context to produce satisfaction scores and dissatisfaction-oriented rationales. Meta-evaluation against human satisfaction annotations shows that personalized memory and post-hoc score calibration improve ordinal agreement and dissatisfied-turn detection over supervised, retrieval-based, and generic LLM-as-a-judge baselines. We further introduce PersTurnBench, a personalized turn-level user conversation satisfaction benchmark that uses the verified evaluator to assess generation models via replay. By holding the replay state fixed, PersTurnBench enables controlled comparison of generic generation models and memory-augmented personalized systems without new human labels for every candidate model. The evaluator and benchmark let researchers compare candidate generation models on personalized satisfaction without collecting new user feedback for every model.

</details>


### [151] [Teaching Language Models to Check Grounded Claim Factuality with Human Test-Taking Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29712)
*Yuxuan Ye,Raul Santos-Rodriguez,Edwin Simpson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: The paper proposes a grounded claim factuality checking method that transforms the task into a true-or-false reading comprehension problem, achieving a balance between performance and cost by using test-taking strategy guidance and training small language models (SLMs).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing natural language inference-based classification methods require dataset-specific threshold tuning, while LLM-based approaches using direct prompting cannot fully leverage the model's reasoning capabilities. There is a need for a more efficient and cost-effective solution for grounded claim factuality checking.

Method: 1) Transform grounded claim factuality checking into a true-or-false reading comprehension task, explicitly prompting LLMs with test-taking strategies for efficient reasoning; 2) Use supervised fine-tuning and a self-revision mechanism to train SLMs to replace LLMs, enabling them to improve their factuality judgments and generate supporting rationales.

Result: The method reduces token usage by over 80% compared to unguided open-ended reasoning, achieves competitive performance on two factuality benchmarks and sets a new state of the art on one of them. The trained SLMs perform on par with strong baselines while maintaining low inference costs and interpretability.

Conclusion: By reformulating the task and introducing efficient prompting strategies along with SLM alternatives, the proposed approach successfully balances performance, cost, and interpretability for grounded claim factuality checking, providing a practical solution for LLM applications such as retrieval-augmented generation.

Abstract: Grounded claim factuality checking is important for large language model (LLM) applications such as retrieval-augmented generation, as it helps users assess the correctness of generated outputs. Existing metrics using entailment classifiers require dataset-specific threshold tuning, while LLM-based approaches often use direct prompting, which underutilises the reasoning capabilities of LLMs. We address this by formulating grounded claim factuality checking as a true/false reading comprehension task and prompting LLMs with explicit test-taking strategies for efficient reasoning. Our method reduces token usage by over 80% compared to unguided open-ended reasoning, and achieves competitive performance to more expensive alternatives across two factuality benchmarks, setting a new state of the art on one. To further reduce inference cost, we train small language models (SLMs) to replace LLMs in the checking pipeline. Using supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and a self-revision mechanism, the SLMs learn to improve their factuality judgements. Experimental results show that the resulting SLMs perform on par with strong baselines, combining low inference costs with generating supporting rationales to support interpretability. Code and datasets will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [152] [Leveraging Routing Dynamics in Mixture-of-Experts Models for Efficient Language Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29714)
*Aditi Khandelwal,Marius Mosbach,Verna Dankers,Siva Reddy,Golnoosh Farnadi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究了多语言MoE模型中的专家路由动态，发现语言专门化主要出现在最后几层，并基于此提出了参数高效的适应策略，仅更新不到2%的参数即可达到与全层微调相当的竞争性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）模型被广泛用于扩展语言模型，但其在多语言设置中的专家路由行为和适应机制仍未得到充分探索。

Method: （1）对英语中心的MoE模型在多语言语料库上进行持续预训练，分析跨语言的专家使用变化；（2）研究发现语言专门化主要出现在最后几层后，提出参数高效的适应策略，仅更新最后几层MoE中的语言特定专家和共享专家。

Result: （1）持续多语言预训练导致早期和中间层的路由呈现扩散、语言无关的特征，语言专门化主要出现在最后几层；（2）语言间的词元级词汇重叠对语言路由起重要作用；（3）在MultiBLiMP和Belebele上的实验表明，该方法在更新不到2%参数的情况下，达到了与完整最后层微调相当的竞争性能。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了MoE模型在持续预训练中语言专门化出现的位置和方式，为低资源多语言适应提供了实用见解。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models are widely used to scale language models, yet their expert routing behavior and adaptation in a multilingual setting remain underexplored. In this work, we study multilingual routing dynamics during continual pre-training of an English-centric MoE model on a multilingual corpus, analyzing how expert usage varies across languages. We find that continual multilingual pre-training leads to diffused, language-agnostic routing in early and middle layers, with language specialization primarily emerging in the final layers. We also show that token-level vocabulary overlap between languages plays an important role in how languages are routed. Motivated by these findings, we propose a parameter-efficient adaptation strategy that updates language-specific and shared experts in the final MoE layers. Experiments on MultiBLiMP and Belebele show that our method achieves a strong performance-efficiency trade-off, attaining competitive performance relative to fine-tuning complete final layers, while updating less than 2% of the parameters. Overall, our findings provide insights into where and how language specialization emerges in MoEs during continual pre-training and provide practical insights for low-resource multilingual adaptation. Our code is available at https://github.com/aditi184/moe-routing-adaptation.

</details>


### [153] [User-Aware Active Knowledge Acquisition for Emotional Support Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29715)
*Mufan Xu,Kehai Chen,Jiahao Hu,Xinchao Xu,Muyun Yang,Tiejun Zhao,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了UKA框架，通过Theory-of-Mind不确定性估计和主动学习机制，解决了情感支持对话系统中用户需求获取困难的问题，在多个基准测试中取得了优于基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感支持在对话系统中起着重要作用，但其成功依赖于适应用户在多轮交互中不断演化和隐式的需求。现有方法面临的核心挑战是：用户需求信号通常微弱、间接，且只能通过多轮交互才能消歧，导致现有情感支持方法难以高效获取和泛化相关的对话知识。

Method: 提出了User-Aware Active Knowledge Acquisition (UKA)框架，这是一个无梯度的主动对话学习框架。核心创新包括：(1) 明确表示用户需求的不确定性；(2) 将主动学习机制融入知识获取和响应选择两个环节；(3) 提出Theory-of-Mind不确定性估计机制，使模型能够优先处理响应，从而引导用户提供更多有价值的反馈；(4) 在训练阶段高效探索与用户对齐的对话知识，同时在测试阶段保持鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个对话基准测试和不同模型架构上的实验结果表明，UKA方法在对话质量和用户对齐两个维度上均持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模用户需求不确定性并结合主动学习策略，UKA框架能够有效解决情感支持对话系统中的知识获取难题，为构建更智能、更贴合用户需求的对话系统提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Emotional support plays an important role in dialogue systems, and its success depends on adapting to a user's evolving and implicit needs across multi-turn interactions while leveraging the strong reasoning capacity of large language models. However, since signals about user needs are often weak, indirect, and can only be disambiguated through multi-turn interaction, existing emotional support methods often struggle to acquire and generalize relevant conversational knowledge efficiently. To bridge this gap, we introduce User-Aware Active Knowledge Acquisition (UKA), a gradient-free active dialogue learning framework that explicitly represents uncertainty about user needs and incorporates active learning into both knowledge acquisition and response selection.We propose a Theory-of-Mind uncertainty estimation mechanism that allows the model to prioritize responses, thereby eliciting more informative user feedback. UKA is capable of efficiently exploring user-aligned conversational knowledge during training while maintaining robustness at test time. Experiments across multiple dialogue benchmarks and model architectures demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms strong baselines in dialogue quality and user alignment.

</details>


### [154] [HTAM: Hierarchical Transition-Attended Memory for Operator Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29734)
*Yining Zhang,Mingyang Yi,Chen Wang,Xuwen Xiang,Tianhe Jia,Zedong Dan,Chengqing Zong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出HTAM(层次化转移注意力记忆)框架，通过构建两层层次化转移图(HTG)组织优化经验，解决LLM自动生成GPU算子时面临的粒度失配问题，在KernelBench基准测试中显著提升正确性、快速求解率和加速比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能GPU核函数对高效部署LLM至关重要，但优化过程需要大量专家知识。现有LLM代码生成方法在算子优化中存在粒度失配问题：粗粒度提示虽可复用但难以执行，细粒度记忆虽可执行但扩大搜索空间且模糊优化瓶颈。核心挑战在于以适当粒度组织优化经验。

Method: 提出HTAM(层次化转移注意力记忆)框架，构建两层层次化转移图(HTG)组织三种信息：粗粒度全局方向、细粒度局部策略、优化步骤间的转移经验。每步进化时，HTAM从当前状态和近期优化历史中选择全局方向，检索对应的局部策略记忆，并用于指导具体CUDA代码生成。

Result: 在完整KernelBench测试集上的实验表明，HTAM在正确性、快速求解率和加速比上均持续优于LLM基线方法。后端测试和Robust-KBench研究表明，结构化记忆带来可迁移的收益。

Conclusion: HTAM通过层次化组织优化经验，有效解决了LLM算子优化中的粒度失配问题，实现了正确性、效率和可迁移性的全面提升，为自动GPU算子优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: High-performance GPU kernels are essential for efficient LLM deployment, yet optimizing them remains expertise-intensive. Recent LLM-based code generation makes automatic GPU operator generation promising, but operator optimization remains a hardware-aware search problem. Existing LLM-based methods face a granularity mismatch: coarse hints are reusable but hard to execute, whereas detailed memories are actionable but enlarge the search space and obscure optimization bottlenecks. The key challenge is therefore to organize optimization experience at an appropriate granularity. To address this issue, this paper proposes HTAM (Hierarchical Transition-Attended Memory), a coarse-to-fine framework for LLM-based operator optimization. HTAM builds a two-level Hierarchical Transition Graph (HTG) to organize coarse global directions, detailed local strategies, and transition experience between optimization steps. During each evolution step, HTAM selects a global direction from the current state and recent optimization history, retrieves the corresponding local strategy memory, and uses it to guide concrete CUDA code generation. Experiments on the full KernelBench suite demonstrate that HTAM consistently improves correctness, fast-solution rate, and speedup over LLM-based baselines, while backend and Robust-KBench studies indicate transferable benefits from structured memory.

</details>


### [155] [Multi-Legal-Bench: Evaluating LLMs on Legal Reasoning Across Jurisdictions, Languages, and Legal Traditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29738)
*Volodymyr Ovcharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出Multi-Legal-Bench，首个跨司法管辖区的法律基准，涵盖6个国家、4个语族、1.34亿份法院判决的5个相同任务。通过评估11个LLM发现：少样本效应可跨司法管辖区复现、无单一模型占优、跨语言迁移依赖标签对齐而非语言亲缘性、tokenizer效率不显著预测准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律NLP基准主要评估单一语言或聚合跨司法管辖区根本不同的任务，导致无法进行跨语言比较。亟需一个在多司法管辖区评估相同任务的基准，以实现有意义的跨语言比较。

Method: 构建Multi-Legal-Bench基准，包含5个任务（法院类型分类、判决形式分类、案件结果预测、法律规范抽取、原因类别预测），覆盖6个国家（乌克兰、法国、荷兰、波兰、捷克、立陶宛）、4个语族、1.34亿份法院判决，形成5x6任务-司法管辖区稀疏矩阵（30个单元格中20个填充）。通过AWS Bedrock评估7个前沿LLM和4个中小型模型（3-12B），采用零样本和3样本提示。

Result: 四个主要发现：（1）在乌克兰发现的任务依赖性少样本效应在所有司法管辖区均可复现；（2）无单一模型在所有场景占优，排名随任务和司法管辖区变化；（3）跨语言少样本迁移不遵循语言亲缘性：UA->FR（罗曼语族，-2.1 pp）迁移优于UA->PL（斯拉夫语族，-13.7 pp），标签集对齐比语族更能预测迁移质量；（4）tokenizer生育率虽达2.3倍差异，但不显著预测跨语言准确率（r=-0.27, p=0.14）。

Conclusion: 模型架构和预训练数据主导了tokenizer效率，而非后者决定跨语言性能。论文公开所有数据、提示和模型预测结果供社区使用。

Abstract: Legal NLP benchmarks overwhelmingly evaluate a single language or aggregate tasks that differ fundamentally across jurisdictions, making cross-lingual comparison impossible. We introduce Multi-Legal-Bench, the first cross-jurisdictional legal benchmark that evaluates identical tasks across six countries (Ukraine, France, Netherlands, Poland, Czech Republic, Lithuania), four language families, and 134 million court decisions. The benchmark defines five tasks court-type classification, judgment form classification, case-outcome prediction, legal norm extraction, and cause category prediction mapped to structured metadata from national court registries, forming a deliberately sparse 5x6 task-jurisdiction matrix (20 of 30 cells filled). We evaluate 7 frontier LLMs under zero-shot and 3-shot prompting via AWS Bedrock, with 4 additional small/medium models (3-12B) for scaling analysis. Our results reveal that: (1) task-dependent few-shot effects discovered in Ukrainian replicate across all jurisdictions; (2) no single model dominates any language rankings shift with both task and jurisdiction; (3) cross-lingual few-shot transfer does not follow language proximity: UA->FR (Romance, -2.1 pp) transfers better than UA->PL (Slavic, -13.7 pp), with label-set alignment predicting transfer quality better than language family; and (4) tokenizer fertility, despite a 2.3x spread, does not significantly predict cross-lingual accuracy (r=-0.27, p=0.14), suggesting that model architecture and pretraining data dominate tokenizer efficiency. We release all data, prompts, and model predictions.

</details>


### [156] [AfriScience-MT: Towards Decolonizing Science in Africa through Text Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29741)
*Idris Abdulmumin,Tajuddeen Gwadabe,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad,David Ifeoluwa Adelani,Nomonde Khalo,Ibrahim Said Ahmad,Abiodun Modupe,Anina Mumm,Sibusiso Biyela,Michelle Rabie,Johanna Havemann,Marek Rei,Jade Abbott,Vukosi Marivate*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AfriScience-MT，一个涵盖6种非洲语言和11个科学领域的平行语料库，用于支持非洲语言的科学翻译研究。研究对机器翻译系统和大型语言模型进行了基准测试，发现闭源模型（GPT-5.4和Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite）在句子和文档级别均优于开源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 殖民语言在非洲教育和科学交流中的主导地位限制了数亿非洲语言使用者获取和产出科学知识的能力。核心障碍在于这些语言缺乏既定的科学术语。

Method: 研究团队构建了AfriScience-MT平行语料库，涵盖阿姆哈拉语、豪萨语、卢干达语、北索托语、约鲁巴语和祖鲁语等6种非洲语言，跨越11个科学领域。专业翻译人员与科学传播专家合作，将科学论文的通俗语言摘要翻译成目标语言，并在缺少术语时创造新术语。研究在零样本、少样本和微调设置下对机器翻译系统和大型语言模型进行了基准测试。

Result: 闭源模型在句子和文档级别均优于所有开源模型：GPT-5.4和Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite的句子级COMET平均分分别为68.3和68.0，文档级COMET平均分均为48.3。开源系统中，微调的NLLB-1.3B在句子级别达到67.3分，TranslateGemma-12B在文档级别（1-shot上下文学习）达到44.0分。

Conclusion: 研究团队发布了AfriScience-MT数据集，以支持非洲语言的基准测试和文档级科学机器翻译研究，为解决非洲语言科学术语缺乏问题提供了重要资源。

Abstract: The dominance of colonial languages in African education and scientific communication limits how hundreds of millions of speakers of African languages access and produce scientific knowledge. A core obstacle is the lack of established scientific terminology in these languages. We introduce AfriScience-MT, a parallel corpus covering six African languages (Amharic, Hausa, Luganda, Northern Sotho, Yorùbá, and isiZulu) across 11 scientific domains. Professional translators, working with expert science communicators, translated plain-language summaries of scientific papers into each target language and created new terms where none existed. We benchmark machine translation systems and large language models in zero-shot, few-shot, and fine-tuned settings. Our results show that closed-source models outperform all open-source models at both the sentence and document levels: GPT-5.4 and Gemini-3.1-Flash-Lite lead with average sentence-level COMET scores of 68.3 and 68.0, respectively, and tie at an average document-level COMET of 48.3. Among open systems, fine-tuned NLLB-1.3B reaches 67.3 at the sentence level, and TranslateGemma-12B reaches 44.0 at the document level with 1-shot in-context learning. We release AfriScience-MT to support benchmarking and document-level scientific MT for African languages.

</details>


### [157] [DySem: Uncovering Dynamic Semantic Components via Multilingual Consensus for Calculating Semantic Textual Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29751)
*Kaijie Zheng,Weiqin Wang,Yile Wang,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DySem框架，一种无需训练的方法，通过构建文本依赖的联合语义集，使用动态的、样本特定的语义维度来计算语义文本相似度，在降低维度的同时取得了更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于大语言模型(LLMs)的语义相似度计算方法存在两个局限性：(1) 最后一层隐藏状态编码了更多通用知识而非纯粹的语义知识，使其在语义相似度计算上表现不佳；(2) LLMs的隐藏层维度通常很大，这为语义表示引入了冗余和噪声。

Method: 提出DySem框架，通过多语言共识探索LLMs中更相关的语义内部组件；摒弃静态表示空间，通过构建文本依赖的联合语义集，转向动态的、样本特定的语义维度；在该共享维度子集上计算相似度。

Result: 在多个LLMs上的广泛实验表明，该方法持续优于最新基线方法，同时保持了更低的相似度计算维度。

Conclusion: 动态、样本特定的语义维度方法比传统的固定维度方法在语义文本相似度计算上更加有效，证明了选择性地利用语义相关维度可以提升性能并降低计算复杂度。

Abstract: Calculating semantic textual similarity is a foundational task in natural language processing. Current large language models (LLMs) based methods typically rely on extracting last-layer hidden states with fixed dimensions to compute similarity for every text pairs. We argue that this paradigm is suffer from two limitations: (i) The last hidden layer encodes more general knowledge rather than just semantic knowledge, making it suboptimal for semantic similarity computation; (ii) The hidden layer dimensions of LLMs are generally very large, which introduces some redundancy and noise for representing semantics. In this work, we propose DySem, a novel training-free framework that investigates more semantic-related internal components of LLMs via multilingual consensus, and shifts away from static representation spaces in favor of dynamic, sample-specific semantic dimensions by constructing text-dependent joint semantic set and computes similarity over this shared dimensional subset. Extensive experiments across various LLMs show that our method consistently outperforms recent baselines while maintaining lower dimensions for similarity calculation. The code is released at https://github.com/szu-tera/DySem.

</details>


### [158] [ActTraitBench: Quantifying the Knowledge-Decision Gap in Large Language Models via Human-Grounded Behavioral Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29791)
*Yutong Yang,Chenxi Miao,Weikang Li,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ActTraitBench评估框架和Chain of Cognitive Alignment (CoCA)干预方法，用于测量并缓解LLMs在显性自我报告与隐性行为决策之间的知识-决策差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs能够在显性自我报告中令人信服地模拟人格，但在隐性行为决策中经常偏离，揭示了显著的知识-决策差距。现有基准由于构建效度有限、多维纠缠以及基于LLM评估的分布偏差，难以测量这种不对称性。

Method: 提出ActTraitBench，一个基于人类数据的评估框架，建立心理测量侧面与行为范式之间的一对一映射，并应用分位数映射的分布校准程序来对齐LLM评判分数分布与人类规范。进一步引入Chain of Cognitive Alignment (CoCA)作为即插即用的推理时干预方法。

Result: 在14个主流LLMs上的实验揭示了普遍存在的知识-决策不对称性：更大、更有能力的模型尽管在自我报告中高度一致，但往往表现出更强的行为分歧。CoCA改善了具有推理能力的前沿模型的对齐性，但暴露了小型架构的明显能力限制。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了LLMs中广泛存在的知识-决策差距问题，提出的评估框架和干预方法为测量和缓解这一问题提供了有效工具，但也表明小型模型在此方面存在明显局限性。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) can convincingly simulate personas in explicit self-reports, they often deviate in implicit behavioral decisions, revealing a substantial Knowledge-Decision Gap ($G_{\text{KD}}$). Existing benchmarks struggle to measure this asymmetry due to limited construct validity, multi-dimensional entanglement, and distributional biases in LLM-based evaluation. To address these issues, we propose ActTraitBench, a human-grounded evaluation framework for measuring personality consistency in LLMs. Grounded in empirical human data, ActTraitBench establishes one-to-one mappings between psychometric facets and behavioral paradigms, and applies a Distributional Calibration via Quantile Mapping procedure to align LLM-judge score distributions with human norms. Experiments on 14 mainstream LLMs reveal a pervasive knowledge-decision asymmetry, where larger and more capable models often exhibit stronger behavioral divergence despite highly consistent self-reports. To mitigate this gap, we further introduce the Chain of Cognitive Alignment (CoCA), a plug-and-play inference-time intervention that improves alignment in reasoning-capable frontier models while exposing clear capability limitations in smaller architectures.

</details>


### [159] [Metric-Dependent Annotation Saturation for Learning from Label Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29797)
*Guneet Kohli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 标注者分歧所需的标注人数取决于评估指标：熵相关性需要20-50名标注者收敛，而分布匹配（KL散度）仅需约10名即可达到饱和。软标签显著优于标签平滑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标注者对标签的分歧本身蕴含信号，但捕获该信号所需的标注者数量尚不明确。研究旨在根据不同评估指标确定最优标注者数量，为标注预算分配提供依据。

Method: 从ChaosNLI数据集（每项含100个独立标注判断）中子采样标签分布，对NLI模型进行微调。识别指标依赖性饱和现象，比较软标签与标签平滑的效果，在DeBERTa和RoBERTa两种架构、非NLI预训练基线及跨域内容安全任务上进行验证。

Result: 在三分类NLI设置中，熵相关性（识别引发分歧的项目）需20-50名标注者收敛，分布匹配（KL散度）约10名标注者即达饱和（覆盖5个模型种子87-95%的改进）。软标签的熵相关性达r=0.643，显著高于标签平滑的r~0.45-0.49（p<0.001），差距源于平滑无法区分模糊项与清晰项。结果在双架构、基线模型和跨域评估中均复现。

Conclusion: 标注预算应根据目标评估指标而非统一设置来确定，这为资源有限条件下的标注决策提供了实证指导。

Abstract: When annotators disagree on a label, the disagreement itself carries signal -- and the number of annotators needed to capture it depends on the evaluation metric. We fine-tune NLI models on label distributions subsampled from ChaosNLI, a dataset providing 100 independent annotator judgments per item, and identify metric-dependent saturation. In our 3-class NLI setting, entropy correlation -- whether the model identifies which items elicit disagreement -- requires N ~ 20-50 annotators to converge, while distributional match (KL divergence) saturates by N ~ 10 (87-95% of improvement across five model seeds). This finding rests on a prior observation: soft labels carry item-specific signal that label smoothing cannot replicate. Across five smoothing intensities, entropy correlation clusters at r ~ 0.45-0.49, while soft labels reach r = 0.643 (p < 0.001); per-item analysis traces this gap to smoothing's inability to distinguish ambiguous items from clear ones. The soft-label advantage replicates across two architectures (DeBERTa, RoBERTa), a non-NLI-pretrained baseline, and an exploratory cross-domain evaluation on content safety. These results suggest that annotation budgets should be informed by the target evaluation metric rather than set uniformly.

</details>


### [160] [Does The Way You Plan Matter? An Empirical Study of Planning Representations for LLM Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29927)
*Alejandra Zambrano,Sara Vera Marjanovic,Imene Kerboua,Xing Han Lù,Leila Kosseim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PlanAhead框架，系统评估了四种计划表示形式（顺序子目标、叙述、伪代码、检查表）对基于LLM的网页智能体性能的影响，发现计划表示形式和生成计划的LLM均显著影响智能体的鲁棒性和任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管近期有所进展，基于LLM的网页智能体仍面临探索受限、遗漏关键步骤、对任务约束敏感等问题。先前研究表明这些失败源于规划缺陷，但不同自然语言计划表示形式的影响尚未被探索。

Method: 1) 提出PlanAhead静态规划器-执行器框架；2) 自动将WebArena任务分为3个难度等级（无需人工标注）；3) 在困难任务上系统评估4种计划表示形式（顺序子目标、叙述、伪代码、检查表）；4) 跨多个多模态LLM家族（OpenAI、阿里巴巴、Google）测试；5) 引入两个新评估指标：Achievement Rate (AR)和Solved-Task Consistency (STC)以处理随机变异性。

Result: 计划表示形式的选择和生成计划的底层LLM均显著影响网页智能体的鲁棒性和任务成功率。

Conclusion: 计划表示形式和生成计划的LLM选择都是影响基于LLM的网页智能体性能的关键因素，这为改进智能体规划能力提供了新方向。

Abstract: Despite recent advances, LLM-based web agents still struggle with limited exploration, omission of critical steps, and sensitivity to task constraints. Prior work suggests that many of these failures stem from weaknesses in planning, yet the impact of alternative natural language plan representation remains unexplored. To address this, we introduce PlanAhead, a static planner-executor framework that evaluates the impact of plan representation in agent performance. We first automatically categorize WebArena tasks into 3 difficulty levels, enabling consistent difficulty grading without human annotation. Then we systematically evaluate 4 different plan representations on the tasks categorized as hard: sequential subgoals, narrative, pseudocode, and checklist; across different families of multimodal LLM powered agents (OpenAI, Alibaba, and Google). To account for stochastic variability, we introduce two novel evaluation metrics: Achievement Rate (AR) and Solved-Task Consistency (STC). Our results show that both, the plan formulation and the underlying LLM generating the plan, significantly influence web-agent robustness and task success.

</details>


### [161] [Causal Interventions on Continuous Variables: A Case Study on Verb Bias in Steering Vectors for In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29971)
*Zhenghao Herbert Zhou,R. Thomas McCoy,Robert Frank*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对连续变量的因果干预方法，并应用于动词偏差特征，证明该方法能够系统性地改变语言模型的下游结构偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型表示的因果干预主要针对离散特征（如语法数量），但语言模型也必须使用分级特征。因此需要开发针对连续变量的因果干预方法。

Method: 提出了一种针对连续变量的因果干预方法：给定与分级目标变量配对的激活向量，定位该变量的低维方向，并使用该方向将向量编辑到反事实目标值。该方法应用于动词偏差这一连续特征（反映哪些句法结构倾向于跟随给定动词）。

Result: 动词偏差在大语言模型的引导向量中被因果表示；对动词偏差的反事实编辑能够系统性地改变下游的结构偏好。此外，引导向量编码了可能驱动上下文学习中的错误驱动更新行为的错误信号，但这些方面在下游生成中未被因果使用。

Conclusion: 因果干预方法可以成功应用于连续变量，但将连续变量与上下文学习机制联系起来仍然是一个挑战。

Abstract: Causal interventions in language model representations have largely targeted discrete features, like grammatical number. However, language models must also make use of features that are graded. We introduce a method for causal intervention on continuous variables: given activation vectors paired with a graded target variable, we localize a low-dimensional direction for that variable and use this direction to edit a vectors toward counterfactual target values. We apply this method to a continuous feature that is well-studied in psycholinguistics, namely verb bias (which reflects which syntactic structures tend to follow a given verb). We show that verb bias is causally represented in steering vectors extracted from large language models: counterfactual edits to verb bias systematically shift downstream structural preferences. Verb bias has also previously been linked to in-context learning; in further analyses, we find that steering vectors encode error signals that could drive the error-driven update behavior seen in in-context learning but that these aspects of the steering vectors are not causally used in downstream production. Overall, these results show causal interventions can be applied to continuous variables, though connecting continuous variables to in-context learning remains a challenge.

</details>


### [162] [Adapting Multilingual Embedding Models to Turkish via Cross-Lingual Tokenizer Surgery and Offline Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29992)
*M. Ali Bayram,Banu Diri,Savaş Yıldırım*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了embeddingmagibu-200m，一个针对土耳其语的句子嵌入模型，通过高效的三阶段适应流程（分词器优化、教师模型克隆、离线蒸馏），在单GPU上4小时、成本仅5-20美元的情况下训练完成，在性能上超越了300M参数的教师模型，同时参数量减少33%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 句子嵌入是语义搜索、聚类、分类和检索增强生成的基础组件。针对土耳其语的句子嵌入模型需要更大的上下文窗口（超越早期基于BERT的土耳其编码器512 token的限制），同时需要降低训练成本和参数量，实现成本-质量的更好权衡。

Method: 采用三阶段适应流程：（1）构建土耳其语优化的多语言分词器，通过在40语言语料库上进行频率分析，剪枝冗余token并融入多语言token，形成131,072词汇表；（2）克隆教师嵌入模型，保留transformer主干权重，通过均值组合token映射初始化兼容的嵌入表；（3）在平衡的40语言维基百科语料库上，使用余弦相似度目标从预计算教师向量进行离线嵌入蒸馏，避免训练时的在线教师推理。

Result: 在STSbTR上获得Pearson/Spearman相关性77.55%/77.45%，超越300M参数教师模型（73.84%/72.92%）；在TR-MTEB（26个任务）上达到平均分63.9%（26个模型中排名第7）；模型约200M参数，训练时间约4小时（单GPU），总成本5-20美元，参数量比教师模型减少33%。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过高效的三阶段适应流程，可以在极低成本下训练出性能优异的土耳其语句子嵌入模型，在参数量减少33%的情况下仍能超越教师模型性能，提供了具有竞争力的成本-质量权衡。所有模型权重、分词器文件、预计算嵌入数据集和开源工具均已发布，便于复现和下游应用。

Abstract: Sentence embeddings are a foundational component for semantic search, clustering, classification, and retrieval-augmented generation. This paper presents embeddingmagibu-200m, a Turkish-focused sentence embedding model that produces 768-dimensional L2-normalized vectors and supports an 8,192-token context window, far exceeding the 512-token limit of earlier BERT-based Turkish encoders. Instead of full pretraining, an efficient three-stage adaptation pipeline is introduced: (1) construct a Turkish-optimized multilingual tokenizer with a 131,072 vocabulary by pruning redundant tokens from the teacher's vocabulary and incorporating multilingual tokens via frequency analysis on a 40-language corpus, (2) clone a teacher embedding model while preserving transformer backbone weights and initializing a compatible embedding table for the new vocabulary via mean-composition token mapping, and (3) perform offline embedding distillation from precomputed teacher vectors using a cosine similarity objective over a balanced 40-language Wikipedia corpus. The resulting student model contains approximately 200M parameters and trains in roughly four hours on a single GPU by avoiding online teacher inference during training, at a total cost of $5-$20. Empirically, Pearson/Spearman correlations of 77.55%/77.45% are obtained on STSbTR, surpassing the 300M-parameter teacher model (73.84%/72.92%). On TR-MTEB (26 tasks), a mean score of 63.9% is achieved (7th out of 26 models), providing a competitive cost-quality trade-off with 33% fewer parameters than the teacher. To facilitate reproducibility and downstream use, all artifacts are released including model weights, tokenizer files, precomputed embedding datasets, and open-source cloning and distillation tooling.

</details>


### [163] [Latent Performance Profiling of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30018)
*Tanmoy Chakraborty,Ayan Sengupta,Suparna Bhattacharya,Partha Pratim Chakrabarti,Amlan Chakrabarti,Supratik Chakraborty,Partha Pratim Das,Lipika Dey,Richa Singh,Mayank Vatsa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Latent Performance Profiling (LPP)框架，通过分析大语言模型的隐藏激活和输出分布，实现从传统基准测试向状态中心的内在评估转变，揭示模型潜在特征和隐藏漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试（如MMLU PRO、BBH、IFEval）存在数据污染、任务范围狭窄、与实际可靠性对齐弱等问题，仅能捕获模型在固定测试集上的输出结果，无法评估模型如何处理信息、校准不确定性或构建内部知识，单纯依赖准确率无法全面反映模型能力。

Method: 提出Latent Performance Profiling (LPP)框架，从隐藏激活和输出分布中推导任务无关的诊断指标。在模型的潜在表示和动态上定义一组标量指标，揭示与规模无关的特性。这些指标可实现可解释的模型比较，并提供稳定、架构敏感的特征签名。同时设计针对不确定性和符号推理的合成探针，与内在指标对齐。

Result: 在8个LLM上进行广泛实证分析（参数规模0.5B-14B），发现基准测试分数相似的模型可能展示截然不同的潜在特征（如熵或适应性的差异）。LPP能够揭示隐藏漏洞，提供稳定且架构敏感的特征签名，设计出的合成探针与内在指标对齐并解耦排行榜偏差。

Conclusion: 建议将LPP与传统基准测试并行报告，以提供对模型行为更深层次、可解释的理解。这种方法能支持更可靠的模型选择、安全性评估，并实现超越表面准确率的评估，为LLM评估提供了新的补充视角。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently achieve impressive scores on standardized benchmarks, yet accuracy alone offers a limited view of their capabilities. Evaluating open-source LLMs through leaderboards faces persistent issues like data contamination, narrow task scope, and weak alignment with real-world reliability. Benchmark-based evaluations such as MMLU PRO, BBH, or IFEval primarily capture \textit{what} a model outputs on fixed test sets, not \textit{how} it processes information, calibrates uncertainty, or structures internal knowledge. In this article, we advocate for a shift from benchmark-centric evaluation toward a complementary, \textit{state-centered intrinsic assessment} of LLMs. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Latent Performance Profiling (LPP)} -- a framework that derives task-agnostic diagnostics from hidden activations and output distributions. LPP defines a set of scalar metrics on a model's latent representations and dynamics, revealing scale-independent traits that enable interpretable comparisons and uncover hidden vulnerabilities. Unlike static accuracy scores, LPP provides stable, architecture-sensitive signatures across models of similar size. With extensive empirical analyses across eight LLMs, spanning a size range of 0.5B-14B, we demonstrate that models with similar benchmark scores can exhibit contrasting latent profiles, such as differences in entropy or adaptability. Guided by these insights, we design synthetic probes for uncertainty and symbolic reasoning that align with intrinsic metrics while decoupling from leaderboard bias. We recommend that reporting LPP alongside benchmarks provides a deeper, interpretable understanding of model behavior, enabling more reliable model selection, safety assessment, and evaluation beyond surface-level accuracy.

</details>


### [164] [Recovering Diversity Without Losing Alignment: A DPO Recipe for Post-Trained LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30021)
*Vinay Samuel,Yapei Chang,Mohit Iyyer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出REDIPO流程，通过从基础模型采样响应并重写、过滤、构建偏好对，在保持对齐性能的同时显著提升LLM输出多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式指令通常有多个有效答案，但后训练（如SFT、RLHF）会使LLM的输出空间收窄到少数规范响应，导致响应模式单一化。

Method: REDIPO是一个离线DPO数据构建流程：(1)从基础模型和指令模型采样响应；(2)用指令模型重写基础模型响应；(3)过滤候选以确保安全性和指令遵循质量；(4)构建偏好对，在相似指令遵循奖励的候选中选择边际多样化的响应。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B、OLMo-3-7B和LLaMA-3.1-8B上，NoveltyBench distinct_k分别提升134%、33%、44%（相比DivPO的0%、-6%、-4%）；同时大致维持MTBench、IFEval、Arena-Hard性能，并降低HarmBench攻击成功率。

Conclusion: 通过精心构建偏好数据，可以有效将基础模型生成中的多样有效答案重新引入指令模型，同时保留后训练的对齐优势。消融实验显示边际多样性对选择和基础响应重写是多样性提升的关键，过滤和质量有界配对则有助于维持对齐。

Abstract: Many open-ended instructions have multiple valid answers that users can benefit from seeing, but post-training often narrows an LLM's output space toward a small set of canonical responses. We introduce REDIPO, an offline DPO data-construction pipeline for recovering distinct valid answer modes while preserving the alignment benefits of the instruct model. For each prompt, REDIPO samples responses from both base and instruct models, rewrites base-model responses with the instruct model, filters candidates for safety and instruction-following quality, and builds preference pairs that favor marginally diverse responses among candidates with similar instruction-following reward. Across Qwen3-4B, OLMo-3-7B, and LLaMA-3.1-8B, REDIPO improves NoveltyBench distinct_k by 134%, 33%, and 44% relative to the instruct checkpoints, while DivPO changes diversity by 0%, -6%, and -4% on the same models. These gains largely maintain MTBench, IFEval, and Arena-Hard performance, and reduce direct-category HarmBench attack success rate. Ablations show that marginal-diversity pair selection and base-response rewriting drive the diversity gains, while filtering and quality-bounded pairing help maintain alignment. Overall, our results show that diverse valid answers from base-model generations can be reintroduced through carefully constructed preference data while retaining the alignment benefits of post-training. We release our code and data at https://github.com/vsamuel2003/RiDiPO.

</details>


### [165] [Give it Space! Explicit Disentangling of Positional and Semantic Representations in Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30022)
*Pierre-Antoine Lequeu,Camille Barboule,Benjamin Piwowarski*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究通过解耦语义、绝对位置和相对位置三个信息流，揭示了Transformer中位置编码的处理机制，发现标准位置编码方法无法稳健保留宏观结构，而解耦方法能显著提升语言表示能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 位置编码是Transformer表示序列顺序的关键机制，但其内部处理和存储方式仍未被充分理解。现代位置编码方法（如RoPE）在长上下文理解或检索等任务上仍存在困难，因此深入理解内部位置机制有助于设计更好的位置编码方法。

Method: 基于位置和语义信号在训练好的Transformer中占据近乎正交子空间的证据，修改编码器Transformer以处理三个显式解耦的流：语义流、绝对位置流(AP)和相对位置流(RP)，并将掩码语言建模(MLM)目标仅限制在语义流上。这种解耦方式实现了清晰的机制研究。

Result: 获得三个关键发现：(1)隔离的AP子空间自发坍缩为捕获文档结构的低频二维流形；(2)注意力头分化为结构和语义导向两组，RP专门支持后者；(3)标准位置编码无法稳健保留宏观结构：RoPE和RP仅能微弱编码，而在MLM压力下纠缠的AP在最终层丢失结构信息。

Conclusion: 解耦方法成功保留了位置编码，在Flash-Holmes探测基准的65个语言现象中的49个上改善了语言表示性能，证明了显式分离位置和语义信息的有效性。

Abstract: Positional encoding (PE) underpins how permutation-invariant Transformers represent sequence order, yet how positional information is processed and stored remains poorly understood. Modern PE methods such as RoPE still struggle on tasks such as long-context understanding or retrieval \cite{chen-etal-2025-hope}. Hence, a better understanding of the internal positional mechanism could help design better PE. Building on evidence that positional and semantic signals occupy nearly orthogonal subspaces in trained Transformers, we modify an encoder Transformer to process three explicitly disentangled streams: semantic, absolute positional (AP) and relative positional (RP), and confine the masked-language-modeling (MLM) objective to the semantic stream. This decoupling enables a clean mechanistic study and yields three take-aways. (1) The isolated AP subspace spontaneously collapses into a low-frequency two-dimensional manifold that captures the structure of the document; (2) Attention heads specialize into structure and semantic-oriented groups, with RP exclusively supporting the latter; (3) Standard positional encodings do not robustly retain macroscopic structure: RoPE and RP only weakly encode it, and entangled AP loses it in the final layers under MLM pressure. The disentangled approach preserves positional encoding, which improves linguistic representation on 49 of the 65 linguistic phenomena of the Flash-Holmes probing benchmark.

</details>


### [166] [Who Am I? History-Aware Profiles for Student Simulation in Tutoring Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30051)
*Zhangqi Duan,Shuyan Huang,Alexander Scarlatos,Jaewook Lee,Simon Woodhead,Andrew Lan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了历史条件化的学生模拟任务，通过两阶段框架（用户画像生成器+对话模拟器）并结合强化学习训练，显著提升了基于大语言模型的学生角色扮演预测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的学生模拟工作主要关注对话内模拟，缺乏对学生知识和行为背景的上下文理解，原因在于未能基于学生历史问答和对话交互进行建模。这种局限性影响了辅导模型的评估和训练效果。

Method: 提出了一个两阶段框架：（1）画像生成器：总结学生历史学习记录生成用户画像；（2）模拟器：基于生成的画像预测学生对话轮次。两个组件均采用强化学习（RL）进行训练，使生成的画像能够优化学生模拟的忠实度。研究还收集了首个真实世界的学生对话和问答数据集用于评估。

Result: 在真实数学学习平台数据集上的大量实验表明：（1）所提方法显著优于基线模型；（2）历史信息、用户画像和强化学习训练均对提升模拟效果至关重要。

Conclusion: 历史条件化的学生模拟框架有效提升了LLM角色扮演学生的准确性，证明了整合学生历史学习信息、优化用户画像以及强化学习训练策略的重要性，为自动化辅导工具的开发提供了更可靠的评估和训练方法。

Abstract: A key part of developing large language model (LLM)-powered, automated tutoring tools is student simulation, i.e., using LLMs to role-play as students, which can facilitate tutor model evaluation and training. Existing work mostly focuses on within-dialogue simulation, which lacks context on student knowledge and behavior, partly due to not grounding in past student question-answering or dialogue interactions. In this work, we introduce the task of history-conditioned student simulation, where the goal is to accurately predict student dialogue turns by leveraging information in the student's learning history. We propose a two-component framework in which a profile generator summarizes a student's history and a simulator predicts student turns conditioned on the resulting profile. We train both components with reinforcement learning (RL), yielding profiles optimized for faithful student simulation. We evaluate our method and baselines on the first-of-its-kind real-world dataset of student dialogues and question responses that we collect from a math learning platform. Extensive experiments show that our method significantly outperforms baselines, and demonstrate the importance of history, profiles, and RL training.

</details>


### [167] [HEART-Bench: Do LLM Agents Exhibit Human-like Psychology?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30058)
*Weihan Peng,Chenxu Zhang,Qianao Wang,Yuling Shi,Heng Lian,Qihong Mao,Jiahao Pang,Chunliang Feng,Bowen Li,Xiaodong Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper introduces a novel benchmark to systematically evaluate whether LLM agents can simulate coherent, human-like psychology.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: While LLM agents have demonstrated remarkable task-oriented abilities such as planning, reasoning, and action, few works have treated them as complete human personalities where emotional dimensions hold equal importance.

Method: The benchmark constructs 11 diverse human characters grounded in orthogonal Big Five personality traits, with each profile containing 1,000 structured autobiographical-style episodic memories distributed across theory-grounded developmental life stages. A curated suite of 64 decision-making scenarios is designed, guided by the DIAMONDS taxonomy (Duty, Intellect, Adversity, Mating, pOsitivity, Negativity, Deception, and Sociality). The benchmark evaluates whether agents can consolidate their innate personality traits and autobiographical memories to make behavioral decisions consistent with their specific psychological profiles.

Result: After systematic human validation and filtering, a benchmark consisting of 673 multiple-choice questions (MCQs) was obtained.

Conclusion: This benchmark provides a principled and scalable testbed for studying human-like emotions, personality consistency, and value-consistent behavioural decision-making in LLM-based agents.

Abstract: While LLM agents have demonstrated remarkable task-oriented abilities such as planning, reasoning, and action, few works have treated them as complete human personalities where emotional dimensions hold equal importance. In this paper, we introduce a novel benchmark to systematically assess whether LLM agents can simulate coherent, human-like psychology. Specifically, our benchmark constructs 11 diverse human characters grounded in orthogonal Big Five personality traits, with each profile deeply integrated with 1,000 structured autobiographical-style episodic memories distributed across theory-grounded developmental life stages. To rigorously evaluate the psychological manifestations of LLMs, we designed a curated suite of 64 decision-making scenarios, guided by the DIAMONDS taxonomy, a psychological framework that characterizes situations along eight dimensions: Duty, Intellect, Adversity, Mating, pOsitivity, Negativity, Deception, and Sociality. By subjecting agents to varying scenarios, the benchmark evaluates whether they can consolidate their innate personality traits and autobiographical memories to make behavioral decisions that are consistent with their specific psychological profiles. After systematic human validation and filtering, we obtained a benchmark consisting of 673 multiple-choice questions (MCQs). We believe this benchmark provides a principled and scalable testbed for studying human-like emotions, personality consistency, and value-consistent behavioural decision-making in LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [168] [UniSteer: Text-Guided Flow Matching in Activation Space for Versatile LLM Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30076)
*Yingdong Shi,Ruiming Zhang,Changming Li,Zhiyu Yang,Kaixing Zhang,Jingyi Yu,Kan Ren*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniSteer提出了一种文本引导的激活流匹配模型，通过学习激活空间中的通用条件速度场，实现了对大语言模型的统一控制接口，支持行为控制、真实性引导、细粒度概念引导、多约束指令遵循和激活空间分类等多种任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于激活的控制方法通常依赖固定的引导方向或特定任务的干预模块，难以适应细粒度概念和组合约束，缺乏灵活性和通用性。

Method: 提出UniSteer模型，学习从自然语言条件到残差流激活的条件分布，构建激活空间中的通用条件速度场。在推理时，通过流反演将源激活部分传输到潜在状态，在目标文本条件下重新生成后注入回冻结的LLM。同一条件模型还支持激活空间分类，通过选择具有最低重构能量的文本标签。

Result: 在三个目标LLM上的实验表明，UniSteer在行为控制、真实性引导、细粒度概念引导、多约束指令遵循和激活空间分类等多个任务上提供了统一的接口，展现出良好的适应性和有效性。

Conclusion: UniSteer成功实现了基于文本引导的激活控制的统一框架，克服了现有方法固定方向或任务特定的限制，为LLM行为控制提供了更灵活、更通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Activation-based control steers large language models (LLMs) by intervening on their internal representations during inference, and has emerged as an effective paradigm for controlling behaviors such as persona and style. However, existing methods often rely on fixed steering directions or task-specific intervention modules, making them difficult to adapt to fine-grained concepts and compositional constraints. We propose UniSteer, a text-guided activation flow matching model that learns a conditional distribution over residual-stream activations from natural-language conditions. Instead of fitting a separate intervention for each target behavior, UniSteer learns a universal conditional velocity field in activation space. At inference time, UniSteer performs flow inversion by partially transporting a source activation toward a latent state and regenerating it under a target textual condition before injecting it back into the frozen LLM. The same conditional model supports activation-space classification by selecting the textual label with the lowest reconstruction energy. Experiments on three target LLMs show that UniSteer provides a unified interface across behavioral control, truthfulness steering, fine-grained concept steering, multi-constraint instruction following, and activation-space classification.

</details>


### [169] [Adaptive Targeted Dynamic Chunking for Tokenization-Free Hierarchical Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30080)
*Thang Dang,Akira Nakagawa,Kenichi Kobayashi,Koichi Shirahata*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应目标动态分块（ATDC）方法，通过课程学习逐步调整压缩比，解决无分词分层模型中字节压缩比优化难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无分词分层模型作为传统大语言模型的有力替代方案，能够解决词汇表设计复杂、未登录词错误和语言特定约束等预处理问题。然而，这类字节级方法面临压缩比优化的重大挑战，而压缩比是决定模型处理字节数据分块性能的关键因素。

Method: 提出自适应目标动态分块（ATDC）机制：1）利用课程学习在训练过程中逐步调整压缩比，从低压缩比过渡到高压缩比以稳定学习过程；2）建立目标压缩比与最内层分块字节数（BPIC）之间的关系分析，实现训练阶段分块大小的追踪。

Result: 在FineWeb-Edu 100B数据集上的评估表明：1）配备ATDC的分层模型在比特每字节（BPB）性能上与字节级和词元级基线模型相当；2）相比固定压缩比模型，训练动态更稳定，下游任务性能更优；3）保持了字节级处理固有的鲁棒性和灵活性。

Conclusion: ATDC成功解决了无分词分层模型中的压缩比优化挑战，使模型在保持字节级处理优势的同时实现与传统方法相当的性能，为无分词大语言模型的发展提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Tokenization-free hierarchical models are emerging as a promising alternative to traditional Large Language Models (LLMs), addressing inherent preprocessing issues such as vocabulary design complexity, out-of-vocabulary (OOV) errors, and language-specific constraints. However, a significant challenge in these byte-level methods is the optimization of the compression ratio, a critical factor that dictates model performance for processing bytes data via chunks. In this paper, we propose Adaptive Targeted Dynamic Chunking (ATDC), a novel byte-compression control mechanism designed to enhance the effectiveness of dynamic chunking within hierarchical architectures. Our approach utilizes curriculum learning to progressively adjust the compression ratio during training, transitioning from low to high compression to stabilize the learning process. We provide an analysis establishing the relationship between the target compression ratio and Bytes-Per-Innermost-Chunk (BPIC), allowing for tracking of chunk-size evolution throughout the training phase. Evaluations conducted on the FineWeb-Edu 100B dataset demonstrate that hierarchical models equipped with ATDC achieve competitive Bits-Per-Byte (BPB) performance compared to conventional baselines operating at both byte and token levels. Furthermore, the proposed method exhibits more stable training dynamics and superior final performance across diverse downstream tasks compared to models using fixed compression ratios, while maintaining the inherent robustness and flexibility of byte-level processing.

</details>


### [170] [DirectorBench: Diagnosing Long-Form Video Generation with Personalized Multi-Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30090)
*Jiamin Chen,Qianben Chen,Jiawen Zhang,Yidi Wu,Yuchen Li,Xiaokun Zhang,Wangchunshu Zhou,Chen Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出DirectorBench，一个针对长视频生成的个性化多智能体诊断基准，通过80个元数据、7个用户档案和40个检查点标准（覆盖脚本、视觉、音频、跨模态、稳定性5个维度）进行细粒度评估，揭示工作流瓶颈和用户偏好相关的失效模式，而非仅提供单一聚合分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频生成评估基准主要关注局部视觉质量、短期时序一致性或通用提示对齐，缺乏对工作流程故障和用户偏好依赖性的诊断能力，无法满足分钟级、多镜头、具有叙事结构的长视频生成评估需求。

Method: 提出DirectorBench诊断基准，包含：(1) 80个结构化元数据条目；(2) 7个用户画像；(3) 40个检查点标准覆盖5个维度（脚本、视觉、音频、跨模态、稳定性）。采用多智能体评估框架，实现检查点级别的瓶颈定位和档案感知评估，避免单一聚合评分的信息损失。评估了4个长视频生成工作流、6个基础LLM和7个用户画像。

Result: (1) 发现工作流存在单元间瓶颈：转场质量平均仅0.256，最佳工作流仅0.356；(2) 提示级用户需求满足度平均0.71；(3) 通过14名标注者的人类评估验证，DirectorBench与人类判断高度一致，能捕捉聚合评分无法揭示的工作流和画像相关的失效模式。

Conclusion: 诊断性和档案感知的基准测试对长视频生成至关重要，DirectorBench能够定位细粒度瓶颈、揭示隐藏的失效模式，为长视频生成系统的改进提供更精准的指导方向。

Abstract: Long-form video generation is rapidly moving from short, single-scene synthesis toward minute-long, multi-shot creation with narrative structure, cinematic control, audio, and cross-modal synchronization. However, evaluating such videos remains challenging, since existing benchmarks largely focus on local visual quality, short-horizon temporal consistency, or generic prompt alignment, and provide limited diagnosis of workflow failures and user-dependent preferences. We introduce DirectorBench, a personalized multi-agent diagnostic benchmark for long-form video generation. DirectorBench evaluates generated videos with respect to 80 structured metadata entries, 7 user profiles, and 40 checkpoint criteria across 5 dimensions: script, visual, audio, cross-modal, and stability. Instead of reducing quality to a single aggregate score, DirectorBench localizes checkpoint-level bottlenecks and supports profile-aware evaluation. We evaluate 4 long-form video generation workflows, 6 base LLMs, and 7 user profiles. Across workflows, DirectorBench reveals a between-unit bottleneck: transition quality averages only 0.256 and reaches 0.356 for the best workflow, while prompt-level user demand fulfillment averages 0.71. We further conduct human evaluation with 14 annotators to validate the alignment between DirectorBench and human judgment. The results show that DirectorBench captures human-perceptible quality differences and reveals workflow- and profile-dependent failure modes that are hidden by aggregate scoring. These findings highlight the importance of diagnostic and profile-aware benchmarking for long-form video generation.

</details>


### [171] [Dial HEALTHDIAL for Advice: A Multilingual and Multi-Parallel Spoken Dialogue Dataset for Knowledge-Grounded Information Seeking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30107)
*Songbo Hu,Yinhong Liu,Ej Zhou,Evgeniia Razumovskaia,Xiaobin Wang,Alexander Fraser,Ivan Vulić,Anna Korhonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了HEALTHDIAL，一个大规模、多语言、多平行的RAG语音对话数据集，包含6,000个对话（4种语言）、163小时语音数据及详细的人口统计学标注，基准测试揭示了跨语言的性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建语音对话数据集在方法论上具有挑战性，尤其是大规模构建多语言、多平行数据集时面临更大困难。现有的RAG语音对话系统缺乏高质量的多语言评估数据集。

Method: 创建HEALTHDIAL数据集，包含：(1) 6,000个信息寻求型对话（每种语言1,500个），基于WHO可信内容；(2) 163小时用户语音，采集自阿拉伯语、中文、英语、西班牙语四种语言的母语使用者；(3) 标注人口统计学（性别、年龄）和社会语言学变量（主要语言、原籍地区）；(4) 开发原型系统和数据收集与评估工具包。

Result: 在关键对话任务上的基准测试结果显示，即使在高资源语言之间，也存在一致的性能差异，揭示了多语言对话系统发展的不均衡性。

Conclusion: 为支持未来研究，作者公开了数据集、原型系统以及数据收集和系统评估工具包。

Abstract: Creating spoken dialogue datasets is methodologically challenging, and these challenges are amplified when the goal is to build multilingual, multi-parallel datasets at scale. This work introduces HEALTHDIAL, a large-scale, multilingual, and multi-parallel dataset for developing and evaluating retrieval-augmented generation (RAG)-based spoken dialogue systems. The dataset comprises 6,000 information-seeking dialogues (1,500 per language) grounded in trusted content from the World Health Organization (WHO) and 163 hours of user speech recorded from native speakers of diverse dialects across four official WHO languages: Arabic, Chinese, English, and Spanish. Each speaker is annotated with demographic (e.g., gender, age) and sociolinguistic (e.g., primary language, region of origin) variables. We report benchmark results across key dialogue tasks, which reveal consistent performance disparities across languages, even among high-resource ones. To support future research, we release the dataset, a prototype system, and a toolkit for data collection and system evaluation.

</details>


### [172] [CCS: Clinical Consensus Selection for Radiology Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30131)
*Xi Zhang,Yingshu Li,Zaiqiao Meng,Jake Lever,Edmond S. L. Ho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了临床共识选择（CCS）框架，一种解码器无关的推理时选择方法，通过采样多个候选报告并选择临床共识最高的报告，显著提升了放射学报告生成的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学报告生成（RRG）研究主要关注扩展训练数据、模型容量和检索机制，但在推理阶段提升报告质量的研究不足。作者发现固定的放射学多模态大语言模型（MLLM）在候选池中往往能生成比默认解码选择的报告临床质量更高的报告，这表明推理时决策制定是一个被忽视的瓶颈。

Method: 提出临床共识选择（CCS）框架，这是一个解码器无关的推理时选择方法。CCS采样多个候选报告，然后选择在候选池中具有最高临床共识的报告。该方法将基于文本的效用函数与放射学适应的效用函数（由图像-报告训练的多模态嵌入器计算）统一，能够测量候选报告之间超越表面文本相似性的共识度。

Result: 在三个数据集和多个放射学MLLM上的实验表明，CCS在推理时性能上持续优于单路径解码和通用Best-of-N基线，尤其在临床指标上提升明显。进一步分析显示，基于图像的效用函数形成了一个区别于文本共识的选择维度，且RRG在推理时仍有巨大提升空间。

Conclusion: 推理时决策制定是放射学报告生成的重要研究方向，CCS框架通过多候选采样和临床共识选择有效提升了报告质量，证明了推理阶段优化的潜力，为未来RRG研究提供了新的改进方向。

Abstract: Radiology report generation (RRG) is commonly formulated as a single-path generation task, where a multimodal large language model (MLLM) produces one decoded report as the final output. While recent progress has largely been driven by scaling training data, model capacity, and retrieval mechanisms, improving report quality at inference time remains underexplored. In this work, we observe that fixed radiology MLLMs often generate clinically stronger reports elsewhere in their candidate pool than the one selected by default decoding, suggesting that inference-time decision making remains an overlooked bottleneck. To address this, we propose Clinical Consensus Selection (CCS), a decoder-agnostic inference-time selection framework that samples multiple candidate reports and selects the one with the highest clinical consensus across the rollout pool. CCS unifies text-based utilities with a radiology-adapted utility computed by an image--report-trained multimodal embedder, which measures candidate agreement beyond surface-level textual similarity. Across three datasets and multiple radiology MLLMs, CCS consistently improves inference-time performance over single-path decoding and generic Best-of-N baselines, with particularly clear gains on clinical metrics. Further analysis shows that image-grounded utility forms a selection axis distinct from textual consensus and that substantial headroom remains for improving RRG at inference time.

</details>


### [173] [CorPipe at CRAC 2026: Empty Nodes and Cross-Lingual Transfer in Multilingual Coreference Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30133)
*Milan Straka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CorPipe 26是CRAC 2026多语言共指消解共享任务的获胜系统，在LLM赛道和无约束赛道中均取得最佳性能，相比竞争者分别领先2.8和9.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CRAC 2026共享任务第五版旨在比较生成式大语言模型（LLM）与专门化系统的性能差异，同时引入了5个新数据集和2种新语言，推动多语言共指消解研究的发展。

Method: CorPipe 26是CorPipe 25的改进版本，提出了一种新变体，能够在单一模型中同时预测空节点、提及（mentions）和共指链接，实现了端到端的多语言共指消解。

Result: 系统在LLM赛道中比所有其他提交高出2.8个百分点，在无约束赛道中比所有提交高出9.5个百分点。此外，研究还进行了模型规模、空节点预测方法和跨语言零样本评估等多组消融实验。

Conclusion: CorPipe 26证明了专门化系统在多语言共指消解任务上优于生成式LLM，系统源代码和训练模型已在GitHub公开，为后续研究提供了重要基准。

Abstract: We introduce CorPipe 26, our winning submission to the CRAC 2026 Shared Task on Multilingual Coreference Resolution. The fifth edition of this shared task focuses mainly on the comparison of generative LLMs and specialized systems; additionally, 5 more datasets and 2 new languages are introduced. CorPipe 26 is an improved version of CorPipe 25, with a new variant predicting empty nodes together with mentions and coreference links in a single model. Our system outperforms all other submissions in the LLM track by 2.8 percent points and all submissions in the unconstrained track by 9.5 percent points. Furthermore, we perform a series of ablation experiments with different model sizes, empty node prediction methods, and cross-lingual zero-shot evaluation. The source code and the trained models are publicly available at https://github.com/ufal/crac2026-corpipe.

</details>


### [174] [GRUFF: LLM Pronoun Fidelity, Reasoning, and Biases in German](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30214)
*Fabian Mewes,Anne Lauscher,Vagrant Gautam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了GRUFF数据集，这是首个大规模德语代词保真度数据集，用于研究语言模型在具有复杂语法性别系统语言中的代词推理能力和偏见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于代词保真度（pronoun fidelity）和性别偏见的研究主要关注英语，但英语的语法性别有限且几乎没有性别一致性。德语具有更复杂的性别一致性系统，为研究模型如何处理代词指称和偏见提供了新的视角。

Method: 研究者构建了GRUFF数据集，涵盖四种不同的名词性别一致性系统和四组代词（包括传统代词和新代词xier和en）。该数据集用于评估大语言模型在德语环境下的代词保真度，测试模型在存在干扰性话语实体时正确重用先前指定代词的能力。

Result: 实验发现：1) LLMs在缺乏显式上下文时对阳性和阴性实体表现出强语法一致性，但对新代词xier和en则不然；2) 模型通常对干扰项不够鲁棒，但编码器模型在德语中比在英语中更鲁棒，反映了语法性别的重要性；3) 职业刻板印象在语法格之间以及大多数模型之间相关性较差，除了架构密切相关的模型。

Conclusion: 该研究强调了在具有语法性别的语言中研究性别和代词指称的重要性，揭示了LLMs在处理非传统代词和应对干扰项时的局限性，并为德语中性别包容性语言和指称推理的进一步研究提供了数据集和代码资源。

Abstract: Third-person singular pronouns have long been used to study stereotypical biases in language models and to test their abilities to reason about reference. More recently, the interplay between reasoning and bias has been investigated with the task of pronoun fidelity, which assesses models' abilities to correctly reuse a previously-specified pronoun for a discourse entity, independent of other potentially distracting discourse entities mentioned in between. However, such research focuses on English, which is a language with limited grammatical gender and almost no gender agreement. In this paper we contribute a novel, large-scale dataset, GRUFF, to measure pronoun fidelity in German, covering four different gender agreement systems in nouns, and four sets of pronouns. With this dataset, we show that LLMs show strong grammatical agreement for masculine and feminine entities in the absence of explicit context, but not for neopronouns xier and en. Models are generally not robust to distractors, but encoder-only models are more robust in German than in English, reflecting the importance of grammatical gender. Finally, we show that occupational stereotypes in this context are poorly correlated across grammatical cases, and across most models, except ones with closely related architectures. We release all code and data to encourage further work on gender-inclusive language and referential reasoning in German.

</details>


### [175] [Do Language Models Track Entities Across State Changes?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30233)
*Zilu Tang,Qiao Zhao,Gabriel Franco,Derry Wijaya,Aaron Mueller,Sebastian Schuster,Najoung Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大规模语言模型如何在复杂场景中进行实体跟踪（ET），发现模型并未采用顺序递增的跟踪策略，而是在最后一个token并行聚合相关信息。针对REMOVE操作，模型采用脆弱的全局抑制标签，导致可预测的失败模式，作者提出了消除该标签的机制性解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实体跟踪是复杂推理的基础能力。现有工作主要关注无状态变化情况下的实体绑定，对于具有真实难度、包含多次状态变化的自然语言ET问题，人们对其机制理解有限。本文旨在探究语言模型在复杂场景下处理ET问题的内在机制。

Method: 研究包含多次状态变化操作（PUT、REMOVE、MOVE）的复杂ET场景，分析模型是否跨token递增跟踪世界状态或跨层跟踪查询相关状态。通过行为实验和机制分析相结合的方式，深入研究单个操作的实现机制，并基于机制发现预测和验证失败模式。

Result: 发现语言模型并未跨token递增跟踪世界状态或跨层跟踪查询相关状态，而是在查询出现时于最后一个token位置并行聚合相关信息。研究揭示REMOVE操作由脆弱的全局抑制标签实现，该机制预测了多种失败模式并通过行为实验得到确认。通过消除该标签的机制性方案可部分解决此问题。

Conclusion: 语言模型采用非顺序策略解决本质上顺序的任务。本研究展示了行为分析与机制分析如何相互促进：行为结果指导机制假设的提出，机制分析洞察有助于构建更强的行为评估，预测现有评估中缺失的失败模式。这种分析方法为理解和改进模型的推理能力提供了新思路。

Abstract: Entity tracking (ET), the ability to keep track of states, is a fundamental skill that underlies complex reasoning. An increasing amount of work investigates how transformer language models (LMs) solve entity binding $\textit{without}$ state changes. However, there is limited understanding of how non-toy LMs address ET problems of realistic difficulties expressed in natural language. To this end, we investigate the mechanisms underlying ET in more complex scenarios featuring multiple state-changing operations. We find that LMs do not incrementally track world states across tokens or query-relevant states across layers, but simply aggregate relevant information in parallel at the last token when the query becomes evident. We further investigate mechanisms of individual operations ($\texttt{PUT}$, $\texttt{REMOVE}$, $\texttt{MOVE}$) to characterize this non-incremental ET mechanism. Surprisingly, LMs implement the $\texttt{REMOVE}$ operation with a fragile global suppression tag; this global removal mechanism predicts various failure modes that we confirm behaviorally. We provide a mechanistic solution of nullifying this tag to partially address this issue. Overall, our findings reveal that LMs solve a fundamentally sequential task using a non-sequential strategy. More broadly, our work illustrates how behavioral and mechanistic analyses can fruitfully interact. Behavioral results inform mechanistic hypotheses, and insights from mechanistic analyses help build stronger behavioral evaluations by predicting failure modes missing from existing evaluations.

</details>


### [176] [CommunityFact: A Dynamic, Multilingual, Multi-domain Benchmark for Misinformation Detection in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30241)
*Sahajpreet Singh,Insyirah Mujtahid,Min-Yen Kan,Kokil Jaidka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了CommunityFact，一个可刷新的错误信息检测基准，包含跨五种语言和两个领域的15,992个独立声明。评估显示，网页访问能显著提升LLM的验证性能，但LLM的来源选择与人类评估者存在系统性偏差，这种偏差可通过检索扩展或修剪机制来缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 错误信息验证日益发生在公开、快速变化且多语言的在线环境中，而现有的静态基准无法完整衡量模型在此类动态场景下的可靠性，因此需要构建更具覆盖性、细粒度和可重新分配性的基准。

Method: 引入CommunityFact基准，包含15,992个跨五种语言和两个领域的独立声明；评估了10个大语言模型在不同推理时能力（包括思考能力和网页搜索）下的表现；分析了模型来源选择策略与人类Community Notes评估者的对齐程度。

Result: 闭输入验证仍具挑战性；网页访问带来最大性能提升；启用网页功能的LLM的来源选择策略与人类评估者系统性不一致，但通过检索扩展或修剪机制可以缩小这一差距；在语言-领域切片和证据生态系统方面存在显著差异。

Conclusion: CommunityFact不仅提供了更全面的评估基准，还将Community Notes定位为训练信号，可用于构建声明条件下的来源建议器，从而改进对新颖声明的事实验证能力。

Abstract: Misinformation verification increasingly occurs in public, fast-moving, and multilingual online settings, where static benchmarks provide an incomplete measure of model reliability. We introduce CommunityFact, a refreshable benchmark for misinformation detection in the wild, with three major goals: coverage, granularity, and redistributability. This release contains 15,992 standalone claims across five languages and two domains. We evaluate ten LLMs under varying inference-time capabilities, including thinking and web-search. Our results show that closed-input verification remains challenging, web access yields the largest gains, and web-enabled LLMs' source-selection policies are systematically misaligned with the sources human Community Notes raters converge on -- a gap that closes through model-specific mechanisms of retrieval expansion or pruning. We further find substantial variation across language-domain slices and across the evidence ecosystems used by web-enabled systems. Beyond evaluation, CommunityFact positions Community Notes as a training signal for claim-conditioned source suggesters that could improve factual verification on novel claims.

</details>


### [177] [Knowing What to Solve Before How: Preplan Empowered LLM Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30245)
*Shaojie Wang,Liang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出PPC（Preplan-Plan-CoT）框架，在现有规划推理范式前增加显式的"预规划"阶段用于问题理解，在40项指标中的39项取得最佳结果，maj@16和pass@16分别提升+2.23和+3.06。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于规划的推理方法存在范式层面的缺失：规划和执行阶段都聚焦于"如何解决问题"，而"解决什么问题"这一前置问题（包括识别问题类型、适用工具和可预见的陷阱）始终隐含未显式化。

Method: 提出PPC框架，构建question → preplan → plan → CoT新范式。实现方式包括：（1）设计三阶段合成流水线和spoiler-score检测器，过滤泄露和剧透失败以构建干净的预规划监督；（2）使用复合GRPO奖励确保生成的规划真正遵循预规划。

Result: 在4个骨干模型和5个数学推理基准上的实验表明，PPC在40项指标中的39项取得最佳结果，maj@16和pass@16分别比最强基线提升+2.23和+3.06，且未引入额外推理token开销。

Conclusion: 通过引入显式的预规划阶段，PPC成功弥合了范式层面的缺失，使问题理解显式化，在多个基准和模型上实现了持续的性能提升。

Abstract: Current plan-based reasoning methods improve large language models (LLMs) by inserting a planning stage before execution, giving rise to the question $\rightarrow$ plan $\rightarrow$ cot paradigm. While effective, a closer examination reveals an inherent paradigm-level gap: both the planning and its execution stages decide how to solve a problem, while the prior question of what to solve; recognizing the problem type, the applicable tools, and the foreseeable pitfalls; remains entirely implicit. To bridge this gap, we propose PPC (Preplan-Plan-CoT), a framework that introduces an explicit problem-understanding stage, the preplan, yielding a new question $\rightarrow$ preplan $\rightarrow$ plan $\rightarrow$ cot paradigm. Realizing this paradigm requires safeguarding the conceptual integrity of preplan at both ends. Specifically, we design a three-stage synthesis pipeline with a spoiler-score detector that filters out leakage and spoiler failures to build clean preplan supervision, and a composite GRPO reward enforces that the generated plan genuinely follows from the preplan. Experiments across four backbones and five mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that PPC achieves the best results on 39 of 40 metrics, improving maj@16 and pass@16 by +2.23 and +3.06 over the strongest baseline without introducing additional inference token overhead.

</details>


### [178] [Same Evidence, Different Answers: Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation for Multi-Turn Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30251)
*Zizhuo Lin,Quanling Liu,Jinsheng Quan,Chao Zhang,Yifan Zhu,Xing Shi,Jingtao Xu,Zhihui Li,Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CCOPD方法，通过知识蒸馏对齐多轮对话行为，解决大语言模型在渐进式信息揭示场景下的性能下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在全量提示下表现良好，但在多轮渐进信息揭示场景中性能下降。作者指出关键原因是“self-anchored drift”：部分信息下的响应会引入无依据假设，这些假设会扭曲最终答案。

Method: 提出Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation (CCOPD)：同一基础模型扮演两个角色——基于全量提示的冻结教师模型和通过多轮对话逐步接收信息的可训练学生模型；CCOPD对学生轨迹行为与教师的规范全上下文行为进行对齐。

Result: 仅使用数学问题对话训练，CCOPD在数学及五个零样本跨域任务上，相比原始基础模型在RAW-SHARDED性能上平均相对提升32%，同时基本保持全上下文性能。

Conclusion: CCOPD增强了对用户证据的依赖性，降低了对早期助手轮次污染的敏感性，有效缓解了多轮对话中的自我锚定漂移问题。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often solve a task when all instructions are given in a single prompt, but fail when the same information is revealed gradually across turns. When a clean FULL prompt and a RAW-SHARDED conversation contain the same complete user evidence, the model should still arrive at the same answer. We argue that a key reason for this gap is self-anchored drift: responses produced under partial information introduce unsupported assumptions, and those assumptions later distort the final answer. To reduce this effect, we propose Canonical-Context On-Policy Distillation (CCOPD). During training, the same base model is used in two roles: a frozen teacher conditioned on the clean FULL prompt and a trainable student that receives the same evidence incrementally through a multi-turn conversation; CCOPD aligns the student's behavior on its own trajectories with the teacher's canonical full-context behavior. Trained only on math problem conversations, CCOPD yields a 32\% average relative improvement in RAW-SHARDED performance over the original base model across math and five zero-shot out-of-domain task families, while largely preserving full-context performance. Further analyses suggest that CCOPD strengthens grounding in user evidence and reduces sensitivity to contamination from earlier assistant turns.

</details>


### [179] [How LoRA Remembers? A Parametric Memory Law for LLM Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30260)
*Ziwen Xu,Haiwen Hong,Linsong Yu,Benglei Cui,Longtao Huang,Hui Xue,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型参数记忆的量化容量极限,发现了损失下降与有效参数和序列长度之间的幂律关系,并提出了MemFT优化策略来提升记忆保真度和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型需要在动态环境中持续学习和更新知识。虽然LoRA被广泛用于记忆更新,但现有研究主要依赖定性的下游评估,对精确参数记忆的定量容量极限和潜在动力学机制缺乏深入探索。

Method: 1) 将LoRA作为受控的记忆容量探针,在潜在空间中系统量化精确参数记忆;
2) 提出参数记忆定律(Parametric Memory Law),建立损失下降ΔL与有效参数和序列长度之间的幂律关系;
3) 在token级别进行细粒度分析,发现确定性相变现象;
4) 提出MemFT,一种阈值引导的优化策略,动态重新分配训练预算给次阈值token。

Result: 1) 发现了稳健的幂律关系:损失下降与有效参数和序列长度相关;
2) 揭示了确定性相变:预测概率p>0.5是贪婪解码下逐字回忆的充分条件;
3) 实验验证MemFT能够增强记忆保真度和效率。

Conclusion: 本研究填补了参数记忆量化研究的空白,为理解LLM记忆容量提供了理论基础,并提出了实用的优化策略MemFT,在记忆保真度和效率方面展现出优越性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) must continuously learn and update knowledge to remain effective in dynamic real-world environments. While Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is widely used for such memory updates, existing studies mainly rely on qualitative downstream evaluations, leaving the quantitative capacity limits and underlying dynamics of exact parametric memory largely unexplored. To bridge this gap, we employ LoRA as a controlled memory capacity probe within the latent space to systematically quantify exact parametric memory. We introduce the Parametric Memory Law, a robust power law linking loss reduction Delta L to effective parameters and sequence length. At the token level, fine-grained analysis reveals a deterministic phase transition, demonstrating that a prediction probability of p > 0.5 constitutes a sufficient condition for verbatim recall under greedy decoding. Driven by these insights, we introduce MemFT, a threshold-guided optimization strategy that dynamically redistributes the training budget toward sub-threshold tokens. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that MemFT can enhance memory fidelity and efficiency. Code will be released at https://github.com/zjunlp/ParametricMemoryLaw.

</details>


### [180] [Loong: A Human-Like Long Document Translation Agent with Observe-and-Act Adaptive Context Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.30274)
*Yutong Wang,Xuebo Liu,Derek F. Wong,Zhilin Li,Rongqing Jiang,Min Zhang,Shimin Tao,Daimeng Wei,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了Loong，一个基于3E记忆模块（Essence-Exemplar-Entity）的长文档翻译智能体，通过自适应上下文选择实现显著的翻译质量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档级翻译对大语言模型仍是重大挑战，主要受限于：(1)有限上下文窗口阻碍全局连贯性；(2)冗余上下文信息降低翻译质量。

Method: 提出Loong智能体，核心包括：(1)3E记忆模块存储摘要、句对和实体记录作为历史上下文；(2)通过深度推理自适应识别最优翻译指导上下文；(3)基于强化学习优化上下文策略，利用从自身采样观察-行动推理轨迹中获得的偏好数据。

Result: 在英中、英德、英法翻译方向上实现显著质量提升，三个评估指标平均增益达13.0分；跨领域泛化能力强，对上下文噪声鲁棒，在超长文档翻译中保持稳定性。

Conclusion: Loong通过智能上下文管理有效解决文档级翻译挑战，在多语言和多领域场景下展现出优越性能，为长文档翻译提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Document-level translation remains one of the most challenging tasks for large language models, which are constrained by limited context windows that impede global cohesion, while simultaneously suffering from redundant contextual information that degrades translation quality. To address this, we propose a human-like long document translation agent called Loong, which leverages a 3E memory module (Essence-Exemplar-Entity) to store summaries, sentence pairs, and entity records as historical context. Instead of passively attending to all history, Loong performs deep reasoning to adaptively identify the optimal context for translation guidance. Loong optimizes its context policy through reinforcement learning, utilizing preference data derived from its own sampled observe-and-act reasoning trajectories. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that Loong achieves substantial translation quality improvements in English $\Leftrightarrow$ Chinese, German, and French directions, with average gains of up to 13.0 points across the three evaluation metrics. Furthermore, Loong exhibits strong generalization across domains and robustness against contextual noise, while maintaining remarkable stability in ultra-long document translation. Our code is released at https://github.com/YutongWang1216/LoongDocMT.

</details>
